meynnaṭpukkuriya paruntuḻik kaṇṭa meypporuḷ nikaḻtaṟ kēṟṟa kālam eytiṟṟāka, pakalcey
maṇṭilam paṉikkaṭal mukaṭṭeḻa, paṟavi yiṉṉicipāṭa, curumpuntēṉum cūḻntārppa,
kaṭikkamalam vāyviḷḷa, pūcciyiṉam poliveyta, puḻuviṉaṅkaḷ kuḻīi maṟuka, ippuḻuvum
pūcciyāyiṟṟu; yākkiyum kāloṭumitakkum kaviṉeytiṟṟu iyaṅkiyavuṭal paṟattal
ceytatu; irumaruṅkum ciṟakukaḷeḻuntaṉa; avaṟṟiṉkaṭ palvakiya voḷiteṟikkum vaṇṇaṅkaḷ
tōṉṟiyaḻaku ceytaṉa.
ivvaṇṇam, aḻakutikaḻap puttuṭal peṟṟup pūntēṉuṇṭu polivukūr cuṟṟamoṭu purintu
vāḻnta cēṭap pūcci, piṉṉarc ciṉṉāṭkaḷuṭṭaṉ ṉiṉṉuyir nīṅkuṅ kāla meytalum, eṇṇaru
miṉpaṅkaṉiya, “im maṟuviṉṟu viḷaṅkum māyiruñālattu, aṟivu vaḷarkkumāṟṟā ṉaminta
poruḷkaḷ mikappalavuḷa; avaṟṟiyāyntu kōṭalaṟiviṉ kaṭaṉ: ataṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta
vuṟuppāvatu uṟutinōkkē (Faith). ataṉi ataṉaruḷālē yaṭiyappeṟṟēṉ. yāṉ peṟṟa iṉpam
peṟuka ivviyakam!! iṉi evi variṉum varuka. yāṉ viṇṇakam paṭarkuval" eṉṟeḻuntu ceṉṟu
viṇṇil maṟintatu.
-----
* (karantit tamiḻc caṅkak kallūriyiṉiṉṟu veḷivanta pañcāpakēcaṉ eṉṉum kiyeḻuttut
tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭṭiṉkaṇ muṉṉar veḷivantatōr moḻipeyarppu. āṅkila nūṟ kaṭṭurikaḷuḷ
amiḻntu tiḷitta niliyil ikkaṭṭuri āṅkilam taḻuviya tamiḻākkamāka amintatu) -
---------

2. uḻippu

“viṉiyē āṭavark kuyirē vāṇutal
maṉiyuṟi makaḷirkku āṭavar uyireṉa
namakkurit tōrum tāmē
aḻāaltōḻi avar aḻuṅkuvar celavē...”
      - kuṟuntoki: 135
naṟkuṇamum naṟceykiyum ciṟanta naṅkiyorutti, aṟivum āṇmiyum perukiya tōṉṟal
oruvaṉi maṇantu illaṟam pūṇṭu vantaṉaḷ. orunāḷ avaṉ, tāṉ ceyvataṟkuriya viṉiyoṉṟu
kuṟittut taṉ maṉiyiṉ nīṅkic cellavēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ. maṇantanāḷ toṭṭut taṉ kaṇavaṉip
pirintaṟiyāta annaṅkikku itu teriyiṉ, avaḷ peruvaruttam eytuvaḷ eṉpati attōṉṟal
eṇṇiṉaṉ. uṇmik kātalvaḻi yoḻukuvārkkup pirivupōlap peruntuṉpam taruvatu piṟitu
kiṭiyātu. "iṉṉātu iṉaṉillūr vāḻtal, ataṉiṉum iṉṉātu iṉiyārp pirivu" eṉṟu
tiruvaḷḷuvarum kūṟiyuḷḷār. ataṉāl taṉ karuttiṉi, avaṉ, avaḷatu tōḻikku urittu,
piṉṉar mellat taṉ maṉiyāṭkut terivikkumāṟu paṇittāṉ. avaḷum ataṟku oruvāṟu
uṭaṉpaṭṭu, kālam vāytta pōtu avaṉatu karuttiṉip piya vuṇarttiṉāḷ. kēṭṭāḷō illiyō,
naṅkikkup peruntuyar uṇṭākiviṭṭatu; kaṇṇīr viṭṭuk kataṟiyaḻat toṭaṅkiṉāḷ. avaḷi
āṟṟuvikkat toṭaṅkiya tōḻi, "naṅkāy, kaṇavaṉ collik kāppatu maṉivikkuk kaṭaṉ. avar
orunāḷ coṉṉatu oṉṟuṇṭu. atu niṉakkum terintatē. aḵtāvatu: 'āṭavarkkut tāṅkaḷ
ceyyum viṉiyē uyir; maṉiyuṟiyum makaḷirkku avvāṭavarē uyir. avarkku uyirākiya
viṉimēṟ ceṉṟāltāṉē, avar niṉakku uyirāka iruttalkūṭum; nī aḻutāl avar
viṉimēṟcelvatik kiviṭuvarē. ataṉāl ētamaṉṟō viḷiyum, "eṉṟaṉaḷ. atu kēṭṭa
annaṅkiyum tēṟi avaṉ pirivi āṟṟit taṉakkuriya aṟattiṉiyum āṟṟivantaṉaḷ.
icciṟiya pāṭṭiṉip pāṭiyavar pāli pāṭiya peruṅkaṭuṅkō eṉṉum cāṉṟōrāvar.
ivvāṟu viṉi mēṟ cella niṉinta talimakaṉ orunāḷ mālippōtu varakkaṇṭu, "ā. ippōtu
nam kātali nam maṉiyakattu viḷakkēṟṟi ataṉ muṉ niṉṟu, 'iṉṉum kātalar vantilarē' eṉṟu
niṉikkum pōtu aṉṟō?" eṉṟu taṉakkuḷ niṉittāṉ. niṉittavaṉ, immāli,
‘uḷḷiṉēṉ allaṉō yāṉē, uḷḷiya
viṉimuṭit taṉṉa iṉiyōḷ
maṉimāṇ cuṭaroṭu paṭarpoḻu teṉavē.”
      - naṟṟiṇi 3
eṉat taṉakkuḷ niṉintu iṉintāṉ eṉa iḷaṅkīraṉār kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivviru ceyyuṭkaḷiyum
nōkkuvōmāyiṉ, namakku ōr uṇmi pulappaṭum. aḵtāvatu, ōr āṇ makaṉukku avaṉ
ceytaṟkuriya viṉitāṉ uyir; ataṉi muṭitta vaḻiyuḷatākum iṉpam kātaliṉpam pōlvatu
eṉpatām.
viṉiyeṉpatu nām ceyyum ceykiyākum. ovvōruyirum ulakil tōṉṟumpōtē viṉi
ceyvataṟkeṉṟē piṟakkiṉṟatu. viṉiyē oruvaṉ eytum iṉpattukkum tuṉpattukkum
kāraṇamāvatu. iṉpattiyē evvuyirum peṟa virumpum; tuṉpatti virumpum uyir
etuvumilli. tuṉpam eṉṟa collik kēṭkumpōtē nam uḷḷam cuḷikkum; collutaṟkum
nākkuk kūcum. iṉpattit tarum viṉiyē yāvarum virumpum toḻilākum. ataṉāltāṉ
iṉpam tarakkūṭiyataṉi nalviṉi, naṟceyki eṉṟum, tuṉpam taruvataṉit tīviṉi, tīcceyki
eṉṟum collukiṉṟōm.
iṉi ivviṉiyic ceyyumpoḻutu nam uṭalum uyirum peritum pāṭupaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ivviraṇṭum oruṅkē kūṭic ceyyāta vaḻi, entat toḻilum naṭipeṟuvatu illi.
ettoḻilic ceyvatāyiṉum ivi uḻikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟaṉa. uyir uṭaloṭu
kūṭiyiruppatu ivvuḻippukkē; ivvuḻippāl, uyir uṭalpaṭum tuṉpamaṉittiyum tāṉ
paṭukiṟateṉṟālum, uyirkku uṭaliṉmēl uḷḷa paṟṟiṉāl uḻikka uḻikka, ataṟku ataṉmēl
paṟṟu mikukiṉṟatu; "tuṉpam uḻattoṟum kātaṟṟu uyir"eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟukiṉṟār.
eṉavē, uṭalōṭu kūṭi vāḻum ovvōruyirkkum uḻippu iyalpāka amintiruppati
aṟikiṟōm. āṉāl, ivvuyirkaḷuḷ makkaḷuyirmaṭṭil, ēṉi uyirkaḷi viṭac ciṟitu
mēmpaṭṭatākum. taṉ uḻippāl āṇṭavaṉ paṭippil uḷḷa ēṉi uyirkaḷiyum uyiril
poruḷkaḷiyum muṟiyē aṭimiyākavum uṭimiyākavum ākkik koḷḷaṟku vēṇṭiya urimi
makkaḷuyir peṟṟirukkiṟatu. ātalāṟṟāṉ iṉṟu maṉitaṉ nilam, nīr, tī, kāṟṟu, vicumpu
ākiya pūtam aintum tāṉ virumpumāṟu amiyac ceykiṉṟāṉ. nilattiṟkuḷ nuḻintu
kuṭintu celkiṉṟāṉ. nīriṉ mēlum kīḻum nimirntu celkiṉṟāṉ; kāṟṟi ēval
koḷḷukiṉṟāṉ; vicumpil paṟantēkukiṉṟāṉ. curuṅkac coṉṉāl, maṉitaṉ āṇṭavaṉukku
aṭuttapaṭiyāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟāṉ eṉṉalām.
maṉitaṉ ittuṇi ēṟṟamuṭiyavaṉāka iruttaṟkuriya urimi avaṉpāl nilittirukka
vēṇṭumāyiṉ, avaṉ calippiṉṟi uḻittal vēṇṭum. uḻippiṉāl avaṉ uṭal
tūymiyaṭikiṟatu. ataṉāl avaṉ uḷḷam oḷipeṟukiṟatu; appōtu āṇṭavaṉ avaṟku aruḷiyuḷḷa
urimi katirviṭṭut tikaḻkiṉṟatu. atuvaḻiyāka avaṉpāl uyariya eṇṇaṅkaḷum, ulavāta
iṉpamum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. makkaḷuṭ cilar, avvurimiyik kāttukkoḷḷum
valikuṉṟiyirukkiṉṟaṉar; urimiyi nilippikkum uḻippiṉi nekiḻttuc
cōmpaluṟukiṉṟaṉar. cōmpal uṭalik keṭukkiṉṟatu; ataṉāl uyir maṭaṅkukiṉṟatu; urimi
nilavum uḷḷam maṭikiṉṟatu. ākavē, cōmpal maṭimikku uyiri iriyākki, uḷḷattē
iruḷiyum uyirkku iṟutiyiyum viḷivikkiṉṟatu. oru periyāratu uṭaṉpiṟantār
iṟantupōṉār eṉpatu kēḷviyuṟṟu, vēṟoru periyār avari nōkkit "taṅkaḷ tampi
evvāṟu iṟantār?" eṉṟu kēṭṭataṟku, avar, "avaṉ uḻittaṟku iṉṟi iṟantāṉ" eṉṟārām.
ākavē, uḻippiṉṟic cōmpiyiruppavaṉ, virivil taṉ uyiri iḻakkiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ uḷḷattē,
uyirkkut tīṅku ceyyum tīya eṇṇaṅkaḷum aṟiyāmiyumē kuṭikoḷḷum eṉa aṟiyalām.
iṉṟu nām aḻakiya nakaraṅkaḷiyum, periya periya māṭa māḷikikaḷiyum, pacum kampaḷam
virittatupōlat tōṉṟum paranta paṇpaṭṭa vayalkaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟōm. kāṭaḻintu
nāṭākiyirukkiṟatu; karuṅkaṭalil peruṅkalam celkiṉṟatu; kampiyiṉṟiyē tanti
pēcukiṟatu; virivil, ‘'kaṇṭēṉ avar tiruppātam, kaṇṭaṟiyātaṉa kaṇṭēṉ" eṉa nam nāvaracar
pāṭiyati nāmum pāṭappōkiṉṟōm. iviyāvum uḻippiṉ payaṉaṉṟō! "uḻippiṉ vārā
uṟutikaḷ uḷavō?" eṉap paṭṭiṉattaṭikaḷ urittatu uṇmi moḻiyaṉṟō uḻippiṉṟi uṭal
cōmpi, uyir oyntu oṭuṅkiyirukkum oruvaṉip pāruṅkaḷ avaṉ uḷḷattil oḷiyilli.
ataṉi avalam, kavali, kiyāṟu, aḻuṅkal, āci, vekuḷi, aḻukkāṟu mutaliyaṉa cūḻntu
arikkiṉṟaṉa; avaṉum urukkulintumelikiṉṟāṉ. avaṉi ētēṉum oru toḻilicceyya
viṭuvōm: uṭaṉē, avaṉatu uṭalum uyirum oruppaṭṭu attoḻiṟkaṇ uḻikkat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
avaṉ vaṉmi muḻuvatum attoḻili muṭittaṟ kaṇ oṉṟiviṭukiṉṟatu. avaṉ uḷḷatti yalitta
aḻukkuṇarvukaḷaṉittum akalappōy eṅkēyō maṟintu pōkiṉṟaṉa. avaṉ maṉitaṉ, maṉitaṉāka
vayaṅkukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉatu uḷḷam pēroḷi viṭṭu miḷirkiṉṟatu; avvaḻukkuṇarvukaḷ
avvoḷiyiṉkaṇ pukintu erintu puttoḷi ceykiṉṟaṉa. ākavē, oruvaṉatu uḷḷattit tūymi
ceyvataṟku uḻippē ciṟanta maruntāka irukkiṟateṉpatu eḷitil viḷaṅkukiṟatu.
nām vāḻum immaṇṇulaku ātiyil ñāyiṟṟiliruntu pōntatu eṉṟum, aṉṟumutal iṉṟu
variyil cuḻaṉṟu koṇṭēyirukkiṟatu eṉṟum nām aṟintirukkiṉṟōm. itaṉi ivvāṟu
cuḻalvitta paramaṉ karuttu yātākal vēṇṭum? iṭiyaṟātu cuḻalum cuḻaṟciyiṉāl nām
vāḻum maṇṇulaku, viṇṇulakil vaṭṭamāka iyaṉṟu, pala vikāra uruvaṅkaḷip peṟṟu,
muṭivil, iṟṟiya uruṇṭi vaṭivattip peṟṟirukkiṟatu. cuḻalalāvatu niṉṟupōmāyiṉ,
innilavulakam yātāy muṭiyum? orucār pakalum maṟṟiya pakuti iruḷumāy evvuyirum
vāḻtaṟkiṉṟi, epporuḷum nilipeṟuvatiṉṟik keṭṭaḻiyumeṉpataṉic collavum vēṇṭumō
aḷittakka immaṇṇakam, iṭiyaṟaviṉṟic cuḻaṉṟuḻalum uḻippiṉāltāṉ itaṉpāl muṟi
tirumputalum, nērmi kōṭutalum piṟa tīmikaḷum ilavāyiṉa; vikāraṅkaḷ nīṅkic cīriya
ivvuruvamum amivatāyiṟṟu.
kulālaṉatuḻippum, avaṉatu taṇṭa cakkaramum maṉitaṉ uṇavaṭṭu uṇṇattoṭaṅkiya nāṇmutal
aṟintaṉavākum. camaya nūlkaḷum ilakkaṇa nūlkaḷum, avaṉiyē avaṉ ceyyum viṉiyiyum,
viṉikkuriya karuvi karaṇaṅkaḷiyum aṭikkaṭi eṭuttukkāṭṭit tam aṟivup poruḷi
vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. accakkarattiṉ naṭuvil vikkappaṭum maṇtiraḷip pāruṅkaḷ! ataṉiṭattil
ētēṉum pāṇṭavamippukkuriya vaṭivamuḷatō? aḻakiya uruvamum tiruntiya vaṭivamumuṭiya
maṭkalaṅkaḷ accakkarattiṉ cuḻaṟciyāl piṟakkiṉṟaṉa. aḵtu illiyāyiṉ akkulālaṉ maṭkalaṉkaḷi
yākkutal kūṭumō? ettuṇit toḻiṟciṟappum, uliyāvuḻippu muṭiyaṉāyiṉum avaṉ
yātuceyya muṭiyum? avaṉ ceyya muyalum kalaṅkaṭku aḻakum vaṭivum amiyuṅkol!
iyaṟkiyulakum, cakkarattuṇiyillāta kulālaṉippōla, ōyntu oṭuṅki niṟkumāyiṉ ataṉ
payaṉ eṉṉāy muṭiyum! avvāṟu ceyyātu calippiṉṟi, uḻikkumāṟu aruḷiya āṇṭavaṉi
eppōtum vaṇaṅkuvōmāka.
uḻittaliṉṟic cōmpik kiṭakkum oruvaṉ ettuṇip poruṇalaṅkaḷ uṭiyaṉāyiṉum, avaṟku
avi oru payaṉiyum ceyyā. cakaṭacakkaramiṉṟik kiyaṟṟu niṟkum kulālaṉpāl uḷḷa maṇtiraḷ
pōla, uḻippuc ciṟappillāta oruvaṉum veṟuntacip potiyum ciṟappil piṇṭamumāvatallatu
piṟitilli. kulālaṉi makaṉākavum, avaṉatu cakkaratti uḻippākavum koṇṭu nōkkuka.
uḻippu uyarvu tarum; uḻippāḷi ulakāḷvāṉ. ākavē, uḻippup peṟṟavaṉ
uṟutipeṟṟavaṉākiṉṟāṉ. uyir vāḻkkiyiṉ uṟutippayaṉ avaṉ pālēyuḷatu. uyarvu
vēṇṭuvōṉ uḻippu vēṇṭuka. karuvarivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum kikalantu
kalittōṭukiṉṟaṉa. iṭippaṭṭa māṇporuḷi varaṉṟi yēkum uḻippu vaṉmiyāl avi kalittuc
cerukkukiṉṟaṉa. aḻukkum, mācum, kuppiyum, kūḷamum avaṟṟiṉ neṟiyiṟ paṭṭu
alittuk koṇṭēkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kāl kālāy vayaliṭippaṭintu uramākit tām paravum nilap
parappip pacum pullum, naṟumalarum kaṇkavar vaṉappumuṭiya vaṇpoḻilākkum ciṟṟāṟukaḷiṉ
māṇṭoḻili eṉṉeṉpatu! avaṟṟiṉ teṇṇīr uṇṇīrākiṟatu. atu cumantu koṇarnta
aḻukkukaḷum avaṟṟōraṉṉa mācukaḷum māyntu pōkiṉṟaṉa. itaṉālaṉṟō, "uṇaveṉap paṭuvatu
nilattoṭu nīrē; nīrum nilaṉum puṇariyōr, īṇṭu, uṭampum uyirum paṭittici ṉōrē"
eṉak kuṭapulaviyaṉārum pāṇṭiyaṉ neṭuñceḻiyaṟkup pāṭiyaṟivuṟuttiṉār. ittakiya nīr
yāṟē uḻippeṉavaṟika.
pala tiṟamāy, palvakip poruḷkaḷiyākki, nāṭukaḷi nāṭā vaḷamuṭiyavākac ceytu, makkaḷi
iṉpamum, poruḷum, aṟamum, vīṭum iṉitu eytuvittu, avartam vāḻkkiyiṉ perumpayaṉi
nukarvippatu uḻippēyākum. ākavē, uḻippē uyir eṉa aṟikiṉṟōm. uyir uṭalōṭu kūṭi
vāḻkki naṭattuntōṟum, ataṉpāl pēriṉpam peṟutalpōla, uḻippāḷiyum uḻikkuntōṟum
uvaṭṭāta pēriṉpamum ūkka mikutiyum peṟukiṉṟāṉ. āṇṭavaṉ tiruvaruḷ avaṉ uḷḷattē
ūṟukiṟatu. uyar nōkkum, oḷḷaṟivum, perun taṉmiyum avaṉpāl uḷavāki yōṅkukiṉṟaṉa.
nōṉmi, tuṇivu, muyaṟci, aṟivup pēṟṟiṉkaṇ ārvam, taṉkuṟṟam tērntu kaṭital
mutaliyaṉa iṉpa vāḻvukku ākkamākum nallaṟaṅkaḷ. uṇartta uṇarvatilum, ivaṟṟi nāmē nam
uḻippu vāyilāka uṇarntu naṉṉeṟikaḷiṟ celuttuvatē nam uyir vāḻkkiyākum. uḻippu
vāyilāka ivaṟṟi nām uṇartal vēṇṭum. nōṉmi eṉpatu ettuṇi iṭukkaṇkaḷ
aṭukkivariṉum, avaṟṟiṟku aḻiyātu, avaṟṟāl varum tuṉpattip poṟuttu mēṟkoḷḷum
taṉmiyākum. aṅṅaṉam mēṟ koḷḷutaṟkuriya tuṇiyākum maṉattiṭpamē tuṇivu eṉpatu.
tuṇintavaḻi, iṭiyil vantu takiyum iṭiyūṟukaḷāl uḷḷamuṭiyāmal, ceyviṉiyik kaṭipōka
uḻittu veṟṟikāṇum tiṟam muyaṟciyākum. ataṉkaṇ aṟiyātaṉavaṟṟi aṟintāṟṟum viraku
vēṇṭiyiruttaliṉ, aṟivaṟiyum ārvam vēṇṭuvatāyiṟṟu. avvārvattāl, mutaṟkaṇ tāmē
tērtalum piṟar kāṭṭak kāṇṭalum ākiya iru neṟikaḷāl tam kuṟṟattik kaṇṭu ataṉik
kaṭivatum, ceyta kuṟṟatti iṉic ceyyā vakiyil tammip pātukāttu, aṭutta muṟiyil
naṉkāṟṟal vēṇṭum eṉṉum naṟpaṇpuṭiyaṉāvatum uḻippāḷikku iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavākum.
ippaṇpukaḷik kikkoṇṭu uḻippōrmuṉ, vāṉṟōy maliyaṉiya pēriṭarkaḷ vantu niṟkum.
"naṟceyaluk kaṉṟē nāṉūṟu iṭiyūṟu." āyiṉum uṇmi yuḻippāḷiyō, ēṉiyavaṟṟāl varum
"iṉpam viḻiyāṉ; iṭumpi iyal peṉpāṉ; tuṉpam uṟutal ilaṉ." karumamē kaṇṇāka uḻikkum
avaṉ, "mey varuttam pārāṉ; paci nōkkāṉ; kaṇ tuñcāṉ; evvevar tīmiyum mēṟkoḷḷāṉ;
cevviyarumiyum pārāṉ; avamatippum koḷḷāṉ." cuṭac cuṭarum poṉpōl, avaṉ uḷḷam
tuṉpaṅkaḷ toṭarntu pōntu cuṭaccuṭa, oṭpamum tiṭpamum peṟṟu ōṅki yoḷirum.
nilavulakiṉum, avaṉ pukaḻē ōṅkip poṉṟātu niṉṟu nilavum, attuṉpaṅkaḷum "eri
muṉṉar vittuṟu pōlak keṭum.”
ilaṇṭaṉ nakarattil uḷḷa ceyiṉṭ pāl katīṭral eṉṉum peruṅkōyil uliyāvuḻippiṉ
uruvuṭip payaṉākum. ataṉi yeṭuttaṟku muyaṉṟa car kiṟistapar reṉ eṉpār, kaṇitanūl,
vāṉanūl, ciṟpanūl mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ perum pulamiyum viṉittiṭpamum uṭiyar. āyiṉum,
avar itaṉik kaṭṭiyamittaṟku muyaṉṟa kālattu uḷavākiya iṭiyūṟukaṭku aḷavēyilli. urōm
nakarattuc ceyiṉṭ pīṭṭars peruṅ kōyil pōlavē ikkōyiliyum eṭuttal vēṇṭumeṉa eṇṇi
muyaṉṟa avartam eṇṇam tuārntu pōmāṟu, araciyaṟ pēratikārikaḷum camayap paṇiyāḷarum
piṟarum maṇṇaḷḷi eṟinta vaṇṇamē iruntaṉar; poruḷutavi purivōr iruḷil maṟintaṉar;
kiṟistaparukkum iṭukkaṇ pala aṭukki varalāyiṉa. āyiṉum eṉ? "maṭuttavāyellām
pakaṭaṉṉāṉ uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ iṭarppāṭuṭittu" eṉṟa tiruvaḷḷuvap perumoḻipōla,
avviṭiyūṟukaḷum iṭarppaṭṭut tāmē yaḻintaṉa. katīṭral kaṇkavar vaṉappum maṇmakiḻ tōṟṟamum
peṟṟu iṉṟum niṉṟu nilavum peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ tikaḻum cemmi yeytiṟṟu. iṉṟum
ataṉik kāṇpōr, "periyār" eṉap poṟittuḷḷa perum peyarik kāṇuntōṟum, nam
kiṟistapariṉ viṉittiṭpam, muyaṟci mutaliya viṉimāṇpukaḷi niṉintu pārāṭṭā
niṟkiṉṟaṉar.
tiruvum kalviyum ciṟantu viḷaṅkum nāṭukaḷuḷ ippōtu amerikka nāṭu ettuṟiyilum
īṭumeṭuppumiṉṟi viḷaṅkuvataṉi nām aṟikiṉṟōm. itaṉ uṇmiyiṉi yuṇarntu kaṇṭu
uritta kolampas eṉṉum periyāri mēṉāṭṭavar aṉivarum, nam nāṭṭavaruṭ kaṟṟavarum
naṉkaṟivar. nam nāṭṭiṟkup putuneṟi yoṉṟu kāṇappukunta apperiyār avvamerikka
nāṭṭiṉik kaṇṭaṟintaṉarā yiṉum, ataṉi mutaliṟ kāṇṭaṟkaṇ avar uṟṟa iṭukkaṇ urikkun
taramuṭiyataṉṟu. putuneṟi kāṇṭal kūṭumeṉṟa eṇṇam avar uḷḷattiṟ pukunta nāḷ mutal,
putupperu nāṭākiya ataṉit tam kaṇkaḷāṟ kāṇun tuṇiyum, avari, avamatippu,
accuṟuttal, aṟiyāmi, ikaḻcci, ēmāṟṟam, vacivu, koli, accam mutaliya pala ceyalkaḷ
pallāṟṟālum arittu alittaṉa. nāṭṭiṉ kāṭciyi yeytutaṟku iraṇṭoru nāḻiki
yirukkumpōtum, avar ceṉṟa vaṅkattiliruntōr avarip paṟṟik koli ceytuviṭavum
tuṇintaṉar: malipōl aliyeḻuntu muḻaṅkum maṟikaṭal, muṉṉē kiṭantu, maruṭci
viḷivittatu; piṉṉē avaratu nāṭu neṭuntoliviṟ kiṭantu, vaṟitē tirumpiṉ avari
ikaḻnteḷḷi iṉṉaṟ , paṭuttaṟku etirnōkkiyiruntatu; arukil cūḻa viruntōr
maṭittavāyum, veṭitta collum, kaṭutta nōkkum uṭiyavarāyt tīviṉiyē cūḻntu
koṇṭiruntaṉar.
kaṇantōṟum, niliyiṉṟip puraḷum alikaḷ avartam kalattic celavoṭṭātu taṭuttaṟ keḻuntaṉa
pōla veḻuntu alippa, avaṟṟiṭiyē oṭuṅkā vuḷḷamum kalaṅkāk koḷkiyu mallatu
piṟitillāta avar maṉat tiṭpam, avvalikaḷiṉ taliyi mitittēkiyatu; virikaṭalic
curukkiṟṟu; neṭumpōtu kuṟum pōtāka, nīrkkāṟṟu nilakkāṟṟāka, pakal māya, irā
vaṇuka, viṉi muṭiviṉ tuṉivu mika, amerikkap putu nāṭṭiṉ aṭikari pulaṉāyiṟṟu,
aṭart taṟkeḻunta alikaḷ avarit talimēṟ cumantu tālāṭṭiṉa; vaṉkāṟṟāy maṭittaṟku niṉṟa
kaṭaṟkāṟṟu, meṉkāṟṟāy avar melivu tīrttu makiḻvittatu, ītaṉṟō viṉimāṇpu! eṉṟum
poṉṟāta iṉpavici nalkum - viṉiyiṉum, uḻippiṉum ēṟṟamuṭiya toṉṟu uṇṭukol!
illi! illi! eññāṉṟum illi!
iṉi ivvuḻippiṉi nam paṇṭic cāṉṟōr āḷ - viṉiyeṉṟē vaḻaṅki yirukkiṉṟaṉar.
uḷḷattiṉ āṭci vaḻi niṟkum uṭal puriyum viṉi uḻippātalāl, ataṉi āḷviṉi yeṉṟatu
ettuṇi aḻakuṭittu! kāṇmiṉ, avvāḷviṉiyē āṭavarkku uyir eṉpataṉāl, āṭavaṉi āṇmakaṉ
eṉṟal ettuṇi aṟivuṭi moḻi! kāṇmiṉ āṇmakkaḷē! uṅkaḷatu āṇmi, āḷviṉiyē, ayarā,
uḻippē, ataṉiyuṭiya nīvir eṉ ceykiṉṟīrkaḷ? ayarntu uṟaṅkukiṉṟīrkol? uṟaṅkaṉmiṉ;
uṟaṅkutal uyvaṟṟārkkē uriyatu, ōyvu uliyā uḻippuṭiyārkkē uriyatu. uḻippavar
ōyvu kōṭaṟkuriyar, ōyvuṟṟavar uṟaṅkutaṟ kuriyar; uṟaṅkuvōr mīṭṭum uḻittaṟku uṭaṟkum
uḷḷattiṟkum urantarupavarāvār. uṅkaḷatu uṟakkam mīṭṭum uḻittal vēṇṭi uṅkaṭku ūkkam
ceytaṟkamintatu; ataṟkuriya pōtu iravuppōtē, ākavē, pakaṟpōtu uḻippuk kuriyatākum.
uḻippukkuriya appakaṟpōtu uṟaṅkutaṟkuriya taṉṟu; ataṉāl pakaṟpōtil naṉku uḻimiṉ.
ulakam virintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu; itaṉpāl uyariya payaṉ uḷatu; uḻippavar appayaṉi
nukartaṟkuriyar; ulakiyaḷitta oruvaṉ uṅkaṭku uṭampu nalkiyuḷaṉ; avvuṭampiṉi ulakil
iyakki uṟupayaṉ peṟutaṟkuriya uṟukaruviyāka uṅkaṭku, avaṉ uḷḷattiyum utaviyuḷaṉ,
uḷḷattāl uḷkiyavaḻi uḻittaṟ kurimiyum, āṟṟalum uṅkaḷpāl uḷa eṉpatu viḷaṅkum.
avaṟṟi veḷippaṭuttal uṅkaḷ kaṭaṉ. āṟṟalum urimiyumuṭiya nīvir uḻikkumiṭattu,
eṅkeṅku muṟi piṟaḻcci tōṉṟukiṉṟatō, aḵtu uṅkaḷ uyirkkiṟuti payakkum
uṟupakiyātaliṉ, aṅkaṅku muṟimiyi niṟuvutal vēṇṭum; nelviḷiyum vayaliṭattup pul
muḷiya viṭuvirkol turumpāyiṉum virumpi pīṭṭumiṉ paruttiyiṉ pañcu kāṟṟiṟ
paṟakkum puṉmittāyiṉum viṭātu paṟṟumiṉ, īṭṭumiṉ; pāṅkuṟa nūṉmiṉ; aḵtu aṟṟam
maṟikkum āṭiyākumē; "aṇiyellām āṭiyiṉ piṉ.” uḻippiṟku “ūṟu ceyvaṉa piṟavum
cilavuḷa. avi aṟiyāmiyum, maṭamiyumākum. aṟitaṟkriya amiti peṟṟum, aṟiya
vēṇṭuvaṉavaṟṟi aṟiyaviṭātu, maruṭciyum accamum payappittuc ceyviṉi citaṟuvittaliṉ,
aṟiyāmi uḻippiṟku ūṟu ākum. aṟivuṭiyōr aṟivuṟuppavum, āṟṟaṟkuriya viṉiyum
urimiyum arukil iruppavum, āḷ viṉikkaṇ karutti iruttātu, maṭimikkaṇ maṭintu
kiṭappittaliṉ, maṭamiyiṉit takart teṟitalum āṇmakaṟku aṟamākum. ataṉic ceyyavēṇṭu
meṉpatu āṇṭavaṉ āṇi. "iṉṟu", “ippoḻutu", “ikkaṇam” eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum kālamē, ataṉi
ayarātu āṟṟaṟkaminta arumik kālamākum. ayarntavaḻi iravu vantuviṭum; akkālattil
evarum uṟaṅkuvarē yaṉṟi uḻittalic ceyyār.
ellā vaki uḻippum ēṟṟamuṭiyaṉavākum; avaṟṟuḷ kiyāl uḻikkum uḻippil
kaṭavuṭṭaṉmiyuṇṭu eṉak kārlil eṉṉum periyār kaviṉak kūṟukiṉṟār; ikkaṭṭurikkaṇ
aṭaṅkiya karuttukaḷi vaḻaṅkiya perum pulavarum avarē. uḻippu ulakēpōl paranta
paṇpiṉiyuṭiyatu; itaṉ uyarvu vīṭṭulakiṉ ucciyiluḷatu. mutaṟkaṇ uṭalilum, piṉ
mūḷiyilum, muṭivil uḷḷattilum oṉṟiyulavip payaṉ uṟuvikkum uḻippē, kepḷar kaṇṭa
kōṇilik kaṇakkum, niyūṭṭaṉ nikaḻttiya neṭum poruḷārāycciyum, aṟiviyaṟkalikaḷum,
āṇmic ceyalkaḷum iṉṉuyir vaḻaṅki ici naṭum iyalpum yāvum tōṟṟuvitta ēṟṟamuṭiyatu.
ātalāl, āṇ kaṭaṉ iṟukkum māṇpuṭi makkāḷ! uḻippē āṇṭavaṉ uruveṉavuṇarmiṉ. ataṟku
aḻiyātu āṟṟalē avaṉatu tiruppaṇiyeṉa aṟimiṉ; paranta ulakil ciṟantōṅkiya perumakkaḷ
kaṇṭuṇarn turitta mutuporuḷum ituvēyām.
uḻippiṉi veṟuttu nonturikkum oruvaṉ uḷaṉō? uḷaṉāyiṉ, avaṉi atu ceyya vēṇṭāveṉa
urimiṉ. oṭuṅkikkiṭakkum avaṉ muṉ ceṉṟu, "aṉpa, oṭuṅkutal oḻika. itō, uṉpōl
uḻitta tōḻarkaḷ uyarnili yulakattil iṉpañ ciṟantu iṉitē uṟikiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷaṉṟō āṅkē
iṉpuṟukiṉṟavar; piṟar evarum ilarē in nilavulakiṉum, uḻippunalattāl uyarnta varaṉṟō,
makkaḷ maṉanilattil maṉṉi, vāṉavarum muṉivararum pōlak kālaṅkaḻiyiṉum kōlaṅ kaḻiyātu
eṉṟum niṉṟu nilavukiṉṟaṉar", eṉat teruṭṭit teḷivuṟuttumiṉ.
------------

3. varaṉeṉṉum vippu

uṭampoṭu kalantu tōṉṟum uyirttokiyuṭ ciṟappuṭiyaveṉa uyarntōrāṉ uṇarttappeṟum
makkaḷuyirkku uṟutipporuḷeṉap periyōr vakuttuk kūṟum nālvakip poruḷkaḷuḷ
vīṭeṉpatum oṉṟu. atu cintiyum moḻiyum cellā nilimittu eṉap periyōr kūṟuvar.
kūṟiṉum, atu ivvulakam pōl aḻitaṉmālittāya iṉpattiṉit tarātu, eññāṉṟum kuṉṟā
viṉpam tarum pērulakameṉavum, ataṉi nāṭiyē uyirkaḷ niṟṟal vēṇṭumeṉavum nam āṉṟōr
kūṟuvataṉōṭu amiyātu, avvulakanāṭṭam uyirkaṭku iyaṟkiyilē amintuḷateṉṟum, atu,
āyum uṇarvuṭiyārkkē pulaṉāmeṉṟum kūṟiṉar; kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. akkūṟṟiṉi oruvāṟu
ārāyntu kaṇṭa vaḻiyē, īṇṭuk kūṟappeṟukiṉṟatu.
puviyiṉukku aṇiyāy āṉṟa poruḷtantu, caviyuṟat teḷintu, taṇṇeṉṟu oḻukkamum
taḻuvic cāṉṟōr kaviyeṉak kiṭanta kāṉyā ṟoṉṟiṉ aṭikariyiṉkaṇ, veyiṉuḻipaṟiyāk
kuyiṉuḻi potumpa roṉṟiruntatu. ataṉ kaṇ eṇṇiṟanta puḷḷiṉam, maṇṇuṟu vāḻkkikku
māṇpuṟuttum ceviccuviyamutam kēṭpōr cevippulam vaḷam peṟa aḷittu vāḻtara,
avviṉattu ḷoṉṟāya iṉṉicic cempuḷoṉṟu peṭiyuṭaṉ kiḷimalka av vaṭiciṉip
potumparuḷ, peṟṟatukoṇṭu, pēraṟam nērntu, peṟalarum iṉpamē tuyttu vantatu. ataṉāl,
atu, eṅkeḻileṉ ñāyiṟu eṉṉum - iṟivaṉaruḷā leytum itaṉic cempakam eṉṟum
vaḻaṅkupa.
neñciṟumāppum, paḻiyañci yīṭṭum poruḷiṉpa muṭimiyiṉ, vaḻiyeñcā vāṉporuḷum
uṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ākavē, ataṟku iṉi vēṇṭaṟpāla teṉṉām? oṉṟumilli yaṉṟō!
niṟka, orunāḷ, ic cempuḷḷiṉ vāḻkkittuṇiyāy, ottavuṇarvum pāṉmiyu muṭiyatāy,
tuṇiccēvaṟku oruvāṟṟāṉu muyarvutāḻvillāk kaviṉuṅ kāṭciyu muṭiyatāy viḷaṅkiya
peṭippuḷ, taṉ kūṭṭakat tamarntu, pārppukkaṭku uṇavāvaṉavaṟṟi yaḷittu uvantirukkiyil,
pakalceyyum ceññāyiṟu pāviyiṟ paṭiya, piya iruḷ vantu parava, iṉpatteṇkāl
iṭivantulava, arukōṭiya aruvikka ṇeḻunta ciṟutiri porutuvīḻtaliṉ eḻunta viḻumiya
vōciyāṅkaṇum parava, virikatirmatiyam viṇṇakat teḻuntu, maṇṇaka maṭantiyiṉ mayak
koḻippatupōlat taṉ oṇkatir parappiṟṟu. atu kāli, ap pārppukkaḷu ḷoṉṟu, taṉ
ciṟutaliyi nīṭṭi, aṉpukaḷiaiyum āymuka nōkki, "aṉṉāy! vāḻi! nam paṭappit
tēṉmayaṅku pāliṉum iṉiya it teṇṇīryāṟu celliṭam yātukol” eṉa viṉava, kēṭṭa
tāyppuḷ, viṭiyaṟiyāmiyiṉ, viḷampal kūṭātu, kuṟunaki kāṭṭi, “makkāḷ! yāṉ
aṟiyēṉ. kaliṉ eṉak kalikkum tūkkaṇaṅkuruviyiyātal, kaṭpulaṅkatuvā viṭpulam paṭarum
vāṉampāṭiyi yātal kēṭṭal vēṇṭum. āṇṭut tōṉṟum kōṭṭeṅkiṉ kulimītu avi orukāl
vantamariṉum amarum. atu pōḻtu nīvir maṟavaṟka. nīvir kēṭṭatum, ataṉōraṉṉa piṟavum
aṟitalām eṉṟu kūṟi, vāṉampāṭiyiṉ vaḷḷiciyit taṉ meṉkuralāl, vaṭittum untiyum
uṟaḻntum icippa, kurīikkuñcukaḷ kuḷirppeyti, “vāṉampāṭikol ik kāṉam pāṭiyatu”
eṉṟeṇṇalāyiṉa.
maṟṟu, av vāṉampāṭum vaṇkuruku, taṉ mellici koṇṭu, kāṭum, kāvum, kaviṉpeṟu
turuttiyum, yāṟum kuḷaṉum, vēṟupal vippum, ūrum nakaramum uṟitarum illamum,
ūṟucey vilaṅkum, ūṉuṇ vēṭṭuvar virikkum kaṇṇiyum, piṟavum meypeṟa vicittal
marapu. ataṉiyoru kālēṉum arukamarntu naṉku kēṭṭaṟiyā ic cempuḷ, pāṭuṅkāl, av
vicipporuḷ muṟiyiṟ ciṟitu piṟaḻtalum kuṟitalumāyiṉa. iḻukkuṭiya pāṭṭāyiṉum,
ici naṉṟāyiṉmi koṇṭu pēruvaki mīturat tāmum, tam aṉṉiyoppa, mellici miḻaṟṟal
toṭaṅkiṉa. iṅṅaṉam kuravarāyiṉār ceyyum ceyviṉiyi makkaḷāyiṉār, nuṇuki nōkki,
ammuṟiyē ceytal uyirttokiyuṭ ciṟantu viḷaṅkum iyalpukaḷ palaviṉuḷoṉṟu. ituvē,
ēṉiyaṟivuvaḷarccikkum piṟavaṟṟiṟkum orutaliyāka vaminta aṭippaṭi yeṉpatu kalviyāḷar
kōḷ. itaṉi yīṇṭurippiṟ perukum.
veḷḷi muḷittatu; viṭiyal vantatu; paṟavi pāṭiṉa; palluyirum paravukkaṭaṉ ceytaṉa;
viḻunta ñāyiṟum eḻuntatu; meṉkāṟṟu vīcaluṟṟatu; pūnāṉkum potuḷi veṟi tici
nāṉkum pōyulava, curumpu tōairntaṉa. cēkkiyuḷuṟaṅkik kiṭanta cempuṭkuñcukaḷ, uṟakka
nīṅki, orupuṭi vantu, uvaki kūrntaṉa. atupōtu, tāyppuḷ, taṉ celvac ciṟu
kuñcukaḷiṉ maruṅkamarntu, taṉ melliciyit toṭuttu, yāṟṟunīrccelaviṉmēṟ pāṭiṟṟu.
akkāli kariyāk kallum pullum kaṉinturukiṉa. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ, avvici yiṉpattāl,
“maruviyakāl vicittaciyā; maraṅkaḷmalarc ciṉicaliyā;
karuvarivīḻ aruvikaḷum kāṉyāṟum kalittōṭā;
perumukiliṉ kulaṅkaḷpuṭi peyarntoḻiyap puṉalcōrā;
iruvicumpiṉ iṭimuḻaṅkā; eḻukaṭalum iṭituḷumpā.”
eṉun tīṅkaviyē amiyum. -
iṅṅaṉam ciṟantōṅkiya iciyoṉṟil, ap puḷ vīṟuṟṟatē yoḻiya, ataṉuḷattil, ciṟukuñcu
viṉaviya poruḷmāttiram pulaṉāyiṟṟaṉṟu. āyiṉum tāṉum taṉpārppukkaḷum orupakal
avvāṟu celliṭam cēṟal vēṇṭu meṉṉum tippiya vuṇarvoṉṟu ataṉ neñcat takalātu
nilavuvatāyiṟṟu. ivvāṟu nāṭkaḷ pala ceṉṟaṉa. appuḷḷum yāṟṟunīrccelavu poruḷākap
pāṭi vantamiyi nīkkip piṟitōr iṭamoṉṟaṉi nāṭip pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkāli
avviṭamē, ivvāṟu celliṭamām eṉpatu ataṟkut tuṇipāyiṟṟu. ivviṭattik
kēṭkuntoṟum ap pārppukkaḷum tam tāyppaṟaviyi, ataṉik kuṟitta viṉākkaḷ pala
nikaḻttātu oḻivatiṉṟu.
niṟka, ivviṭam yātāka virukkalām? cilar itaṉi nāmyāṇṭiruntu pōntōmō āṇṭiyatu
eṉpar. vēṟu cilar nām iṉic cellumiṭam yātu atu veṉpar. cilar, ittakiya iṭamē
illi yeṉpar. vēṟu palar nāmum piṟavum nīṅkiyatum, nīkkamaṟa iṉic cēruvatu māya iṭam
eṉpar. itupaṟṟik kūṟaṟpālaṉa oruvāṟu kōṭaṟpāla valla veṉpatu kāṭci yoṉṟiyē aḷaviyāk
koḷvār tuṇipu. maṟṟu, nām kāṇṭal, karutal, uri yeṉṉu mūṉṟiyum aḷaviyāk koḷḷum
pāṉmiyē mākalāṉum, avaṟṟāṉārāyuṅ kālattu, uḷattiṟ ṟōṉṟum cila vuṇarvukaḷum,
uṇarntōr palar kūṟiya urikaḷum appeṟṟittāya iṭamoṉṟuṇṭeṉpataṉi valiyuṟuttukiṉṟaṉa.
āyiṉum, ataṉi oruvāṟu nām kāṇṭaṟkumuṉ aṟiyāvulakam eṉa vaḻaṅkuvōm. mutaṉūlārum
ivvāṟē vaḻaṅkukiṉṟār. aṉṟiyum, ivvulakip paṟṟi, iruṇṭa vuṇarvukaḷē ap paṟaviyiṉ
uṇarvuṭ kalantu kiṭantaṉa.
iṉi, at tāyppuḷ, tāṉē taṉittamarntu, avvulaku paṟṟiya vuṇarvukaḷ taṉ
maṉatteḻuntōṟum, icittuk kuḷirntu nāvil ūṟāta amiḻtūṟa. uṭal puḷakittu
uḷḷamellām urukik kaḷikkum. ik kaḷiyāṭṭiṉip piṉṉar atu, taṉ pārppukkaḷ kāṇavum
kēṭkavum ceytayartal toṭaṅkiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉam ceytaṟkuk kāraṇam yātākum? ap pārppukkaḷum
itaṉit toṭarvaṉavallavō? avaṟṟiṟkum avvulaka vuṇarviṉi yuṇarttal vēṇṭumaṉṟō? atu
pōlum kāraṇam!
iv vaṇṇam nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. orunāḷ ak kuñcukaḷu ḷoṉṟu, āymukanōkki, av vulaku
yāṇṭuḷa teṉṟu kēṭpa, atu, “avvulakam uṇṭeṉpatu uṇmi: maṟṟu, yāṉ aḵtu uṟumiṭam
aṟiyēṉ. ākaliṉ,
----
“I may call it the Unknown Land."
-------------
avviṭatti yāṉ kūṟutal kūṭātu" eṉṟatu. eṉṟalum, avaṟṟuṭṭalik kuñcu muṉṉiṉṟu,
"iv yāṟu cellumiṭam yātu, atu av aṟiyāvulakam!" eṉṟatu .itu uṇmi kol? aṉṟu.
av yāṟu, maṟuvilmāṉavar malinta mūtūr veṟitu cēṟal viḻuppamataṉṟeṉa malipaṭuporuḷum
kāṉpaṭu celvamum talimaṇantukoṇṭu, perunakaraṭintu, kālvaḻic ceṉṟu, vayaliṭip
paṭarntu, ataṉkaṇ viḷiyum piṅkūḻ vaḷarttu, vaṇikar marakkalam aṇipeṟa mitappa, maṅkiyar
kuṭintu māṇalam puṉiya, purinūl mārpar aruṅkaṭaṉāṟṟa, mīṉuṇkurukum, tuṇṭil
vēṭṭuvarum, tiṇṭimilvāḻnarum celvam mīkkoḷap parantu ceṉṟu, kōṉōkki vāḻuṅ
kuṭiyaṉittum vāṉōkkaliṉ, ataṟkiṉṟiyamiyāp perumaḻi piṟakkum peruntirip
puṇariyip puṇarum! ippuṇariyō aṟiyā ulakeṉappaṭuvatu pakaliṉ pāṉmiyum iraviṉ
eḻilum puḷḷiṉattuk korupolivum māviṉattukkorumāṇpum vayaṅkat taruvaṉavāka,
iviyaṉittum tanta voruvaṉ, ivi celliṭamoṉṟic ceyyātu viṭuvaṉō?
itu niṟka, "aṉṉāy aṟiyāvulakiṉ aḻakiṉik kūṟuvatāl ākumpyaṉ yātukol ataṉip
palakālum pāṭip pukaḻtal eṟṟukku?" eṉṟu toṭaṅkiya oru kuṟumpuḷ. "tēṉuāṟṟeṭuppa
maṇanāṟum pūmpoḻilakattiviṭṭu nām vēṟiṭam cēṟal eṉṉi? meṉṟaḷirum naṟumalarum
mikipaṭac ceṟinta kūṭoṉṟākki, nām ēṉ eṉṟum īṇṭē vāḻtal kūṭātu? vāṉkuyilka
ḷicipāṭa varivaṇṭu pāṇmiḻaṟṟa, māṉkaṉṟu payiṉṟuḷḷa, palluyirum poliveyta viḷaṅkum
ivviḷamarakkāviṉum iṉitukol av aṟiyāvulakam ? āruyiraṉṉāy! nām vēṟiṭañcēṟal
vēṇṭā. itupōtu, cintāmaṇi teṇkaṭalamirtam tilliyāṉaruḷāl vantālikaḻappaṭumē
muyaṟciyiṉṟiyē perum payaṉ eytutaṟkuriya iṭam atuvāyiṉum, yāṉ ivviṭam viṭṭup
peyarēṉ. oḻika. iṉi vēṟupulañ cēṟal. pintāṭ kōriyum, paciya taḷirniṟinta putalum
mikka iv vaṭikariyiṉum iṉitukol av aṟiyāvulakam ākaliṉ, aṉṉāy! iṉippāṭutaloḻika!
pāṭutaṟku uḷam oruppaṭutalum oḻika!" eṉṟatu.
piṉṉar attāyppuḷ, taṉmaṉattup palavēṟuvakippaṭṭa eṇṇaṅkaḷ paṭarnteḻun taṭaṅka,
veytuyirttu, vēṟu kūṟā toḻiya, maṟṟoru ciṟukuruku kūṟum. atu, ‘iṟutta iruḷ
keṭa, yāṇṭum iḷaṅkālum, iṉṉoliyum eḻa, poṉṉurukkeṉṉa iḷaveyil parappi, viṭiyaliṟ
kīḻkkaṭaliṉ mukaṭṭeḻum iḷañāyiṟṟiṉik kāṇāy! teṇṇiriṉ tiriyiṭi, acceññāyiṟṟiṉ
ceṅkatirkaḷ tavaḻtalum, āṇṭup pal vaṇṇaṅkaḷ tōṉṟi miḷirtal kāṇāy! varikkeṇṭiyum
paruvarālum naṇpakaloḷiyil miṉceytu maṟitalum, iḷantaḷirkaḷ oḷiteṟittalum piṟavum
kāṇāy! taṇṭeṉṟal taḷiracippa, pārppukkaḷ kiḷipaṭarntu icipāṭa, putumalariṉ maṇam parava,
kāliyaṇintu pakalellām oḷiyiṉṟiyirunta kaviṉaṉittum, māliyiṟṟuvara muṭittut
tōṉṟum iyalaṇaṅkiṉ eḻiṟ cevviyi maṟattalum kūṭumā? īṇṭāyiṉum, āṇṭāyiṉum,
yāṇṭāyiṉum amarka. veṇmatiyiṉ taṇkatirkaḷ pin taḻikaḷiṉmītu paravi, kalittōṭum
aruvinīril āṭal payiṉṟu, pūṅkāvi vāṉamākki, kāṉyāṟṟi, attiṅkaṭ puttē ḷurntu
cellun tērvaḻiyākka, viḷaṅkum iravup polivi niṉittoṟum neñcam vēṟōriṭattiyum
niṉikkuṅ kol tiṇkalamum ciṟunāvāyum celvuḻiccelvuḻi yeḻum oyyeṉum ōci nam
uṟakkam keṭuppa, neṭuntaruviṉ piṅkiḷi paṭarntu pāṭali nam iṉam payila, iṉpakkāṭciyē
yāṇṭum ilaṅkum in nilattiṉum av aṟiyānilam aḻakuṭittō?
"tuñcuvatu pōla iruḷi, viṇpaka
imippatu pōla miṉṉi, uṟikkoṇ(ṭu)
ēṟuvatu pōlap pāṭuciṟan(tu) urii
nilaneñ(cu) uṭka ovātu cilittāṅ(ku)
ārtaḷi poḻinta vārpeyaṟ kaṭināḷ.”
nām nam paṭiyami cēkkiyip paṭarntu, oṉṟōṭoṉṟu pulli irukkiṉ kārceypaṇippum
kalaṅkutuyar ceyyātāka, eḻunta vemmiyāl iṉpam mikum ivvirum poḻiliṉum, nī kūṟum
appūm poḻil ēṟṟamuṭittāmō? ātaliṉ, aṉṉāy vēṟupulam muṉṉutali viṭṭoḻittu,
ippulam paṟṟiya iṉṉiciyē miḻaṟṟuti!’ eṉṟatu.
piṉṉar, attāyāya cempuḷ, iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟiya taṉ celvakkuñciṉ peruñcoṟkaḷik kēṭṭu,
oruvāṟu, uḷattit tirutti, “celvaṅkāḷ iṉi nīvir virumpiya vaṇṇamē vēṟoru pāṭṭē
pāṭuval. pōṟṟik kēṇmiṉ eṉṟu oru mellici, poruḷ ceṟiyap pāṭutal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
apporuḷ:
“paranta ulakiṭit tōṉṟiya yāvum taṉ poṟipulaṉkaḷiṉ vaḻiyē maṉattiṟkuk kuṉṟāviṉpamum,
accamaṟiyā viyalpum nalkat tāṉ peṟṟuvāḻntamiyum, av vāḻkkiyil nāṭpala cella,
taṉakku uḷḷiruntē, iḵtu niṉṉiṭamaṉṟu eṉa vōrporuṇmoḻi yeḻunticittamiyum;
ammoḻi, piṉ palamuṟiyum icippak kēṭṭa tāṉ, orukāl uvakiyum, orukāl veṟuppum,
palakālum kēṭṭaṟ koruppaṭātu cēṟalum koṇṭiruntamiyum; irukkiyil, tāṉ vāḻumiṭamē
mētakka iṭamāvatu, ataṉi nīṅkutal aṟavē kūṭātu eṉṉum uvakiyuṇarvu tōṉṟiṉamiyum;
piṉṉarttaṉ maṉakkiṉiya tuṇippaṟavi pōtantu, kātal ceytu kaḷittuk kalantamiyum;
atukāli, ammoḻip poruḷ paṉmuṟiyum eḻuntu, ūṉkalantu, uṭalkalantu, uyirkalantu,
piṉṉar uṇarvuṅ kalantu orōvōciyāy uruveṅkuṅ kalantuniṉṟamiyum; akkalappē, taṉ
tuṇippuḷḷiṟkum nikaḻa, iraṇṭum uḷaṅkalantu ikkāṉyāṟṟaṭikari yaṭintu, ciṟṟiṟ
camittuc cevviya vāḻkki naṭātti vantamiyum piṟavumām.
itaṉikkēṭṭa pārppiṉuḷ oṉṟu, "aṟṟēl, aṉṉāy! nī paṇṭē yirunta iṭam yāṇṭiyatu?
arukuḷatāyiṉ, yām aṉivarum āṇṭē paṭarntu, iṉpantuyttu iṉitu vāḻalāmē!” eṉṟatu.
eṉavum, tāyppuḷ uḷaṅkalaṅki, āruyirc celvamē! ataṉiyeṅkuḷateṉpēṉ aṟiyāvulaka
meṉṭēṉō aṉṟi mikamikac cēymiyiluḷḷatōr viṇṇiṭam eṉpēṉō yātu kūṟuvēṉ! yāṉ
aṟiyēṉ.
āyiṉum, aṉṟeḻun ticitta poruṇmoḻi iṉṟu meḻuntu īrkiṉṟatu. atu aṉṟiṭṭa āṇiyi
mēṟ koṇṭaṭaṅkiya yāṉ iṉṟu viṭuttal kūṭumō? ayarāviṉpattiṟṟiḷittirunta aṉṟē
avvāṇiyi yēṟṟa yāṉ, iḷamiyum, ataṉāṉām iṉpamum iḻanta inniliyiṟ koḷḷātoḻital
muṟiyō? ākavē, eṉ arumic celvaṅkaḷē! av aṟiyāvulakam yāṇṭamin tiruppiṉum
irukka. nām aṉivarum ataṉinōkkic celvam. vammiṉ yāṉ kūṟuvatu uṇmiyē eṉṉum
uṟutinōkkaṅ (Faith) koṇṭu oruppaṭumiṉ’ eṉṟatu.
eṉṟalum, “nīyum emmoṭu varutiyaṉṟē. āyiṉ, yāṉ iṉṉē varuval” eṉṟu mikacciṟiyatōr
pārppu viḷampit taṉ tāyicitta taṉiyiciyiṟ ṟāṉuṅ kalantu pāṭal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
iṉṉaṇam, collāṭalālum iciyālum poḻutu kaḻiya, “veñcuṭar veppan tīrat taṇṇaṟuñcōli
tāḻntu niḻaṟceyyavum, taṇpatampaṭṭa teṇkaḻi mēyntu palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa puḷḷellām
kuṭampi nōkki uṭaṅkupeyaravum, puṉṉi mutaliya pūviṉāṟṟam muṉṉiṉṟu kañaṟṟavum,
neṭuntiri yaḻuvattu nilākkatir parappavum’ māli vantatu. varakkaṇṭa tāyppuḷ, taṉ
pārppukkaḷit tamittuviṭṭu iritēṭuvāṉ ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟuḷoṉṟu aṇmiyilōṭiya
aruviyiṉ maruṅkuniṉṟa putaṟkaṇamarntu, aruviyiṉ kalippum, aṭiciṉi yacitali ṉeḻu
mārppum kēṭṭalum kaḷimīkkūrntu, aṅku miṅku mōṭiyōṭit taṉṉallici koṇṭu iṉṉici
miḻaṟṟat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. akkāli atu icikka muyaṉṟa icikaḷ palavum iyipupaṭātoḻiya,
iṟutiyiṟ ṟaṉṟāy pāṭiya aṟiyāvulakaviciyi iyakkippārkka, atu naṉkaṉam kivaravuvantu
ataṉiyē eṭuttum paṭuttum iṉitu pāṭik koṇṭiruntatu.
"ā! yāḻō! kuḻalō!! iṉṟēṉ perukkō!!! eṉpelām urukkum iṉṉici miḻaṟṟavallatu
yātukol! ā! tarukkaḷum caliyā; munnīrc calatiyum kaliyā, nīṇṭa poruppiṉiṉṟum
iḻiyum aruvikkālum natikaḷum puraṇṭu tuḷḷā; epporuḷiyum taṉvayamākki, uḷniṟi
yuyirum meyyum urukkukiṉṟatē!’ eṉap pukaḻvatu pōlum iḻittakka kūṟikkoṇṭē
puṟavamoṉṟu āṇṭu ataṉarukē vantatu. itu puṟaṅkuṉṟi kaṇṭaṉiyatēṉum mūkkiṟ karumiyu
muṭittu; aṉṟiyum oṇmi yuṭiyam yām eṉṉum veṇmi tali ciṟantatu. ekkuṭikeṭukvō,
etaṉalam tūṟṟavō īṇṭu iḵtu vantuḷatu. itaṉiyalpaṟiyāc ciṟucempuḷ, tāṉ kēṭṭa itaṉ
poyyuṟu pukaḻcciyi meyyuṟu pukaḻcciyeṉ ṟeṇṇi, peritum muyaṉṟu pāṭalum,
immaṭappuṟavam, taṉ ciṟutaliyip piyattuḷakki, ‘naṉṟu, naṉṟu, yāṉum pala viṭaṅkaṭkuc
ceṉṟuḷēṉ. pala iṉṉicikaḷāṟ ṟōṭkap peṟṟu muḷēṉ. orukāl, makkaḷakattup pōṟṟi
vaḷarkkap peṟum pēṟum peṟṟuḷēṉ. eṉavē, icinalaṅ kāṇṭaliṟ ciṟanta vaṉmi peṟṟuḷēṉ
eṉpatu naṉku tōṉṟum. yāṉaṟintavaḷavil, āṇiyiṭṭuk kūṟuvēṉ, niṉṉoppār ‘ nallici
vallār makkaḷiṉum ilar. āyiṉum oṉṟu, niṉṉāṟ pāṭappeṟum poruḷkaḷ yāvi? avi yāṉ
itu kāṟuṅ kēṭṭaṉavallavē!!’ eṉac ciṟitu cuḷittu viṉaviṟṟu.
kuṟumpuḷ:- ā. avi yāvum aṟiyāvulaku paṟṟiyaṉa.
puṟavam :- evi? aṟiyā...
kuṟumpuḷ:- avi aṟiyā vulakiṉ aḻaku, iṉpam, vāḻkkiyiyalpu, ataṉiyaṭiyumāṟu
mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṟuvaṉa?
puṟavam :-(nakittu) atu aṟiyāvulakāyiṟṟē! ataṉip paṟṟi nī aṟintatiyāṅṅaṉam? ammamma
muṟṟāk kāyellām muḻutta kēḷvi vallavāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. kāṭum ceṭiyum avāvaṟuttal
meyyuṇartal mutaliyaṉa ceykiṉṟaṉavē! meyyāka, yāṉ ivaṟṟi yaṟital vēṇṭum. mūttu
mutirnta mutumi mikka yāṉ, avvulakiṉiyaṟintu āvaṉa ceytukōṭal vēṇṭumē! naṉṟu!
aṟiyāvulakameṉṟu nī kūṟuvatu yātu? yāṇṭuḷatu? aruḷceytu kūṟuka.
kuṟumpuḷ:- yāṉ aṟiyēṉ. (uṇarvukalaṅki) orunāḷ nāṅkaḷ avvulakiṉukkuc cēṟalkūṭum
eṉpatiyaṉṟi vēṟu oṉṟum yāṉ aṟiyēṉ.
puṟavam :- naṉṟu. aṟintēṉ. pētiyōr pētiyōr eṉa nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. avar yāvar,
yāṇṭuḷar eṉa āyntukoṇṭē varutal eṉ iyaṟki. aṉpē iṉṟu kaṇṭēṉ. niṉmāṭṭē
annūlkaḷiṉ kūṟṟu vāymiyātal kaṇṭēṉ. nīyē ataṟkut takka cāṉṟu. ākavē, nīviṟ
aṟiyāvulakiṟkuc cellum celavu mēṟkoṇṭuḷḷīr. āṟṟuppaṭiyiṉṟiyē pōlum
appeṟṟittāya celavu nī nayantaṉirāyiṉ, num naṉṉar ceñcattu iṉṉaci vāyppa, muṉṉiya
yāviyum iṉṉē peṟuka! yāṉ varuval eṉṟu kūṟik koṇṭē paṟantu ciṟitu cella, ak
kuṟumpuḷ, virutuppaṭṭikku virintu pōṅ kaliyik kuṟukkiṭṭu uṟuvili koṭuttu
vāṅkiyāṅku, “niṟka ciṟitu tāḻttal vēṇṭum. naṉṟu, tāṅkaḷ kūṟiyatu yātu? eṉ
aṟivu mayaṅkukiṉṟatu” eṉṟatu.
puṟavam :- aṟiyāvulakattiṟku amintuḷḷa nūntam celavu mētakkatē. āyiṉum, iṭippaṭṭa
eṉakkum uṉakkumē ac celavu ceytaṟkēṟṟa neṟi tōṉṟātu pōlum.
kuṟumpuḷ:- ō, ataṉi viṭuka. avvulakupaṟṟiya ummuṭiya kōḷ yātu? ataṉi yēṉum
kūṟuka.
puṟavam :- eṉṉi? ē turumpē! eṉṉiyō ikaḻkiṉṟaṉi. naṉṟu! uṉṉotta cīriya icip
pulamiyum, nuṭpamum tiṭpamumamintu aṟivumuṭiya evvuyirēṉum aṟiyāta oṉṟiṉmāṭṭu
veṟitē niṉintu cāmpuṅkol vēṇṭiṉ, nī niṉṉiyē koḷḷalām. uṉṉippōl, vāḷātu
poḻutu kaḻippatāyiṉ eṉṉiyum koḷḷalām. oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ; yāvarukkum olluva tollum
eṉpatē ciṟantatu. mikuttuk kūṟal mikka ētamē tarum.
kuṟumpuḷ :- aṟṟēl, nīr av vaṟiyāvulakiṟkup pōkīr pōlum!
puṟavam :- orukālum illi. mutaṟkaṇ, yāṉuṟiyu miṭam, muḻaṅkukaṭal ñālam muḻuvatum
variṉum, vaḻaṅkat tavā vaḷappamum, viḻivu viṭutta viḻumiyōrkkum viḻivu
tōṟṟuvikkum viḻuppamum vāyntatu. ākaliṉ yāṉ vēṟiṭam virumpēṉ. mēlum, peṟṟatu
koṇṭu peṟum pēṟē pēṟeṉap paṭuvatu. iraṇṭāvatāka, kēṭkappaṭuvaṉa yāvum vāymiya eṉa
eḷitu ārāyātu koḷḷum nūmmōraṉṉōr pōlum ciṟṟaṟivuṭiyēṉ yāṉalaṉ. aṉṟiyum, nīr
kūṟum ulakamoṉṟu uṇṭeṉpatiyēṉum yāṉ eṅṅaṉam aṟivatu?
kuṟumpuḷ :- eṉṉi īṉṟōr eṉakku aruḷiṉar.
puṟavam :-īṉṟōrkaḷō kūṟiṉar aṅṅaṉamā aṟṟēl, nī mikamikac cīriyatōr paṟaviyē! niṉ
īṉṟōr kūṟuvaṉavaṟṟik kūṟiyavāṟē uḷaṅkoḷum nīrmi vēṇṭaṟpālatē avarkaḷ orupakal, nī
ceññāyiṟṟic ceṉṟu cērntu vāḻtaluṅ kūṭum eṉparāyiṉ, nī āmeṉṟē koḷḷuvāy
pōlum!
kuṟumpuḷ :-(cīṟiyatu pōl) īṉṟu puṟantanta eṉ kuravar orupoḻutēṉum eṉṉi
vañcittatiṉṟē!
puṟavam :-eṉṉi, yeṉṉi vekuḷuvateṉṉi? eṉṉi ceyti niṉpeṟṟōr niṉṉi vañcittaṉar;
vañcar eṉṟu oruvarum kūṟiṟṟilarē! avar, niṉṉi vañcittilarāyiṉ, oṉṟu kūṟuvēṉ,
avar pētiyarē. aṉṟēl, poruḷaṟiyātārē. nūmmōraṉṉōr māṭṭu oṉṟukūṟal viḻivōr,
uṇmiyil, uḷanēry kōṭal ulakiyal, 'ēvavum ceykalāṉ, tāṉtēṟāṉ, avvuyir pōomaḷavum
ōr nōy.' niṟka, avar kūṟṟu vañcaṉiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō, poruḷaṟiyāmiyiṉpāṟ paṭuvatō,
nīyē ārāyka. ulakiyalum uṇmiyaṟivu milarōṭu uḷḷōr uriyāṭal oruvāṟṟāṉum
ollātākaliṉ, yāṉ ceṉṟu varuval. vāḻka! vāḻka!! (maṟikiṉṟatu.). iṅṅaṉam am
maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya coṟkaḷāṟ ṟaṉ uṇmiyaṟivu kalakkaṅ koṇṭa kuṟumpuḷ oru vāṟu
teḷitaṟkuḷ, atu kaṭpulaṅ kaṭantatu.
maṟupakal, vēṉil veppamum, vevviya kāṟṟum, ilavāka, taṉ taṉmiyilōr tiripuṇmiyik
kāṭṭiṟṟu. nīṉiṟa vāṉam māṉiṟaṅ koḷḷak koṇmūviṉam viṇ muṭiṉa. kārkōḷ mukanta
kamañcūl māmaḻi vaḷḷuṟi citaṟit talippeyal peytatāka, kariporu tiraṅkum kāṉyāṟuvāṉ
yāṟoppap perukkiṭṭōṭi, kuḷirppu mikutalum, uṟintu paṭa, māmēyal maṟantaṉa; manti
kūrntaṉa; paṟavipaṭivaṉa vīḻntaṉa; kaṟavi kaṉṟukō ḷoḻintaṉa; kuṭāvaṭi uḷiyam mutaliyaṉa
peruṅkal viṭaraḷiyiṟ ceṟintaṉa. āmā nallēṟu cilittaṉa; kāṟṟuṟ ṟeṟitalāl mayiliṉam
vīṟṟuviṟ ṟōṭiṉa. iṭampeṟa vaminta kuṭampikaḷ, paṟaviyum pārppum uṟiyuḷiṉṟoḻiya,
nilampaṭa vīḻntaṉa. varaṉeṉum vippil uraṅkoṇṭa aṉpu pūṇṭa cempuḷḷiṉ cēkkimaṭṭil
ūṟoṉṟiṉṟi iṉitiruntatu. ataṉuḻi yirunta kuñcukaḷ iv vaṉittuṅ kāṇṭalum,
peruviyappuṟṟu, pakalōṉ karantaṉaṉ; paṉi mikuntuḷatu eṉkol eṉum eṇṇan
talikkoṇṭiruppa, oṉṟu, "peruvāṉam virikati riṉṟiyum, mukiṟkulam paravappeṟṟum
uḷatu! teṇṇīryāṟu maṇṇīrmiyuṟṟu aciviṉṟi yuḷatu pakalavaṉ oḷiyē yiṉṟip, pārmuṟṟum
paciya iruḷ ceṟiyappeṟṟuḷatu eṉṉō” eṉṟatu.
maṟṟum oṉṟu:- nerunal uḷaṉoruvaṉ iṉṟilli yeṉṉum perumi ivvulaku uṭittaṉṟō!
tāyppuḷ:- (nakittu) ām! āyiṉum, virikatirc celvaṉi nām nāḷi kāṇṭal kūṭum.
iḵtuṇmi! niṟka, ikkālattup pakaṟpoḻutu kuṉṟivarutali nīvir aṟintilīr pōlum!
iṉṟeḻunta puyalē ippakaṟ poḻutiṉik kuṟittatu; ātalāṟṟāṉ, ceññāyiṟṟiṉ
veṅkatirum, im mukilvaḻi nuḻintu veyileṟittal kūṭātu pōyiṟṟu. itukiṭakka. it
talippeyal nam kuṭampi muṟṟum īrañceytiṟ ṟiṉṟākaliṉ, uḷḷē vammiṉ kuḷirppu nīṅka,
yāṉ ap perum peyarc celaviṉmēṟ pāṭṭoṉṟu pāṭukiṉṟēṉ. pētiyir, nīvir ip pakaṟ
poḻutē eṉṟum kāṇṭal kūṭu meṉa niṉikkiṉṟīrkollō?
in nilavulakiṉ kāṭcinalaṉit tūkki yuritta ciṟupuḷ muṉpōntu, "aṉṉāy! yāṉ,
paṇṭu, eṉṟum kāṇṭal kūṭu meṉṟē eṇṇiṉēṉ, niṉittēṉ; maṟṟu, iṉṟē yāṉ ataṉkaṇṇum
tiripuṇmi kaṇṭēṉ. āyiṉum yāṉ ataṟku iṉi añcēṉ paruti paravi paṭiya, karumukil
peru maḻi poḻiya, vāṉaṅ karuka, kāṉyāṟu perukiṉ varum nōytīr maruntum aṟintuḷēṉ.
atu, nām aṭiya virumpum aṟiyāvulakiṉ āynta nallici. kēṭṭa tāy, uvaki kiḷarnteḻa,
nuṉikkompa rēṟi, kēṭkum ōraṟivuyir mutal āṟaṟivuyir uḷḷiṭṭa yāvum
meypaṇikkumvaṇṇam, tēmpiḻipōlum iṉṉici miḻaṟṟiṟṟu. atukāli, ataṉōṭu
cēvaṟpuḷḷum, ciṟupuḷḷum otticittu uvakittēṉ cuvittaṉa. yāvum otticittaṉa
veṉiṉum oṉṟumaṭṭil antō uḷam oruppaṭātu, uvakiyuṅ kūrātu veṟutta nōkkoṭuniṟpa,
icipperukku niṉṟatu. niṟṟalum atu, ēṉic ciṟupuṭkaḷi viḷittu, "ivvici,
itaṉiṉum pattaṭuttatām, ataṉuṭ kūṟappeṟum ulakupaṟṟiya yāvum uḷḷavāṟu uṇarappeṟiṉ"
eṉṟatu. ivvāṟē, pirivu kāraṇamākat tuyaruṟ ṟirunta peṭicciṟupuḷḷoṉṟu, nakittu,
"ataṉip paṟṟi nām mikipaṭa vuṇarvēmāyiṉ, īṇṭi nalattāṉ namakku maṉavamiti yuṇṭākā
taṉṟō!" eṉṟatu. ivaṟṟik kēṭṭa maṟṟoṉṟu, “ām, nām avvulakucārpāka oṉṟum aṟintilē
maṉṟō uṇmiyil, aṅṅaṉamōr ulaku uṇṭeṉpati yēṉum eṅṅaṉam aṟitalām?" eṉṟalum, maṟṟoru
peṭippuḷ, "aṉṟu, aṅṅaṉa moṉṟu uṇṭeṉpati nām naṉku uṇarkiṉṟōm. yātō? nam aṉṉi
kūṟiya uḷḷoli eṉṉuḷ eḻunticittatu! yāṉ uṇarntēṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiṟṟu.
ik kūṟṟiṉik kēṭṭa talikkuñcu, "uṉṉuḷ eḻunticittatāka nī niṉittaṉikollō atu
tāṉē ā iviyāvum veṟṟurikaḷē. iṅṅaṉam niṉṉik kūṟumāṟu nam aṉṉi kūṟiṉāḷpōlum
ammamma! nī kūṟuvaṉavum, aṉṉi kūṟuvaṉavum piṟavum vīṇ poruḷil yāppukkaḷēyaṉṟip
piṟitilli. nuṇṇiya kēḷviyaṟivu mikiṉum iyaṟkiyaṟivu kūṭiṉaṉṟō atu māṭci peṟum
yāṉum appullaṟivē mēṟkoṇ ṭiruppiṉ, av vuḷḷoli eṉṉuḷḷu meḻunticitta teṉpēṉ.
iṉi yāṉ av vulaku cēṟum celaviyum niṉiyēṉ. cēṟaṟku uṭaṉpaṭalum paṭēṉ. celka" eṉac
ciṟitu vekuṇṭurittatu.
“iḵtaṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam’ eṉpatu poruḷāka vaminta nallici yoṉṟi eṭutticittavaṇṇam,
kuṭampiyiṉ puṟattē tāy vara, akattirunta pārppukkaḷuṭ cila, tāy pāṭi yatiyē tāmum
pāṭat toṭaṅkiṉa. anniliyil, at tāyp puḷḷiṉ uḷḷum av voli yeḻuntatu. eḻavē,
maṟumuṟiyum, atu an nalliciyiyē toṭaṅki, “iḻumeṉōtiyiṉ aruvi yiraṅkalum,
maruṅku kari koṉṟu kāṉyāṟu cēṟalum, viṇṇakaṅ kaṭantu koṇmū vēkalum, mālvari
tuḷaṅkak kālporu tōṭalum, virikatirpparuti paraviyiṉ maṟitalum epporuḷ nāṭic
ceykiṉṟaṉa. itaṟkuk kāraṇamāvatu, avaṟṟi ṉakatteḻuntu, “iḵtu aṉṟu nī niṟkumiṭam” eṉ
ṟolikkum aruḷoli yaṉṟō yāṟṟoṭu kūṭuka mukiloṭu moḻika kāliṉi viṉavuka! nīvir
celliṭam yāteṉṟu aṟiyāvulakamē! varaṉeṉum vippēyaṉṟō! 'nī celliṭam yā teṉa
veyilavaṉi viḷittuk kēṇmiṉ. avaṉ cēṟumiṭaṉum ap peṟalarum ulakē! kuṟitta nāḷ
varuṅkāl nām aṉivarum āṇṭup paṭarkuvam" eṉa muṭittatu.
iv vicivayappaṭṭu maṉantiruntiya attalikkuñcu, “aṉṉāy! aṉṉāy! nī kūṟuvatu vāymi
kollō yāṉ koḷḷumāṟu yātu? av vulakam uṟumiṭam yāṇṭiyatu? aruḷ ceyka," eṉak
kacintaḻutu, taṉ tāymuka nōkki, taṉakku am maṭappuṟavaṅ kūṟiya aṉittum muṟiyum
poruḷum vaḻātu viḷampiṟṟu. viḷampuṅkāṟum amitikoṇṭēṟṟa ac cempuḷ, “arumanta
celvamē! alamaral vēṇṭā, yāṉ vēṟōr nallici yicippal kēṭṭi" eṉṟu toṭaṅkit tāṉ
paṇṭē yakaṉṟa ulakiyalippaṟṟik kūṟal toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
aṅṅaṉam, avviṭattiṉiṉṟum atu akaṉṟañāṉṟu, tāṉ ēṉ akaṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟu niṉittatuṇṭō
eṉiṉ iṉṟu; cellumiṭam iṟṟeṉa aṟiyāmē, paṇivum, uṟutinōkkum, nīṅkuta
ṉalattiṟkeṉum tiṇṇiya veṇṇamum āya iviyē ataṉ uḷapporuḷā yamintiruntaṉa.
peruṅkaṭalulakiṟ pirintu varunāḷ, ac celavukkuriya kāraṇap poruḷi
yuṇarttuvārēṉum, “iḵtaṉṟu, atu niṉ neṟi" eṉak kūṟuvārēṉum, atu kaṇṭatuṇṭō
veṉiṉ, aṉṟu. aññāṉṟu, am maṭappuṟavam kaṇṭiruttal kūṭumāyiṉ, atu tāṉum ivvāṟu
kūṟaṟpālatāmō? ivi yaṉittum ārāyātu pōnta ac cempuḷ tāṉum piṉṉar uriya nalattit
tuykkā toḻintatō? ataṉi yaṅṅaṉam cēṟal ceyyumāṟu ūkkiya poruḷāya uḷḷoli yātām?
atuvē tiruvaruḷ eṉpatu. appaṟavi, ak kāṉyāṟ ṟaṭikari yaṭinta nāḷē uṇmiyi
oruvāṟuṇarntatu. ēṉeṉiṉ, āṇṭut tāṉ atu ciṉṉāḷvarit taṅkutal viḻintatu. piṉṉarē,
ataṉ kātaṟ ṟuṇippaṟavi pōtantatum, vāḻkkiyiṉpam nukarntatum piṟavum nikaḻntaṉa.
aṉparkaḷē! atu pōtu, kātaṟ cēvaloṭu kalantu atu kuṭampi yamikkumpōtu, taṉvayiṟ
piṉṉart tōṉṟuvaṉavāya pārppukkaṭkeṉa ak kuṭampiyic camikkum pōtu, am maṭappuṟavaṅ
kaṇṭirukkumēl, ettuṇi ikaḻcci ceytirukkuṅkollō! piṟa vāp pārppukkaṭkuṅ
kūṭamittal pētimi eṉ ṟeḷḷiyirukkuṅ kollō! aṉṟi, “etirviṉi yaṟital yāṅṅaṉam? nī
iṅṅaṉ ceytal vīṇ poruḷilyāppē, iḵtu aṟivuṭiyār toḻilaṉṟu" eṉṟellām
viritturit tirukkumō! yātō? niṟka, ic ceykikkellā māvatōr kāraṇattiyēṉum
ārāyātu atu ceytatu vañcaṉiyō? aṉṟu. iviyaṉittum at tiruvaruḷiṉ ceyalē yām
kūṭamittuc cēvalōṭu kūṭi vāḻkkic cākā ṭukittaṟku, itaṟkup pāṅkā yamintatu
avvuḷḷoli yāya tiruvaruḷaṉṟō ataṉvaḻi niṉṟataṉālaṉṟō, arumanta pārppukkaḷ peṟṟu,
ayarāviṉpattil atu tiḷittiruntatu! itu kāli atu, taṉ celvaṅkaṭkuc celvakkālattum
allaṟ kālattum kōṭaṟpālaṉavāy uṟutip poruḷkaḷi yuṇarttaṟkēṟṟa aṟivamiti peṟṟatō?
iṉṟō? "ivi yaṉittum poruḷil puṇarcci, kaṭṭukkati, pulavar puraṭci". eṉṟellām
pitaṟṟiya am maṭappuṟaviyē viṉavumiṉ, avvuḷḷoli yataṉi vañcittatō veṉṟu. aṉṟiyum,
ac cempuḷ, “celvaṅkāḷ eṅku, ēṉ cēṟal vēṇṭumeṉṉum ārāycciyiṉṟi, iṉṉē
oruppaṭumiṉ. ikkāli yām aṟintilōmāyiṉum, maṟṟorukāli yaṟital kūṭum" eṉṟum,
"paṇivum (Obedience) aṟivum (Faith) paṇpaṭṭa kālattaṉṟē, meyyuṇarvum,
poruṇalamum, viḷaṅkat tōṉṟalum kāṭṭappeṟutalum uḷavām," eṉṟum kūṟiyataṉi appuṟavam
uṇaral muṭiyātu pōlum!
piṉṉar nāṭkaḷ cila ceṉṟaṉa. kārum kūtirum nīṅkiṉa. maḻiyum paṉippum mikuntamiyiṉ,
varaṉeṉṉum vippup paṟṟiya nallici, avaṟṟiṭi, ēnticiyum tūṅkiciyumākip piṉṉarc
ceppalōciyāy, icikkuntoṟum kēṭṭavi meycilirppavum, kaṇṇir vāravum, ūṉkalanta
uyirkalantu, uḷaṅkalantu uṭalamellām uvaṭṭāniṟkum tēṉkalantu,
---
paṇivu - vāveṉa varutalum pōveṉap pōtalum eṉṉum kēḷvippayaṉ. aṟivu - nallataṉkaṇ
nalaṉum, tīyataṉkaṇ tīmiyum kāṇṭal kūṭum eṉṉum uṟutinōkkiṉip payappatu.
------------
tittikkum taṉmiyiṟ ciṟantatu. iḵtuṇṭātaṟkuk kāraṇam, eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avi uṇmi
yaṟintaṉa; avvuṇmiyiyuṇarntaṉa; avvuṇmiyi uṟuti yeṉavum koṇṭaṉa. ākalāṟṟāṉ, avi
āṇṭi yiṉpattiyum īṇṭēyuṇaralāyiṉa.
niṟka, orunāḷ, ciṟukuñcukaḷ kuṭampiyiṉiṉṟum veḷic ceṉṟiruppa, avaṟṟiṉ uḷaṅ
kalaṅkutaṟ kētuvāya pēroliyoṉṟu arukē yeḻuntatu. eḻa, pēraccaṅkoṇṭu, avi tam
kūṭaṭintaṉa. kūṭu akkāli, nilikulintu, taḷarntu, nīrāl naṉippuṇ ṭiruntatu.
iruppiṉum, avi, oṉṟi yoṉṟiṟukappullic ciṟitu pōtu kiṭappa, āyppuḷ illāmi
terintatu. etir nōkkiya kaṇkaḷ pūttuppōyiṉa; maṉam peruntuyar pūṇṭatu. eṉ ceyyum!
piṉṉar avi, kūṭṭiṉiṉṟum veḷippōntu, nōkku miṭantoṟum tam tāyppaṟaviyit
tēṭicceṉṟu, taḷarvum, kaḷippum mītura, īṉṟārik kāṇātu ēkkuṟṟa neñcam tuyarntu
cāmpa, kāṇṭal kūṭumō kūṭātō eṉṉum eṇṇam orupuṭi yalippa, perumpētuṟṟakāli
cēṟum nīrum kalantu ciṟiya nīrniliyum, cuṟṟu mōṅkuta lillāp putalum uḷḷatōr
tuṉpaniliyam avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉart tōṉṟiṟṟu. kūrntu nōkkiyakālattu, paṟavic ciṟakum
ciṟumayirum cāmpiya vōciyum āṇṭut tōṉṟiṉa. kaṇṭa kuñcukaḷ. vēṇavā mīkkoḷa, aṅkuc
ceṉṟu, iṟanta toṉṟum, iṟakkap pōvatoṉṟumāya iraṇṭu puṭkaḷik kaṇṭu, iṟakkappōvatu tam
yāyātal uṇarntu, paṭṭati eṉ kūṟuvatu paṭuvatik kaṇṭa tāyppaṟavi, celvaṅkāḷ "eḻumiṉ
ceṉmiṉ| tiruvaruḷiṉ kāriyamāya uḷḷoli untam uḷattum iṉṉē eḻunticikkiṉṟatu.
ceṉmiṉ ceṉmiṉ paṇimiṉ paṭarumiṉ tāḻttaṟkuc ciṟitu pōtum iṉṟu! iṉpanāṭṭamē
koṇmiṉ!!" eṉṟatu. eṉakkēṭṭa kuṟum puṭkuḻām, "eṉṉi? enti yāṇṭuḷār! aiyakō!
aṉṉāy! aṉṉāy!” eṉṟu alaṟik kūyiṉa. "amimiṉ! amimiṉ! yāṅkaḷ āṇṭu ummōṭu cūḻa
viruttal muṭiyātu; kūṭātu. iṉi..." eṉṟu kūṟikkoṇṭē taṉ taliyik kīḻē cāyttu,
varaṉeṉṉum vippil muḷittatu.
niṟka, itaṉi yīṇṭu urittukkoṇṭē celliṉ perukum.
tiruvaruḷ kikūṭṭumāṟṟiṉi vāḻkkimukattāṉ uṇartta vuṇarnta paṟavikkuñcukaḷ,
iṉpavulaki yeṇṇit tāmum pēraṟam nērntu, ap pērulakiṉi yaṭital vēṇṭi muṭittaṉavāka,
palavum palavēṟu neṟiyiṟ paṭarntu ceṉṟaṉa. avi yāṇṭuc celkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟēṉum, ēṉ
cellukiṉṟaṉa veṉṟēṉum oruvarēṉum kaṭṭurittal kūṭātu. kaṉaviṟ kaṇṭaṉa voppa, tam
tāyniṉivum, tam paṇṭik kūṭṭi ṉuṟivum piṉṉar avaṟṟiṉ pulattut tōṉṟalum
maṟitalumāyiṉa. peṟṟatukoṇṭu pēriṉpan tuykkuṅ kālattut tam tāyniṉivu tōṉṟic
ciṟitu mayakkalum, avi piṉṉart teḷitalum āya iṉṉōraṉṉa pala nikaḻuṅkālattu, oṉṟu
kūṟiyatu, ‘itaṉiṉum ciṟantatōr iṉpavulakātal vēṇṭum nam tāy paṭarnta vulakam.
aṉṉatēyāka, aṅṅaṉam nam tāy kūṟiyatum uṇmiyē pōlum” eṉpatu.
----------

4. piṟappokkum : ciṟappovvā

vaṭuvilā viyattu maṉṉiya mūṉṟaṉuḷ naṭuvaṇateyta iru taliyumeytum eṉṉum
cemporuḷuṇarntu, annaṭuvaṇatāya poruḷīṭṭal karutit tirikaṭalvaḻiyum, mivarivaḻiyum pala
nāṭukaḷukkuc cellum makkaḷ uḷarātalpōla, ciṟṟuyirkaḷuḷ tēṉ tēṭippalaticikaḷukkuñ
celvaṉa palavuḷa. avaṟṟiṟkut tēṉ eṉavum, aḷiyeṉavum, vaṇṭeṉavum, karumpeṉavum
palapeyarkaḷ uṇṭu. niṟka, īṇṭut tēṉ tēṭicceṉṟ vaṇṭoṉṟiṉ varalāṟṟiṉiyē yāṉ
kūṟattoṭaṅkukiṉṟēṉ.
pututtaḷir tōṉṟa, pūkkaḷ malara, naṟumaṇam eṅkum naṉku parava, maliyānilam
maruṅkuvantaciya, yāṇṭum iṉpamē ilakittēṉāṟum iḷavēṉiṟkālattu mutupakaloṉṟiṉ
piṟpakalil, tēṉiyoṉṟu naṟuntēṉ vēṇṭik kāvum, cōliyum, kaviṉpūnturuttiyum
pukku, āṇṭāṇṭu malarntu vayaṅkum pūttoṟum ceṉṟu, koḷaṟkariya tēṉ mikippaṭakkoṇṭu,
ataṉāleḻunta uvakiyāl virintu eḻuntu, taṉkūṭu nōkkic cellat toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
āyiṉum, atupōtu, atu pūviyal naṟavam māntip punti mayakkuṟṟiruntamiyāl,
vaḻiyaṟiyātu mēleḻuntu ceṉṟu, arukirunta or pērakattiṉ mēṟcāḷarattiṉuṭē ataṉ
uṭpuṟam aṭintatu.
akkāli appērakattiṉkaṇ ētō oruciṟappu naṭipeṟṟatu: iṉiyavum maṇamikkavumāya paṇṭaṅkaḷ
pala malintiruntaṉa. mikappalamakkaḷum kūṭiyiruntatōṭamiyātu collāṭalālum,
icikkaruvikaḷālum piṟavaṟṟāṉum pērōciyum ceyvārāyiṉar. ivaṟṟikkaṇṭa tēṉīyiṉ
karuttil accantōṉṟi oru puṭivaruttiṉum, paṇṭaṅkaḷiṉ cuviyum maṇamum ataṉi veḷiyē
kaviṭātu orupuṭit takintaṉa. takiyavē, tēṉiyum naṟumaṇappaṇṭaṅkaḷ vittuḷḷa taṭṭoṉṟiṉ
puṟattētaṅki, avaṟṟiṉ maṇatti nukarntum cuviyit tērntum ciṟitupōtu kaḻittu
niṟka, "ā! itō pār. tēṉī! tēṉī!" eṉakkūṟikkoṇṭē ciṟuvar iruvar ataṉarukēvantaṉar.
iccoṟkaḷik kēṭṭa avviyum, uṭaṉē accattāl uṭal naṭuṅka veḷiccelvāṉ muyaṉṟu
orucāḷarattiṉ mēṟpāyntatu. pāvam! ataṉ katavu kaṇṇāṭiyālāyatākaliṉ, akkaḷivaṇṭu
ataṉiyuṇaraviyalātu mayaṅki, āṭiyil mōtuṇṭu kalaṅkik kīḻēvīḻntu, piṉṉarc ciṟitu
tēṟi, cāḷaraccaṭṭattiṉ kīḻppuṟattē tāṉ mayakkattālum accattālum iḻanta maṉavamitiyum
vaṉmiyiyum mīṭṭum peṟaṟporuṭṭu viḻuntu kiṭantatu.
niṟka, itaṉikkaṇṭa ciṟuvarkaḷiruvarum arukēpōntu, itippaṟṟiya oru collāṭal
nikaḻttuvārāyiṉar.
ciṟuvaṉ :-taṅkāy! itutāṉ tēṉi. itaṉit toḻilpuri tēṉi eṉpatum vaḻakku. itaṉ
irutuṭikaḷukku miṭiyiluḷḷa meḻukkup piyiṉiyum kāṇ. ā! eṉṉa ūkkamum uḻippu
muṭiyatu itu teriyumā!
ciṟumi :- aṇṇāl meḻukkiyum tēṉiyum ākkuvatu -
ittēṉi tāṉō?
ciṟuvaṉ :-ām. pūkkaḷiṉ uḷḷirukkum iṉiyatēṉi itu avaṟṟiṉ uṭceṉṟu koṇarkiṟatu.
muṉṉoru nāḷ nām nampūmputariṉ akattum puṟattum "kam kam" eṉa eṇṇiṟanta īkkaḷ
moyttu icittatu kaṇṭō maṉṟō avi aṅkumiṅkum paṟantu tirintatik kaṇṭu
nakittōmallavā? iḷantaḷirkaḷiṉ mītu ivi paṭaruṅ kālattu ivaṟṟiṉ meyyaḻaku nam
uḷḷamellām koḷḷi koḷḷavilliyā? niṟka, itu iṉṟu kāliyil tēṉiṭṭuṅ kālattum yāṉ
pārttatuṇṭu. itaṟkut tēṉiṭṭaloṉṟu tāṉ toḻileṉal ākātu.
itaṉoṭu vēṟupala kāriyaṅkaḷum itu ceytal vēṇṭum. tēṉ kūṭu kaṭṭutal, ataṉi yaḻaku
ceyal mutaliya ellāk kāriyaṅkaḷiyum itutāṉ ceytal vēṇṭum. itu paṟṟiyētāṉ itu
toḻilpuri tēṉi eṉṟaḻikkap peṟutalum kāṇka. pāpam! pāpam!!
ciṟumi :- toḻilpuritēṉi eṉpatu yātu? aṇṇā tāṅkaḷ "pāpam! pāpam!" eṉṟu
itaṉmāṭṭu iraṅkuvatēṉ!
ciṟuvaṉ :- ēṉ teriyātu? nam attāṉ nēṟṟu namakkuc coṉṉati maṟantuviṭṭāy pōlum:
tammic ciṟitum ompātu piṟarnalamē yōmpi yavarkkē āṭpaṭṭuc ciṟu toḻil puriyum
makkaḷaṉivarum iraṅkaṟpālarē! avar vakiyiṉ pāṟpaṭuvatē ittēṉiyumākaliṉ, yāṉ itaṉ
māṭṭum maṉamiraṅkal vēṇṭiṟṟu. ivvīkkaḷukku araca ī oṉṟuḷatu. ataṉi īyaraci yeṉpatuvē
peru vaḻakku. atu oru ciṟutoḻilum, ceyyātu, eññāṉṟum toḻilīkkaḷ amitta
kūṭṭiṉkaṇṇē iruntu, avi koṇarum tēṉiyuṇṭalum, koṇarnta īkkaṭku vēṟupaṇi
paṇittalum, tāṉpayanta ciṟṟikkaḷip purattalum, ivaṟṟōraṉṉa piṟavumē ceytu koṇṭuvarum.
ataṟku vēṇṭum ciṟupaṇikaḷi, maruṅkiruntu ārkkum vaṇṭukaḷ ceyyāniṟkum. ivaṟṟiṟku
vēṟāka veṟṟikkaḷ palavuḷa. avi oru toḻilum ceyyātu vīṇē aṅkumiṅkun
tirintukoṇṭiruppavi. ēṉiyavi yāvum ivviyippōṉṟu tēṉiṭṭip
piṟavaṟṟiṟkaḷittalōṭu, avaṟṟiṟkām ciṟutoḻilip puripaviyē yākum. ikkūṟiyavaṟṟi nam
attāṉ aṟivārēl avarāl nakittu aṭaṅkutal muṭiyātu.
ciṟumi:- āṉāl, icceytikaḷ attāṉukkut teriyāvō?
ciṟuvaṉ :- illi. avarukkut teriyum eṉṟu yāṉ - niṉikkavilli. teriyumāyiṉ,
avar ivaṟṟip piṭittalum piṭittuttuṉpuṟuttalum ceyyāraṉṟē. yāṉ evvāṟu
aṟintēṉeṉiṉ, eṉakku nam cōlikkāvalaṉ kūṟiṉāṉ. niṟka, nerunal avar kūṟiyavaṟṟi
nōkkiṉiyā? ivvulakil, aracar, araciyār eṉa uyirkaḷuṭ cilariruttal iyaṟkikkē
māṟāṉatām; ellārum orē accāka iruppatē itaṟkup pōtiya cāṉṟām; iyaṟkiyiṟ ṟōṉṟum
ovvoru poruṭkum piṟappiṉāl oruvaki vēṟupāṭum iṉṟām; eṉavē, aracar eṉavum
araciyareṉavum iruppavar naṭunili varampik kaṭantavarē yāvarām.
ciṟumi :-āṉāl, ivaṟṟi uyttuṇarntu kōṭal ivvikkaḷukku iyalāteṉa yāṉ
niṉikkiṉṟēṉ.
ciṟuvaṉ :- niṉippateṉṉa uṇmiyilē atu avaṟṟiṟkuk kiṭiyātutāṉ. cōlikkāvalaṉ
kūṟiyavaṟṟi maṭṭil ittoḻilīkkaḷ kēṭṭirukkumāyiṉ, ettakiya ciṉaṅ koṇṭirukkum! nīyē
niṉittuppār!!
ciṟumi :- avaṉ kūṟiyateṉṉa?
ciṟuvaṉ :- araciyiyum āṭpaṭṭoḻukum ciṟṟikkaḷiyum cīr tūkkuṅkāl piṟappiṉāl
avaṟṟiṟkiṭiyil oruvaki vēṟupāṭum illi. piṟappum tōṟṟamum oṉṟē. eṉiṉum
ivvēṟupāṭṭit taruvaṉa uṇṭivakiyum, uṟiyuḷiṉ taṉmiyumēyām. icciṟṟikkaḷi vaḷarkkum
peṭi vaṇṭukaḷ, cilavaṟṟiṟku oruvaki uṇavum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṟku oruvaki uṇavum;
cilavaṟṟiṟku oru vakikkūṭum, vēṟu cilavaṟṟiṟku vēṟuvakiyāṉa kūṭum tarukiṉṟaṉa.
ataṉāl cila īyaracikaḷākavum, cila toḻilīkkaḷākavum māṟukiṉṟaṉa. ikkaruttē nerunal nam
attāṉ mēṉmakkaḷiyum toḻil makkaḷiyumpaṟṟik kūṟiyavaṟṟuḷ amintu kiṭantatu.
‘piṟappokkum ellāvuyirkkum.” eṉpatu tamiḻmaṟi. niṟka, uṇṇuṅkālam āyiṟṟu. vā,
pōkalām.
ciṟumi :- aṇṇāl ciṟitu nilluṅkaḷ. yāṉ ivviyi veḷiyē viṭuttuvarukiṟēṉ.
iṅṅaṉam kūṟikkoṇṭē, acciṟumi taṉ kittuṇiyāl ataṉip piyaveṭuttu aruḷoḻuka
nōkki, “antō ē! koṅkutēr vāḻkki añciṟittumpi! kuḻavipparuvattē uṉṉi vaḷarttōr
uṉakku uṇṭiyum uṟiyuḷum uyarntaṉa koṭuttirupparēl, ā! iṉṟunīyum oru īyaraciyāka
iruppāyaṉṟō! eṉṉa iḻinta niliyi uṉakku avar tantuḷḷār: aṟitiyō? kiṭakka, nī iṉi
veḷic ceṉṟu, uṉvāṉāḷ muṟṟilum meḻuku ceytalilum, tēṉiṭṭalilum celaviṭal
vēṇṭum. celka. celka. nī mēṟkoṇṭa ciṟutoḻil cīrttoḻilāmāka!" eṉa vāḻtti,
cāḷarattiṉ puṟattē viṭuttaṉaḷ. veḷippōnta vaṇṭu, kaḻipēruvakikoṇṭu taṉ vaḻiccelki
toṭaṅkiṟṟu.
ā! eṉṉa iṉiymālikkālam! kāṭcikkiṉiyamālik kālam! kātalarkūṭik kalanturiyāṭum
mālikkālam! iṅkaṉam iḷavēṉiliṉ mutumāli, maṉṉuyirkkup pēriṉpam payantu iṉitāyt
tōṉṟa, attoḻilikkumaṭṭil attaṉmiyiṉ māṟupaṭṭut tōṉṟiṟṟu. ataṉ maṉam ētō or
poruḷāṟ paṟṟappeṟṟut tuyarpūṇṭiruntatu; aṭikkaṭi atu oruvakiyuṇarcciyāl nāṇi
mika varuntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. ivvāṟu varuttamum tuyaramum mayaṅkiya uḷḷamoṭu atu taṉ
kūṭṭi - paṇiceytu kiṭappatē taṉṉaṟamākum eṉṉum koḷkiyuṭaṉ aṉṟu kāliyil
viṭṭuppirinta taṉ kūṭṭi aṭintatu. aṭitalum, aṭakkalākā veṟuppum vekuḷiyum ataṉ
uḷḷattut tōṉṟa, atu tāṉ koṇarnta tēṉpiyi nekiḻtteṟintu, maṉam calippaṭiya,
“ellāvuyiriṉum kaṭippaṭṭēṉ yāṉē kāṇ" eṉa aṟintatu.
akkāli, aṅkuc ciṟutoḻil ceytukoṇṭirunta - maṟṟoru toḻil itaṉik kēṭṭu, "eṉṉi
ceyti? eṉṉē niṉ ceyki yiruntavāṟu veṟimikka vēṅkiyiṉ tēṉ uṇṭaṉikollō aṉṟi
akkoṭiya paccilippuḻu nam kūṭukaḷil taṉ muṭṭiyiṭṭuḷatu paṟṟic ciṉaṅ koṇṭaṉiyō?
eṉṉi kāraṇam?’ eṉa viṉaviṟṟu. ataṟku itu, “nī kūṟiya yātēṉum vēṇṭuṅ kāraṇamākātu.
aiyō pāvam! aviyeṉ ceyyum? tāntāmuṉ ceytaviṉiyit tāmē tuyppa tallatu piṟar
ceyvateṉ?" eṉaviṭiyiṟuppa, atu, "piṉṉar eṉṉiyō kāraṇam. uritti" eṉa mīṭṭum viṉava,
itu kūṟum:- "illi. aṉpa! iṉṟu kāliyiṟ ceṉṟa yāṉ mikka cēṇiṟ ceṉṟēṉ.
ceṉṟaviṭattu yāṉ, itukāṟum kēṭṭaṟiyātaṉavellām kēṭṭēṉ. kēṭṭavaṟṟiṉ karupporuḷ
“nāmellōrum iḻitoḻilpuriyum iḻittakka uyirkaḷāvōm eṉpatē.”
ivvuri ataṉ cevippaṭalum, atu tiṭukkiṭṭu, "nī itukāṟum aṟiyāta itaṉi uṉakku
uṇarttiyavar yāvar?” eṉak kēṭpa, itu cālavekuṇṭu, uṇmiyi yuṇarttupavar
yāvarāyiṉumeṉ! yāṉ iṉṟu aṟintatu muṟṟilum uṇmiyē. itumaṭṭil uṟutiyē."
eṉṟatu.
eṉavē, atu, “oru kālum nī kūṟiyatu uṟutiyuṭittaṉṟu. pēticciṟṟuyiroṉṟu iṅṅaṉam
pitaṟṟiyatu koṇṭu, piḻipaṭṭoḻukutal peruntakimiyēyaṉṟu. nī iḻittakkōy eṉa
uṇarvuṇarttiṉārā lurikkappaṭṭa acciṟu poḻutu kāṟum nī appeṟṟiyi yalli yeṉpati
nīyē naṉkaṟivāy. ākavē, avar urittāreṉa nī kūṟum uri veṟṟuriyeṉpataṉiṉum
veḷiṟṟuriyeṉavē yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ. iṉi itip paṟṟik kūṟutalum aṟamākātu." eṉak
kūṟikkoṇṭē tāṉ muṉṉark kuṟipaṭaviṭṭa kāriyattic ceyyat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. -
eṉiṉum, ivvurimuḻuvatum ceviṭaṉ kātil ūtappeṟṟa tivavoliyē pōlap payaṉiṉṟi
yoḻintatu. āṉāl, itumaṭṭil, taṉṉotta īkkaḷ palavaṟṟi oruṅkaḻittut tāṉ tēṉṟēṭic
ceṉṟaviṭat taṟintavi yaṉittiyum teḷivākak kūṟiṟṟu. kēṭṭavaṟṟuṭ cila peruviyappum,
cila peruṅkalakkamuṅ koṇṭaṉa; muṭivil avvīkkuḻātti ṉiṭiyil oru peruṅkuḻappam
uṇṭāyiṟṟu.
piṉṉarc cila īkkaḷ eḻuntu, uṇmiyil tām piṟappiṉāl oppāṉavi yeṉiṉum, tammi araca
īkkaḷ naṭāttu muṟi iḻivāṉatē yeṉavum, atu tammiyaṭimippaṭuttuva toṉṟeṉavum, ataṉi
viriviṟ pōkkuvatu mutaṟkaṭaṉ eṉavum muṭivu ceytaṉa. iṭiyiṟ cila īkkaḷ muṉvantu tām
toṭaṅkiya iyakkattukkut talimi tāṅkit tammi uriya neṟiyil naṭattuvataṟkēṟṟavar
acciṟuvar tam attāṉē yāvar eṉavum kūṟiṉa. atupōtu, vēṟu cila eḻuntu, muṉṉark
kuṟikkappeṟṟavarē talivarāyiṉ, avar tammi oruṅkē yaracīkka ḷākkutalō ṭamiyātu tam
kūṭukaḷiyum cevvaṉam purantu, tamakku oru mētakka ciṟappurimiyum tantaruḷvāreṉavum
vaḻimoḻiya, okkum okkum eṉṉum pēroli kammeṉa akkuḻāttiṭi yeḻuntatu."
ā! ituvum takumā? yāvarum civikiyūrat toṭaṅkiṉ, ataṉik kāvuvār yār inniliyil,
ciṟitu nāḷ mutirnta toḻiliyoṉṟu, akkūṭṭattiṭit taṉ taliyi nīṭṭi, “piṟar
nalattukkeṉat toḻil puriyum īkkaḷaṉittum araca īkkaḷāka māṟiṉ, naṉṟu! naṉṟu!!
avaṟṟiṟku āmpaṇiyip puripavi yāvō?" eṉṟatu. kallā aṟiviṟ kayavarpāṟ kaṟṟuṇarnta
nallār tamatu kaṉam naṇṇuvarō? naṇṇāraṉṟē! ākaliṉ, avvikka ḷaṉittum, itaṉik kēṭṭatum
cālavekuṇṭu, matiyili eṉavum, taṉṉurimi yaṟiyāt taṟutaliyeṉavum kūṟi, “nām
aṉivarum aracākka ḷākuṅkāl, iṟṟip pōtu aracu pūṇṭiruppaṉavum, avaṟṟiṉ
vaḻittōṉṟalkaḷum tām uḷḷaḷavum namakkup paṇiceytu kiṭakkalāmē!” eṉṟaṉa. eṉṟalum, av ī
maṟumuṟiyum eḻuntu, "avaṟṟiṟkup piṉṉar attoḻili mēṟkoḷḷuvōr yār" eṉak kēṭpa,
kam, kam eṉṉum pēroli āṅku mikkeḻuntatu. uṭaṉē, atuvum taṉ nāviyaṭakkik koṇṭatu.
maṟṟoru ī eḻuntu, "aṉpuṭiyīr! eṉakku muṟpēciya īkku nikaḻnta vaci aḻakaṟṟatākum.
atu namakkuṭ piḷaviyuṇṭupaṇṇiṉum paṇṇum. paṇṇuṅkāl, nām kikkoṇṭa iyakkam
veṟṟipeṟātu. nam koḷkikku māṟākavuḷḷa, paḻiya niliyilē tam vāṇāḷic
celavaḻittārkkup putiya koḷkikaḷum, uṇarccikaḷum, iyakkaṅkaḷum, piṟavum veṟuppit
taruvaṉavākaliṉ, avveṟuppi yuṭiyavariyum nam iyakkattiṟkuk koṇartal iṉṟiyamiyātu.
"niṟka, tēṉiṭṭalum meḻuku ceytalumāya toḻil kaḷic ceyyum nām aṉivarum
īyaracikaḷāka māṟiviṭuvōmāyiṉ, namakkāka attoḻilkaḷip puripaviyilavāka, nam nili
mikka tuṉpa niliyāy muṭiyum. itu nīṅkaḷ naṉkaṟinta toṉṟu. itaṉi nīṅkaḷē eṇṇip
pāruṅkaḷ.
"eṉṟālum, eṉakku oru eṇṇam tōṉṟukiṟatu; ataṉi acaṭṭi ceyyātu kēṭṭu,
amivuṭittāyiṉ ēṟṟuṅ koḷḷuṅkaḷ. atu yāteṉiṉ, nām aṉivarum īyaracikaḷātaliṉum,
avvaracurimiyiyē muṟṟilum kaḷintu viṭṭu, aracī mutal veṟṟivariyuḷḷa aṉittīkkaḷum
toḻilīkkaḷākamāṟi, namakkuḷ oruvaki yuyarvu tāḻvumiṉṟi orumittu vāḻtal vēṇṭum"
eṉṟōr coṟpoḻivu ceytatu.
iṭiyil, muṉṉar vacipeṟṟuc ceṉṟa toḻili maṟumuṟiyum eḻuntu, "nām aṉivarum
iṉṟuvarit toḻilīkkaḷākavē uḷḷōm. aṟṟākaviṉ, namakku ivviyakkattāl oru vakippayaṉum
eytutal muṭiyāteṉa niṉikkiṟēṉ" eṉṟatu.
eṉṟalum eḻuntaṉa. īkkaḷ. pukippaṭalam pōlat tiraṇṭeḻuntu kam kam eṉap pēroli ceytu,
tam ciṉatti evvemmuṟiyil ataṟkuk kāṭṭutal kūṭumō avvam muṟiyil kāṭṭiṉa. kāṭṭak
kaṇṭañciya atu muṉpōlavē taṉ iruppiṭam aṭintatu. aṭitalum, pakalavaṉum maṟintaṉaṉ,
paṟavippāṭṭu aṭaṅkiṉa; eṅkum iḷiyiruḷ parantatu; nīlavāṉattu nittilam patittāṟpōlak
kōla mīṉiṉam tam ciṟṟoḷi parappiṉa; piṟpakal muṟṟum kuḻappattiṭiyuḻanta īkkaḷ tattam
kūṭaṭintu uṟaṅkuvavāyiṉa. piṉṉar avviravu muṟṟum vēṟu kuḻappamiṉṟi yoḻiya yāṇṭum
amitiyē taliciṟantu niṉṟatu.
veḷḷi muḷippa, viṭiyal vantatu; tāmariyiṟ ṟuñciya kāmar vaṇṭiṉam, kaṭkamaḻum
neytalukkuc ceṉṟu, tātutip piṉṉar, kaṇpōl malarnta kāmarcuṉimalari yaṭintu oli
ceyyā niṟpa, muṉṉāḷ nikaḻnta kuḻappattil īṭupaṭṭa īkkaḷ maṭṭil oruṅku kūṭi mīṭṭum
tam nilimiyiyum, ataṟkēṟṟa muṟikaḷiyum paṉṉippaṉṉip pēcattoṭaṅkiṉa. vēṟu cila,
kūṭukaḷil, araciyar ēviya paṇikaḷi iṉitu ceytu vantavi, ikkuḻappattip paṟṟiyēṉum,
ataṉiṭiyē nikaḻum virivurikaḷiyēṉum oru poruḷāka niṉiyātu tam toḻililē tam
karuttic celutti niṉṟaṉa. niṟka, kuḻappattiṟ kalanta īkkuḻāttiṉiṭi nikaḻccikaḷ pala
nikaḻa, mutirā īkkaḷiṟ cila muṉivu mītuārak koṇṭu, ataṉāl tammiyum, tam
maṉattiṇmiyiyum iḻantu, peruṅkalakkatti viḷivik kum nilimiyeytiṉa. atupōtu,
ikkuḻappattiṟku mutaṟkāraṇamākavirunta ī avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉar, "aṉparkaḷē! nīṅkaḷ kikkoṇṭa
iyakkam naṉmi payakkak kūṭiyatē. eṉiṉum, iṭiyilirukkum oru poruḷi nīṅkaḷ eṇṇātu
pōyviṭṭīrkaḷē! apporuḷ tāṉ etuveṉiṉ, nīṅkaḷ aṉivarum mutirntu viṭṭamiyaṉṟō? nāṉ
mutirnta uṅkaḷi evvāṟu iṉi araca īkkaḷāka māṟṟuvatu, kūṭātaṉṟō. āyiṉum, nāmkoṇṭa
iyakkattiṉ veṟṟik kaṭiyāḷamāka yātāṉumoru putiya muṭipi eytutal vēṇṭum. eṉ
aṟiviṟkeṭṭiya muṭipoṉṟik kūṟukiṟēṉ kēḷuṅkaḷ. piṟaku uṅkaḷ viruppampōṟ
ceyyuṅkaḷ" eṉṟatu. uṭaṉē, avvīkkaḷaṉittum orupaṭiyāka nī kūṟukiṉṟavaṟṟi yāṅkaḷ mikka
viruppuṭaṉ ēṟṟuk koḷḷukiṟōm. colliyaruḷka" eṉap pēroliyiṭṭuk kūṟiṉa. kūṟalum,
atu puṉṉaki tavaḻum iṉmukaṅkoṇṭu, "aṉparkaḷē! nāḷiṉ mutirnta namakku iṉi araciyar
vēṇṭuvatilli. nām aṉivarum iṉṟiruppatu pōlavē toḻilīkkaḷākavē yiruppōm; nām iṉi
oruvarukku aṭimippaṭṭu avarkaḷ kālāliṭum ēvalit taliyāṟ ceytal vēṇṭā. nāmārkkum
kuṭiyallōm! nalamē vāḻvōm. nammik kēṭpavarkkum, tūya vīrattōṭum “yām yārkkum
kuṭiyallōm. yātum añcōm’ eṉavē, nām iṉikkūṟuvōm. ituvē eṉ ciṟṟaṟiviṟa keṭṭiya
muṭipākum. yāṉ kūṟiyavaṟṟil kuṟṟaṅkaḷiruntāṟ poṟuttuk koḷḷumpaṭik
kēṭṭukkoṇṭu, immāṭṭōṭu eṉ ciṟiya virivuriyi niṟuttikkoḷḷukiṟēṉ, eṉṟatu.
eṉavē, vaṇṭiṉam muṟṟum kammeṉum ici ceytu "itu takum. takum takum!!" eṉa uṭaṉ
paṭṭaṉa. piṉṉar, avikaḷ oruṅkē tiraṇṭu tām iṉittamittu vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa iṭam niṟuvutaṟ
poruṭṭu maṟumuṟiyum oru kūṭṭam kūṭiṉa. itupōtu ivaṟṟiṟkut talimipūṇṭu iyal
neṟiyiṟ celutta vallatāya oṉṟum varavilli. ākavē, akkūṭṭattil oruvakikkaṭṭuppāṭum
oḻuṅkum ilavāyiṉa. eṉ ceyvatu ituvum ulakaviyaṟkiyē!!
niṟka, iṭam niṟuvutaṟ poruṭṭu nikaḻnta ikkūṭṭattiṭi, "oṉṟu eḻuntu,
kāṭcikkiṉiyavum kaḷ niṟintavumāṉa malarkaḷ ceṟintuḷatum, itō nammuṉṉart tōṉṟuvatu
māya cōliyē nām iṉitu vāḻtaṟkēṟṟa iṭamākum" eṉṟatu; maṟṟoṉṟu eḻuntu,
"accōliyiṉum, ataṟkuc ciṟitu cēyttuḷḷa vayalē iṉitākum. cōlikku varuvārum,
varuvaṉavum palavātalāṉum, kāṟṟum eññāṉṟum virivuṭaṉ mōtiyalittalāṉum namakkum, nam
kūṭukaṭkum tīṅkuviḷiyiṉum viḷiyum." eṉṟatu; vēṟoṉṟu, "naṉkaṉam amittuḷḷa
vīṭoṉṟiṉ kūriyē mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa viraṇṭiṉum naṉṟākum. aṉṟiyum atu nammi
maḻikkālattil iṉitu "kākkavallatākum” eṉṟu colliṟṟu. nāṉkāvatāka, vēṟoṉṟu,
"paḻumaramoṉṟiṉ koḻuviya kiḷiyē nallatu" eṉa, maṟṟoṉṟu, mali muḻiñciṉiyum,
vēṟoṉṟu kaṟpāṟiyiyum kuṟittaṉa. iṅṅaṉam, ovvoṉṟum taṉtaṉ karuttiṟ pulaṉāya iṭaṅkaḷik
kūṟic ceṉṟaṉavē yoḻiya, uṟutiyāṉa iṭam oṉṟēṉum kuṟikkappeṟavilli. aṉṟiyum, oṉṟu
kūṟiyatu maṟṟoṉṟiṉ karuttuk kovvā toḻintatu.
ivvaṇṇam īkkaḷ oṉṟuk koṉṟu muraṇiya karuttuk koṇṭu, kalāyttu niṟpak kālamuñ
ceṉṟatu; piṟpakal eytiṟṟu. atupōtu, attēṉī ivviyakkattukkuk kāraṇamāyirunta
attēṉī - vēṟōrīyiṉi nōkki, ‘eṉṉē uṅkaḷ aṟiviṉmi yiruntavāṟu! uṅkaḷik
kāṇumtōṟum eṉ uḷattil vekuḷi yeḻukiṉṟatēyaṉṟi viruppuṇṭākiṉṟatilli. ettakiya
aṟivuḷḷa ciṟṟuyirum ivvaṇṇam vīṇē kālattik kaḻikkātu. antō! itaṉi aṟiviṉmi
yeṉpatā? aṟiyāmi yeṉpatā?" eṉa, atu cālavum ciṉantu, eṉṉi kūṟiṉi? ē, aṟivili!
uṉakku īyaracu peṟavēṇṭum eṉum eṇṇam uḷatu pōlum! aṟintēṉ. aṟintēṉ. iṉṟēl, nī
eṉṉi iṅṅaṉam kēṭṭalkūṭumā? niṟka, yāṅkaḷ kālatti vīṇēkaḻippatāl uṉakku uṇṭākum
iḻaveṉṉi? yāṅkaḷ etiyēṉum ceykiṟōm. atu paṟṟi nī vekuḷutal eṟṟukku? nī uṉ
kāriyattip pārttuk koḷka.” eṉṟaṟintatu.
ittakiya māṟṟatti etir nōkkātu peṟṟa attēṉī, maṉamuḷintu, taṉakkuṇṭāṉa māṉattiyum,
iḻiviyum, uṉṉiyuṉṉip peritum varuntat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. appōtu, tāṉ akkiḷarccikkuk
kāraṇamāyiruntamiyiyum, ataṉāl taṉakkē tuṉpa meytiṉamiyiyum atu ettuṇiyō
aṭakkikkoḷḷa muyaṉṟatu. maṟṟu, ammuyaṟciyāṟ payaṉoṉṟu muṇṭāyiṟṟaṉṟu. ākavē, atu
maṟṟa īkkaḷiṉ mukttik kāṇutaṟkē veṭki, civveṉ ṟeḻuntu pūmputaroṉṟi yaṭintu,
paṇṭēpōl tēṉṟēṭat toṭaṅkiṟṟu. putarkkuṭ pukuntu, āṇṭu aḻaku oḷira malarntu niṉṟa
malaroṉṟiṉ uṭpukuntu, tāṉ itukāṟum kaṇṭaṟiyāta cuviyum, mikutiyumuṭiya tēṉuṇṭu
kaḷittatu. ā" itu pōtaṉṟō atu kaḷivaṇṭeṉṉum peyarkku ilakkāyiṟṟu! atu itupōtu
koṇṭa kaḷippukku or elliyumiṉṟu! atu taṉ vāṇāḷi lōrnāḷilēṉum itupōṉṟa
kaḷippiṉi yaṭinta tiṉṟeṉiṉ vēṟu kūṟuvateṉṉi vēṭki muṟṟuṅ kāṟum naṟavamuṇṭu,
vēṇṭuvaṉavum uṭaṉkoṇṭu taṉ kūṭu nōkki, eṉṟum aṟiyāta oruvakippaṟṟu, aṉṟu ataṉ
maṉatti īrppa, atu virinteḻuntatu. eḻutalum, ataṉ muṉṉar, paṇṭu tāṉ
kēṭṭaṟintaṉavaṟṟi mutaṉ mutalurippak kēṭṭa mutirntavaṇṭu pōtantatu. kaṇṭatum, ataṟkup
peru nāṇ eḻuntu aṟivi mayakka, arukirunta malaroṉṟiṟkuc ceṉṟu maṟintatu. āyiṉum
ammutirvaṇṭu, naṭantaṉavaṟṟik kuṟippāka vuṇarntum, oṉṟum teriyātatiyoppak kūrtta
nōkkattōṭu, "eṉkol! nī ēṉ iṅku vantaṉi? uṉakku uṟṟa teṉṉi? nī vantiruppāy eṉa yāṉ
orukālum niṉittatiṉṟē! ō! uṉ uḻivaṇṭukaḷ yāṇṭuḷa? avaṟṟi nī piritaṟkēynta kāraṇam
yātō? kūṟuti” eṉa viṉaviṟṟu.
itikkēṭṭa akkaḷivaṇṭu, peritum nāṇi, "yāṉ aṟiyēṉ; cōliyiṉ puṟattē yāṉ avaṟṟip
pirintu pōntēṉ ātaliṉ" eṉṟatu.
mu. vaṇṭu:- avi yātu ceytukoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa?
ka.va :- ka-lā-y-k-ki-ṉ-ṟa-ṉa-pō -lu-m
mu.va :- (virintu.) eṉṉō kāraṇam?
ka.va :- tām iṉicceyya vēṇṭuvaṉapaṟṟi.
mu.va :- ā! eṉṉa aruntoḻil purital nērntaṉa! naṉṟu! iḷavēṉiṟkālattu iṉiya kāliyil
mēṟkoḷḷap paṭṭataṉṟō ittoḻil naṉṟu! naṉṟu! ceykaṟiya ceyvataṉṟō periyōratu toḻil!
ka.va - :- eḷḷutal vēṇṭām .ē! aṉṉāy!! iṉiyum iṉṉaṇam iḻittukkūṟaṟka. eṉṉip
poṟut taruḷka. iṉi yāṉ ceyya vēṇṭiyavaṟṟiyum paṇittaruḷka. oṉṟu maṭṭil
kūṟukiṉṟēṉ; yāṉ muṉṉarc ceṉṟirunta ñāṉṟu iyaṟkiyiṉ iyalpiyum,
nammaṉōriṉṟaṉmiyiyum paṟṟik kēḷviyuṟṟa uri muṟṟilum vāymiyākavē tōṉṟiṟṟu;
eṉiṉum, nām avvurivaḻi niṟpāṉ muyaluṅkālattu, vīṇē kalāyttalum veṟuttalum ceytal
nērkiṉṟatēyaṉṟip perum payaṉoṉṟum peṟumāṟilli. eṉṉi?
mu.vā :- niṟka, niṉ vayateṉṉi.
ka.va :- (iḷamit taṉmiyoṭu nāṇmīkkoḷa) ēḻu nāṭkaḷē.
mu.va :-eṉ vayateṉṉi yaṟitiyō?
ka.va :- pala tiṅkaḷāmpōlum.
mu.va :-ām. ām! pala tiṅkaḷ mutirntu uḷēṉ. nī kūṟiya tuṇmiyē. yāṉ nāḷ mutirnta
oru vaṇṭē niṟka. varuka nām iruvarum porutu pārppōm.
ka.va :-ō! ō!atu kūṭātu; kaṭavuḷāṇiyāka atu oru kālum kūṭātu. yāṉ
kaṭṭiḷamiyuṭimiyiṉ payaṉāya valiyuṭiyē ṉākaliṉ, niṉakku ūṟu iḻikkiṉum iḻippaṉ.
mu.va :-eṉavē, nammiruvaruḷ vaṉmiyaṟṟa eḷiyaṉ yāṉaṉṟē. aṟṟāka, iḷamiyum vaṉmiyu mikka
niṉṉi eṉ māṭṭu aṟivuri kēṭkattuṇṭiyatu yātu kol? iḵtu ōr viyappiyaṉṟō
tarukiṟatu!
ka.va. :-aṟṟaṉṟu. meyvaṉmikkum aṟiviṉ vaṉmikkum toṭarpoṉṟu miṉṟu. yāṉ
niṉmāṭṭaṟivuri kēṭpāṉ tuṇintatu nī aṟivuṭiyi eṉpati yāṉ naṉkuṇarntamiyē yākum.
yāṉ aḵtil lēṉākaliṉ, itupōtu iḻittakka niliyi yeytiyuḷēṉ.
mu.va :- mutumiyum iḷamiyum - vaṉmiyum meṉmiyum - aṟivum aṟiyāmiyum - ā!
ēṉō otta piṟaviyum, otta niliyum poruḷākak kiḷarcciceyta vaṇṭukaṭku? eṉiṉum,
varuntaṟka. nām ivaṟṟi yōr oḻuṅkiṟkuk koṇartal kūṭum. niṟka, iṉi nām iruvarum
ottu vāḻtalāmā? eṉṉi niṉ karuttu?
ka.va :-eṉ iṉṉuyi rāṇiyāka, yāṉ - iṉṟē, ēṉ! iṉṉē uṭaṉpaṭukiṉṟēṉ. nām vāḻtaṟku
iṭam yātu? .
mu.va :-aḵtaṉṟu nāṉ mutaṟkaṇ vēṇṭuvatu. nam iruvarkkum karuttukkaḷ muraṇāṅ kālattu
iṭiniṉṟu takuti nōkkuvār yāvarātal vēṇṭum.
ka.vā :- nīyē! eṉṉeṉiṉ nīyē mutumiyum, aṟiviṉ tiṇmiyum uṭiyi.
mu.va :- naṉṟu, nalluṇavāya tēṉ nāṭic ceṉṟu koṇarunar yāvarātal vēṇṭum?
ka.va :- yāṉ. yāṉē!
“iḷiñaṉ valiyaṉ ēviṉa ceyyum
uḷiyā muyaṟciyaṉ oruvaṉ yāṉē.”
mu.va :- mikavum naṉṟu. ākavē, nī eṉṉi itupōtu araca īyākavum niṉṉit
toḻiliyākavum karutiviṭṭaṉi yaṉṟō?
ka.va :- ām ām.
mu.va :-eṉṉē niṉ aṟiyāmi itu muṭipāyiṉ, namatu paṇṭiyakūṭṭukkum, nām
pēṇiniṉṟa. araca īkkum nērnta kuṟi yātō? aṉṟiyum nam iruvarmāṭṭē, oruvar
talivarāka oruvar ēvaliḷiñarātal vēṇṭum eṉpatu peṟappaṭukiṉṟataṉṟō, ākavē, pallōrkūṭiya
kūṭṭattuḷ ivaraṉṉa talivarāvā roruvar vēṇṭappaṭuvataṉi urittalum vēṇṭumō?
itaṉik kēṭṭalum avvīkku nalluṇarvu tōṉṟa, atu mikka uvaki pūttut taṉ ēṉiya iṉaṅkaḷi
nōkkic civveṉṟeḻuntatu. atupōḻtu, ēṉiya īkkaḷellām kuḻappam
ceytukoṇṭēyiruntaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cilamaṭṭum tam vaṉmiyum, eḻucciyum kuṉṟātiruntaṉavē
yaṉṟip piṟavaṉittum kuṉṟip paciyāl varuntiṉa. ciṟitu poḻutu kaḻitalum, cila tam
vaḻakkappaṭiyē tēṉ tēṭacceṉṟaṉa; cila tam kūṭṭi yaṭintaṉa.
iṅṅaṉam ivi perukuḻappaṅ koṇṭu uḻalāniṟpa, iḷamiyum ūkkamum mikutiyākkoṇṭa pala
īkkaḷ puṭicūḻa, ammutirvaṇṭu, naṟuntēṉkoṇṭu īyaraciyirunta veḻiṟ peruṅkūṭṭic
ceṉṟaṭintatu. āṇṭuk kāval purintu niṉṟa ciṟṟiyoṉṟu, ataṉiyuṭpuka viṭātu
takintatu.
avvaṇṭu ataṉik kāraṇamviṉava, atu taṉ īyaraci piṟantupaṭṭatākavum, ataṟkāṅkaṭaṉkaḷ
naṭipeṟuvatākavum, āṇṭu ēṉiya ēkutal kūṭāteṉavum kūṟiṟṟu.
kūṟalum, akkaḷivaṇṭu, uḷaṅkalaṅki, "aiyō! evvāṟu iṟantaṉar nam araciyār! ā!nellu
muyiraṉṟē nīru muyiraṉṟē, maṉṉaruyirttē malartali yulakam ā! iṉi namakkut talimi
tāṅki nammi naṉṉeṟiyil naṭāttuvār yāvarō ē!kāvala! nam araciyār iṟantatu. uṇmiyō
kūṟuka."eṉa iṇintu kūṟalum, atu, “ām. ām. iṉṟukāli kūṭṭik kākkum īkkaḷuṭ cila
veḷicceṉṟirunta poḻuti nōkki, iḷamimikka īyaraciyoṉṟu, veḷippōtal kūṭātu
eṉiṉum, pōtantu, nam araciyoṭu kalāyttuk koṉṟu viṭṭatu" eṉṟatu.
muṭivil akkaḷivaṇṭu taṉ araci cātaṟku aṭippaṭiyāka niṉṟatu tāṉē eṉpatiyum, taṉṉā
ṉikaḻnta kuḻappattiṉ payaṉē itu veṉpatiyum niṉittoṟum ataṉ neñcu ataṉic cuṭṭatu;
piṉṉar ammutirvaṇṭu kaḷivaṇṭi nōkki, “īyaracukaṭkē ottanilimiyiṉṟu; avi
piṟappiṉāl ottaṉa vēṉum tam toḻilāṟṟāṉ ciṟappaṭikiṉṟaṉa: ātaliṉ, piṟappotta
tōṟṟattēm eṉiṉum ciṟappuṟu toḻiṟ ceytu uyarvupeṟutalaṉṟō amivuṭittām"eṉṟatu.
neñcu cuṭa varuntiya avvaṇṭum piṉṉarttaṉ toḻiṉāṭi malar vaṉam pukkatu. yāṇṭum
amitiyē niṉṟu viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
----------

5.yātum viṉaval

ceṅkimāttu vaṭakkil uḷḷatu teṉmali. maliccāralil kāṭu pacuntaḻi pōrttu iṉiya
kāṭci tarukiṟatu. putarkaḷil pala vakip pūkkaḷ malarntu naṟumaṇam kamaḻkiṉṟaṉa. teṉṟal
melleṉa vīcukiṉṟatu. naṇpakal veppattāl vempi viyartta celvamakkaḷ im meṉkāṟṟiṉ varavu
kaṇṭu mikka makiḻcci yaṭikiṉṟaṉar. māli maṇi mūṉṟukku mēlākiyataṉāl veyiliṉ
veppamum ciṟitu taṇintu varukiṟatu.
kāṉattiṉ arukē ōṭum kāṭṭāṟu ciṟitu akalamākavē uḷatu. cekkarc cevēreṉap parantu
viḷaṅkum cemmaṇal, civanta puṭavi yoṉṟi nīṭṭip puraḷaviṭṭatu pōlat tōṉṟukiṟatu. ataṉ
kariyil teṉṉikaḷ ōṅki niṉṟu kulikaḷ tāṅkik kāṟṟil mella acikiṉṟaṉa. kariyarukē
aḻakiya ūroṉṟu tōṉṟukiṟatu. ataṉkaṇ mikavuyarnta māṭi vīṭoṉṟu kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. ataṉ
piṉ puṟattē āṟṟiṟkuk kālaṭippāti varukiṟatu. avvaḻiyē maṅkiyar iruvar varukiṉṟaṉar.
orutti māntaḷir pōṉṟa niṟamuṭiyavaḷ. avaḷatu piṉṉiviṭṭa kūntal iṭimaṟiyumaḷavu
nīṇṭirukkiṟatu. poṉṉil kal vittiḻitta villiyoṉṟu avaḷ kuḻalil iruntu nalla aḻaku
ceykiṟatu. kivaḷikaḷ kaṇkavarum vaṉappuṭiyaṉa. nīla niṟaṅkoṇṭa avaḷatu puṭaviyil nittilam
tittatu pōṉṟa pūvēlikaḷ iruntu polikiṉṟaṉa. avaḷarukē celpavaḷ ittuṇic
ciṟappuṭiyaḷāka illi. ottayāṇṭiṉaḷāyiṉum, oppaṉiyum oḻukkamum nōkkiṉ,
muṉṉiyavaṭku, niliyil tāḻntavaḷeṉṟē ivaḷ tōṉṟukiṟāḷ. maṇiniṟam koṇṭa ivaḷatu
uḷḷattiṉ nērmiyi, mukam iṉitu pulappaṭuttukiṟatu. ivaḷi aṭikkaṭi, avaḷ “tōḻi,
tōḻi" eṉkiṟāḷ; avaḷi ivaḷum "aṉṉāy"eṉa aḻikkiṟāḷ. ākavē, avaḷi nām
taliviyeṉṟum, ivaḷi avaṭkut tōḻi yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkuvōm.
taliviyum tōḻiyum āṟṟiṭikkuṟikkuc celkiṉṟaṉar.ataṉi turutti (an island in
the bed of a river) yeṉṟalum marapu. aṅkē, mā, vēmpu, āl, teṉṉi mutaliya
maraṅkaḷ aḻakuṟat taḻittup pūttuk kāyttu nalam kaṉintirukkiṉṟaṉa. putar taṉpāl ceṟinta
pūvāl naṟumaṇam kamaḻa, teṉ kāṟṟu mella vantu taḷirkaḷi iṉitacippa, tari muḻutum
aṟukupaṭarntu pacuṅkampalam virittatupōl iṉpam ceykiṟatu. kiḷḷiyum puṟavum kuyilum
pūviyum icipāṭum iv viṉiya viṭam iv viruvar maṉattiyum vēṟiṭam peyarātavāṟu
piṇittu viṭukiṟatu. “aṉṉāy, iṅkē ciṟitē amarvōmē eṉṉa iṉiya iṭam;” eṉkiṉṟāḷ
tōḻi.
talivi:-eṉṉi yātum viṉaval. tōḻi, iṉpamuṭiya varkkē iyaṟkik kāṭciyum
iṉpamaḷikkiṉṟatu.
tōḻi :-itaṟkuttāṉ “keṭṭārkku naṭṭārō il" eṉṟu cāṉṟōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. atukiṭakka;
ēṉ ivvāṟu pēcukiṉṟāy? iyaṟki yāvarkkum potu. vāḻkkiyil iṉpamuṭiyārkkēyaṉṟi,
ataṉi iḻantārkkum aḵtu iṉpam vaḻaṅkuvatil iḻukkuvatu kiṭiyātu.
talivi:-appaṭittāṉ aṟivuṭiyōr kūṟukiṉṟaḷar. āṉāl, eṉṉaḷavil atu
māṟupaṭṭirukkiṟatu.
tōḻi :- appaṭiyāṉāl, itō! iṅkē tōṉṟum teṉmaliyum, ceḻuṅkāṉamum, cēyāṟum
uṅkaṭku iṉpam ceyyavilliyō?
talivi :- ivaṟṟik kāṇumpōtu eṉ uḷḷam tikīr eṉṟu kalaṅkukiṉṟatu. māṉiṉam tuṇiyoṭu
kūṭic celvatum, paṟavikaḷ tattam tuṇiyōṭiruntu pāṭi makiḻvatum eṉakku varuttam
ceyyumē eṉṟu eṉ neñcam añcukiṉṟatu.
tōḻi :- (maruṇṭu) aṉṉāy, nī colvatu oṉṟum eṉakku viḷaṅkavilli. talivar uṭaṉ
irukkumpōtu iviyellām uṉakku iṉpattit tāṉē ceyyum? avari viṭṭut taṉiyē
vantiruppataṉāl ivvāṟu pēcukiṉṟāyō?
talivi :- tōḻi, iṉi, taṉiyētāṉē irukkavēṇṭum. ippōtu uṉṉōṭu taṉiyē iruppatu
taṉimiyākumā?
tōḻi :- (piṉṉum maruṇṭu) aṉṉāy, uṉ karutti viḷakkamāyc col, eṉakku uṉ pēccu
ciṟitum viḷaṅkavēyilli.
talivi :- nēṟṟu nam kātalar pēciyatil iruntu avar nammip pirintē pōvar eṉṟu
aṟikiṉṟēṉ. atu mutal eṉ neñcam ivvāṟu tuyaraṭiyat toṭaṅkiviṭṭatu.
tōḻi :- (cuṭṭikkāṭṭi) atō, nam talivar varukiṉṟār. nī ap putariṭiyē maṟintukoḷ.
nāṉ avaruṭaṉ pēci itaṉ uṇmiyi aṟikiṉṟēṉ.
talivi :- nallatu; appaṭiyē cey. (maṟital)(talimakaṉ varukiṉṟāṉ tōḻi pūppaṟippatik
kāṇkiṉṟāṉ; avaḷ arukē varukiṉṟāṉ.)
talivaṉ:- (neruṅki) tōḻi, nī iṅkē eṉṉa ceykiṉṟāy?uṉṉōṭu talimakaḷ vantāḷē eṅkē?
tōḻi :- (kiyāṟ kāṭṭi) atō tōṉṟum putarkkup piṉṉē pūppaṟikkap pōṉārkaḷ.
aḻittu varalāmē. (pōka oruppaṭutal)
talivaṉ:- (taṭuttu) vēṇṭā. nil nil. uṉṉiṭam oru ceyti colla vēṇṭum. aṉṟum nī
tāṉē tuṇi ceytāy?
tōḻi :- (talikuṉintu taṉakkuḷḷē) talimakaḷ coṉṉatu pōl, ivar taṉ pirivittāṉ
ippōtu nampāl colvārpōla irukkiṟatu. irukkaṭṭum.
talivaṉ :-oṉṟumilli. āṇmakaṉ oruvaṉukku uyirāvatu etu? teriyumō?
tōḻi:-aiyaṉē, yāṉō peṇ.eṉakku atu teriyātē.
talivaṉ:-(muṟuvalittu) “viṉiyē āṭavarkku uyir."atāvatu, pukaḻtarakkūṭiya viṉiyic
ceyvatu tāṉ āṇmakaṉukku uyir. uyireṉpatu uyirotta kaṭami.
tōḻi:-āmām. irukkalām. ataṉi eṉakkuc colvatāl payaṉ?
talivaṉ:- antak kaṭamiyic ceyya yāṉ pōka vēṇṭum. cila nāṭkaḷē pirintirukka vēṇṭum.
eṉṉa colkiṉṟāy?
tōḻi :- itittāṉ eṉakkuc colla virumpiṉīrkaḷā? appaṭiyāṉāl, itaṉōṭu vēṟoṉṟu
colvārkaḷē? atu uṅkaṭku niṉivirukkumē.
talivaṉ:-(tiṭukkiṭṭu) eṉṉa atu? eṉakku niṉivilli.
tōḻi:-eṉakku niṉivirukkiṟatu: “viṉiyē āṭavarkkuyirē, maṉiyuṟi vāḷnutal
makaḷirkku āṭavar uyirē"eṉṟu colvārkaḷē.
talivaṉ:-(makiḻntu) āmām. makaḷir tam kaṇavaṉi uyir pōlak karuti oḻukuvatupōla, āṭavar
tām ceyya vēṇṭiya viṉiyi uyirpōl karutic ceyya vēṇṭum. itutāṉē ataṉ karuttu.
tōḻi:-ēṉ? ataṟku uḷḷa poruḷi uḷḷapaṭiyē......
talivaṉ:-(iṭimaṟittu) nāṉ colvatu tavaṟēr?
tōḻi:-...āṭavarkku viṉiyē uyir; avartam maṉiviyarkku avarē uyir. avar pirintāl,
avartam makaḷirum uyir pirivar. itutāṉē uṇmip poruḷ!
talivaṉ:-eppaṭiyō pōkaṭṭum. iṉṟu yāṉ talimakaḷōṭu iṉitiruppataṟku aṉṟu nī ceyta
-------- text (corresponding to printed page 106) missing here!!
----------
veṟṟi peṟṟīrkaḷō eṉa aiyuṟṟup pitaṟṟuvar; ivvāṟu palappala collip paḻippar.
talivaṉ :- ēṉ? nīṅkaḷum maṉiyiluḷḷa piṟarum avvāṟu alar uṇṭākātavāṟu ceyyalāmaṉṟō?
tōḻi :- ulivāyi mūṭalām; ūrvāyi mūṭalāmō?
talivaṉ :- uṇṭō , muyaṉṟāl muṭiyāp poruḷ?
tōḻi :- eṉṉa colkiṉṟīrkaḷ.
“cevviya tīviya colli avaṟṟoṭu
piya muyaṅkiya aññāṉṟu avi ellām
poyyātal yāṉyāṅku aṟikōm maṟṟu aiya!
akalnakar koḷḷā alar talit tantu
pakalmuṉi veñcuram uḷḷal aṟintēṉ
makaṉ alli maṉṟa iṉi."
(kali - 19)
talivaṉ :- (muṟuvalittu) tōḻi! nī ēṉ vekuḷkiṉṟāy?
tōḻi :- aiya! nīṅkaḷ aṉṟu ceytavaṟṟiyum iṉṟu ceyvatiyum pārkkiṉ ippōtu avi .
ellām poyyē eṉpatu veḷippaṭi. poykūṟi oḻukupavaṉ makaṉā (maṉitaṉā)?
talivaṉ :- nī talimakaḷpāl koṇṭa kātalāl ivvāṟu pēcukiṉṟāy. nāṉ ataṟku
makiḻkiṉṟēṉ. āṉāl -
tōḻi :- "āṉāl" eṉpatu eṉṉa?
talivaṉ :- viṉi ceyya vēṇṭiyaviṭattu atic ceyyātu viṭupavaṉ maṉitaṉākāṉ. 'ceyyat
takka ceyyāmiyāṉuṅ keṭum; alla ceyyavum keṭum.' itu nī aṟiyātatallavē! mēlum
kātalarip pirintu oru kāriyam ceytu vanta vaḻi, avar kātal māṇpuṟumē tavira, vēṟu
oṉṟum uṇṭākātu. nāṉ ceṉṟu varukiṉṟēṉ.
tōḻi :- (neṭitu niṉintu) aiya,
-----------
“celiṉi, ceṉṟu nī ceyyum viṉi muṟṟi
aṉpu aṟamāṟi yām uḷḷat tuṟantavaḷ
paṇpum aṟitirō eṉṟu varuvāri
eṉtiṟam yātum viṉaval; viṉavil
pakaliṉ viḷaṅkumniṉ cemmal citiyat
tavalaruñ ceyviṉi muṟṟāmal āṇṭu ōr
avalam paṭutalum uṇṭu”
iṉi nī celka, ceṉṟu aṅkē ceyyum viṉiyi muṭittukkoṇṭa piṉpē iṅkiruntu
varuvōrip pārttu aṉpu illāmiyāl eṉṉāl tuṟakkappaṭṭa talimakaḷ nalatti aṟivīrkaḷō?
eṉṟu viṉavuka; viṉimuṭiyu muṉpu avarkaḷi viṉava vēṇṭā. viṉavil, cūriyaṉpōl talimi
koṇṭu viḷaṅkum umatu talimi citintuviṭum. vēṟu vakiyāl keṭutaṟkillāta umatu
ceyviṉiyum, muṭivaṭiyātapaṭi avalam uṇṭāyiṉum uṇṭākiviṭum.
talivaṉ :- ēṉ? eṉakku viḷaṅkac col.
tōḻi :- eṉ tōḻi niṉ pirivāṟṟātu, iṟantālum iṟantu viṭuvāḷ. - (putar
maṟivilirukkum talivi veḷippaṭukiṟāḷ)
talivṉ :- itō, tirumakaḷ varukiṉṟaṉaḷē.... (ceṉṟu iru kikaḷiyum paṟṟi) - nī
eppōtu iṅku vantāy?
talivi :- kātala, ituvariyum eṉ tōḻi coṉṉatik - kēṭṭīrkaḷā?
talivaṉ :- iṉi ati niṉiyēṉ. ulaku muḻuvatum oruṅku varuvatāyiṉum iṉi uṉṉip
piriyēṉ. vā. ivviyaṟkik kāṭciyik kaṇṭu iṉpuṟalām.
iyaṟkik kāṭci iṉpa ūṟṟu.
------------

6. miyīrōti maṭanallīrē

"miyirōti maṭanallirē, miyirōti maṭanallīrē" eṉṟu moḻintu vāyveruvum immaṭa nallāḷ
yāvaḷ? ivaḷatu malarmukam vāṭiyuḷatu; maḻikkaṇ nīr tuḷikkiṉṟatu; vāyitaḻ mella
acikiṉṟatu; meyyilum ciṟitu taḷarcci pulaṉākiṉṟatu. ivaḷi uṟṟu nōkkuvōm. ivaḷatu
maṉattē peruntuyar niṉṟu alikkiṉṟatu; ivaḷ aṭikkaṭi maruṇṭu nōkkukiṉṟāḷ; ām,
avaḷatu uḷḷattē accam piṟantirukkiṟatu; eṉṉō kāraṇam?”
ivvāṟu tammaṉattōṭu collāṭi varum ipperiyār uṭiyālum naṭiyālum ciṟanta cāṉṟōr
pōlat tōṉṟukiṟār. ivar piṉṉē celvōm. atō teṉ maliyiṉ aṭiyil niṟkum pāṟimītu
ēṟi niṟkiṟār. pakalavaṉum uccikaṭantu mēlitticiyi nōkkukiṉṟāṉ. veyiliṉ veppamum
mikutiyāka illi. kāṭum pacuntali parappi veṇ pū virintu naṟumaṇam kamaḻkiṉṟatu.
māṉiṉam pulmēyntu makiḻcci mikukiṉṟaṉa. kiḷḷiyum puṟāvum kūṭṭaṅ kūṭṭamāyp
paṟantu tirikiṉṟaṉa. tiṉippuṉaṅkaḷilum katirkaḷ pālkaṭṭic ciṉṉāḷil muṟṟum
pakkuvattēyuḷḷaṉa. itō, iccāṉṟōr maliyaṭiyiṉ mēṟṟiciyil uḷḷa tiṉippuṉattē tamkaṭ
pārviyic celutti ūṉṟi nōkkukiṉṟār; ēṉ? naṉṟu, naṉṟu. appuṉattarukē niṟkum
vēṅki marattiṉ aṭiyil vīṟṟirukkum uruvam yātu? teyvamō? peṇṇō? terintilatē!
ām, peṇṇē: avaḷ eṉṉa ceykiṉṟāḷ?
★-★-★-★
itō iccāṉṟōrum avaḷirukkum tikku nōkkiyē celkiṟār, ivar ēṉ avaḷ aṟiyāvaṇṇam
putarkaḷiṭiyē patuṅkippatuṅkip pōkiṉṟār. ammaṅki yarukē celkiṟār pōlum. avaḷōṭu
ētēṉum collāṭa virumpukiṉṟaṉarō? aṟṟaṉṟu; avaḷ pēccoliyik kēṭkum aḷavilirukka
vēṇṭumeṉṟu karutukiṉṟār pōlum. iṉṟēl, ivar apputaraṭiyil amara māṭṭāraṉṟō? eṉṉa
naṭakkiṉṟatu, kāṇpōm, neñcē, nī oṉṟum vēṟāka niṉiyātē; cālpu kuṉṟat takuvaṉavaṟṟic
cāṉṟōr ceyyār.
★-★-★-★
“miyirōti, maṭanallīrē” eṉṟu moḻinta ammaṉṉavaṉ itaṉiyaṟintāl eṉṉa niṉivāṉ?
'miyirōti' eṉṟu aḻitta am maṇimoḻi iṉṉum eṉ ceviyakam niṟiya olikkiṉṟatē! maṭa
nallīrē eṉṟa avaratu māṇpu eṉ maṉattē avari uruppaṭutti viṭṭatē! avaratu vāymi
kāṇamāṭṭāta eṉ tāyatu tāymi eṉṉa payaṉuṭiyatām. mēṉi melikiṉṟēṉ; vēṟoṉṟum niṉi
villēṉ. ētukku eṉ tāy ivvāṟu avvēlaṉi vēṇṭukiṉṟāḷ.'
"viṉaviṉ eṉṉiyō avaṉi?" eṉṟoru colleḻukiṉṟatu. mēlē kūṟiyavāṟu tāṉē
pēcippulampiya maṅki cuṟṟu muṟṟum nōkkukiṉṟāḷ, vāṭiya mukam malarkiṉṟatu; paṇitta
kaṇ aruḷ vaḻikiṉṟatu; viḷarttavāy nāvāl nīr culavap peṟukiṉṟatu. tiṇippuṉattiṉ
uḷḷirunta peṇṇorutti, tiṉittāḷi vilakkik koṇṭu vēṅkiyaṭikku varukiṉṟāḷ. ivaḷ
yār? poṟuppōm; ivarkaḷ pēcciliruntutāṉ ivaḷi iṉṉāḷeṉa aṟital vēṇṭum.
★-★-★-★
“tōḻi, muṭintu pōyiṟṟō? eṉ tāy iṉi eṉṉa ceyyap pōkiṟāḷām? uḷḷataṉ uṇmiyi
uḷḷavāṟu kāṇum aṟivupeṟṟum, makkaḷ ataṉic cevvē payaṉ koḷḷa aṟiyātu alamaru
kiṉṟaṉarē!ivarkaṭku aṟivuccuṭar uṇṭākumō? aḵtu uṇṭāku nāḷ eṉṟō teriyavilliyē"
eṉkiṉṟāḷ. vēṅkiyaṭiyil vīṟṟirukkum melliyal.
tōḻi :- ammā, nāmē avaṉi viṉaviṉāl eṉ?
melli :-(accattōṭu) avaṉiyā? anta vēlaṉiyā?avaṉi eṉṉeṉṟu viṉavuvatu? -
tōḻi :- murukaṉatu vēl avaṉ kiyiluḷḷa vēl. avaṉ āṭum veṟiyāṭṭu am murukaṉ
poruṭṭu. avvēl aṟiviṉ vaṭivam, ākavē, avaṉukku nallaṟivum uḷatāyirukkum. ataṉāl,
avaṉatu aṟivu veḷiyākumāṟu nāmē ataṉi viṉavuvōmē?
melli :-eppaṭi viṉavuvatu?
tōḻi :- eppaṭiyā? appaṭittāṉ?
melli :- eṉakku viḷaṅkavilliyē?
tōḻi :- ēṉ viḷaṅkavilli? aṉṟu naṭantati niṉivil koṇṭu maṟinta coṟkaḷāl avaṉi
viṉavuvatu.
melli :-(mayaṅkiya pārviyuṭaṉ) eṉṟu naṭantati?ataṉi naṉṟākat tāṉ col.
tōḻi :-aṉṟu nām taṭṭi koṇṭu kuruviyōṭṭi viḷiyāṭi yiruntōmaṉṟō?
melli :-ēṉ, nām iṉṟumtāṉ avvāṟē ceykiṉṟōm.
tōḻi :- aṉṟu nam taṭṭi muṟintu pōkavē, itō immūṅkiṟ putariliruntu mūṅkiloṉṟi
yaṟuttut taṭṭiyoṉṟu ceytōmē, aḵtēṉum niṉivilirukkiṟatō?
melli :-(puṉmuṟuvaluṭaṉ). ām. peru muḻakkam ceytu koṭiya ōciyi uṇṭupaṇṇiṟṟē,
antat taṭṭi ceytōmē, aṉṟā?
tōḻi :-ataṉāl tāṉē nām ati “vevvāyt taṭṭi”eṉṟōm.
melli :-ataṉālā, aṉṟaṉṟu; ataṉic cuḻaṟṟiya māttiriyē, tiṉip puṉattil paṭinta
paṟavikaḷ aṉittum paṟantu ōṭiviṭṭa tōṭu, tiṉimaṇikaḷ palavum citaṟiviṭṭaṉa.
tōḻi :-etaṉālō oṉṟu. appōtu nām anta vēṅki marattiṉ kavaṭṭil kaṭṭiṉa paraṇ mītu
iruntapōtu eṉṉa naṭantatu?
melli :- vaṇṭiṉam vantu poṉṉiṟam koṇṭa pūvil tēṉ uṇṭu iṉpamāka muraṉṟatu. nām ataṉ
iṉpavōciyik kēṭṭu ataṉ mītu karuttic celutti niṉṟōm.
tōḻi :-appōtu nām tiṭukkiṭṭuc caṭṭeṉap piṉpuṟam . tirumpiṉōmē, ēṉ?
melli :-(nāṇit tali kaviḻkiṉṟāḷ)
tōḻi :-"miyirōti,maṭanallīrē"eṉṟoru kāḷi nammi aḻittāṉaṉṟō? avaṉ ēṉ nammi
"miyirōti maṭanallirē" eṉṟāṉ. ataṉi yaṟivāyō?
melli :-nam piṉniṉṟa avar kaṇṇiṟ paṭṭatu nam kūntal. ataṉāl avvāṟu aḻittār? itu
tāṉē kāraṇam?
tōḻi :- itaṉi tuṇukkamāka aṟintu koḷḷum uṉakku iṉṉum nāṉ eṉṉa colvatu?
melli :- illi yilli. itu tāṉ kāraṇamāka irukkalām eṉpatu eṉ karuttu. atu
kiṭakkaṭṭum nī col.
tōḻi :-appōtu avaṉ nammi eṉṉa kēṭṭāṉ?
melli :-keṭuti viṉaviṉār.
tōḻi :- atu teriyātā? eṉṉa collit tam keṭuti viṉaviṉāṉ?
melli :-yāṉ eyta ampupaṭṭup puṇ mikuntu perun tuyar koṇṭu yāṉi yoṉṟu taṉ
uyarnta maruppu viḷaṅka ippuṉat tarukē vanta tuṇṭō? eṉṟār.
tōḻi :- avaṉuṭaṉ vanta nāykaḷ kurittuk kutittu nāṟpuṟamum ōṭivara, avaṉ ciṟitu
nēram kaḻintapiṉ ceṉṟāṉaṉṟō?
melli :-tōḻi, avaratu cāntaṇinta mārpum, vil cumanta tōḷum, taṉittirunta
nammiṭattē avar collāṭiya takimiyum eṉ neñcik kavarntu koṇṭaṉa. avar colliya coṟkaḷ
iṉṉum eṉ ceviyil olikkiṉṟaṉa. avar poruṭṭu nām varuntum ivvaruttatti aṉṉi
iṉṉum aṟiyavilliyē. itaṟkoru vaḻiyumilliyā?
tōḻi :-ataṟkuttāṉ takka vaḻitēṭappaṭukiṟatē? ataṟkuḷ nī ēṉ tuyarkiṉṟāy?
melli :- vēlaṉikkoṇṭu veṟi yayarkiṉṟaṉaḷē aṉṉi; ituvō vaḻi.
tōḻi :- atutāṉ eṉṟu nam tāyum niṉikkiṉṟāḷ. avvēlaṉum avaṭkut takkavāṟu tāṉ
kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ.
melli:- eṉṉa atu?
tōḻi :- eṉṉavā? "em iṟi aṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉṟu innōy, taṇi maruntu aṟival" eṉṟu
kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ.
melli :- (tiṭukkiṭṭu) uṇmiyākavā? vēlaṉum ivvāṟu poy kūṟuvāṉā? vēlaṉatu
iṟivaṉ murukaṉaṉṟō; avaṉ nammi aṇaṅkiṉavaṉ illiyē; poymoḻintu oḻukum vēlaṉ
poymmiyi nam tāy aṟintilaḷē, itaṟku eṉ ceyvatu?
tōḻi :- ippaṭittāṉ vēlaṉ kūṟuvāṉ. aṟiyā mākkaḷ ataṉi uṇmi yeṉṟu karutuvar,
iṉṟum atit tāṉ nam tāyum kēṭkap pōkiṉṟāḷ.
melli :- iṉṟum atittāṉē collap pōkiṉṟāṉ?
tōḻi :-āmām. āṉāl, iṉṟu yāṉum vēlaṉik kaṇṭu oṉṟu kēṭkap pōkiṟēṉ. eṉ
kēḷviyāl vēlaṉ poymmiyum, nam oḻukkattiṉ meymmiyum viḷakkamaṭiyap pōkiṉṟaṉa.
melli :-eppaṭi? eṉakku ati viḷaṅkaccol.
tōḻi :- vēlaṉ iṉṟu nam tāyiṭam murukaṉ nammi aṇaṅkiṉaṉ; eṉpāṉāyiṉ, veṟi
koḷvēla'uṉatu iṟivaṉ, mata mikka vēḻattiṉ meyyil ūṭuruvat taṉ ampic celutti, piṉ
ataṉ aṭic cuvaṭupaṟṟi vēṭṭam cellumō? eṉa viṉavak karutukiṉṟēṉ. viṉaviṉ eṉ?
melli :-(muṟuvalittu) viṉaviṉ eṉṉām? yāṉi vēṭṭam purintu vanta vīraṉ oruvaṉi nām
namakku iṟivaṉākak koṇṭuḷḷōm eṉ terintu koḷvāṉ.
★-★-★-★
tōḻi ēṉ ciṟippuṟatti nōkkukiṉṟāḷ. aṅkē ēṉ vēlikkaṇ paṭarntuḷḷa taḻikaḷ
acikiṉṟaṉa. nallatu. aṅkirun toru valviṟ kāḷi eṉ mēṟkut tici nōkkic
celkiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ ētō taṉakkuḷ collik koḷkiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ piṉceṉṟu kēṭpōm.
kāḷi:- iṉi, namakkum im melliyal nallāḷukkum uṇṭākiya oḻukkam avaḷ tāykkum
tamarkkum pulaṉāki viṭum. ākavē, ivaḷi iṉi iṅkē talikkūṭal aritu. ākavē, iṉic
cāṉṟōric celutti varintu koḷvatē kaṭaṉ.
ā, itō iccāṉṟōrum nammuṭaṉ vantu avaṉ collik koḷvatik kēṭṭu makiḻkiṉṟār. ivar
eṉṉa collukiṉṟār pārppōm.
cāṉṟōr:- ivvilvīraṉ ceyta muṭivē ciṟantatu. ivaṉ im muṭiviṟku varak kāraṇam yātu?
it tōḻiyiṉ nalluriyaṉṟō? ivaḷatu aṟivuṭimiyi eṉṉeṉpēṉ, ittali makaṉ ciṟippuṟam
niṟpati yuṇarntu avaṉ im muṭipu ceyyumāṟu pēciviṭṭāḷ. atumaṭṭō, talimakaṭkum
ōrāṟṟāl tēṟutal kūṟiviṭṭāḷ. kaṟpuṭi makaḷir kaṇavaṉiyallatu piṟa teyvaṅkaḷiyum
maṉattālum niṉiyār. vēlaṉ murukaṉ aṇaṅkiṉāṉ eṉṟavaḻi, tali makaḷ am murukaṉi vaṇaṅkal
vēṇṭivarum. aḵtu avaḷ kaṟpu neṟikku māṟākum. ataṉiṉṟum ivaḷ avaḷit
tappuvittuviṭṭāḷ. veṟiyum vilakkiyāki viṭukiṟatu. nallatu, nām iṉṟu iṅkē
vantatum naṉṟē yāyiṟṟu. ivaḷatu ceyki ēṉimakaḷirkku naṉṉeṟi kāṭṭum nayamuṭiyatu.
ākavē, itaṉip pāṭṭiṭi vittut tamiḻulakil niṟuvuvēṉ.”
★-★-★-★
cāṉṟōr, tam uṭimaṭippil iruntu ōr ōli naṟukkum eḻuttāṇiyum eṭukkiṉṟār,
marattaṭiyil amarntu. tām karutiyataṉi eḻutukiṉṟār. ivar eḻutik koṇṭirukkaṭṭum.
nām ammakaḷir irukkumiṭam celvōm.
tōḻi :- atumaṭṭumā, nam tāyum namakkum talimakaṉukkum uṇṭākiya toṭarpi yaṟintu
koḷvāḷ. piṉpu, nam kaṟpu neṟikkum iḻukku uṇṭākātē.
melli :- tōḻi, nī ceyvatu naṉṟē. ippōtē virintu ceṉṟu ataṉic cey.
tōḻi :-naṉṟu. avvāṟē ceykiṉṟēṉ. (pōtal)
★-★-★-★
melliyalum taṉ nallakam pukukiṉṟāḷ; tōḻiyum kaṇmaṟivil ceṉṟuviṭṭāḷ. iccāṉṟōr
tām eḻutiyati muṭittuk koṇṭu paṭikkiṉṟār; ataṉik kēṭpōm;
"amma vāḻi tōḻi nammali
amiyaṟut tiyaṟṟiya vevvāyttaṭṭiyiṉ
naṟuviri yāra maṟaveṟin tuḻuta
uḷikkuraṟ ciṟutiṉi kavartaliṟ kiḷiyamal
peruvari yaṭukkattuk kurīi yōppi
