ōṅkiruñ cilampiṉ oḷḷiṉar naṟuvī
vēṅkiyaṅ kavaṭṭiṭi nivanta vitaṇattup
poṉmaru ṇaṟuntā tūtun tumpi
iṉṉici yōrā viruntaṉa māka;
miyī rōti maṭanal lirē
novviyaṟ pakaḻi pāynteṉap puṇkūrntu
evvamoṭu vanta vuyarmarup poruttalnum
puṉattuḻip pōka luṟumō maṟṟeṉac
ciṉavuk koḷ ñamali ceyirttuppuṭi yāṭac
collik kaḻinta valviṟ kāḷi
cāntā rakalamun takiyum mikanayan
tīṅkunām uḻakkum evva muṉarāḷ,
naṉṉar neñcamoṭu mayaṅki veṟiyeṉa
aṉṉi tanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ
emmiṟi yaṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉ ṟinnōy
taṇimarun taṟival eṉṉu māyiṉ,
viṉavi ṉevaṉō maṟṟē, kaṉalciṉa
miyal vēḻa meyyuḷam pōka
ūṭṭi yaṉṉa vūṉpura ḷampoṭu
kāṭṭumāṉ aṭivaḻi yoṟṟi
vēṭṭañ cellumō nummiṟi yeṉavē.”
      - (akam, 388)
★-★-★-★
pāṭṭiṉ nalam kāṇṭal
itaṉkaṇ "ammavāḻi" yeṉpatu toṭaṅki, "iruntaṉamāka" (9) eṉpatu vari, melliyal nallāḷ
valviṟkāḷiyākiya talimakaṉ keṭutiviṉavi varuṅkāl iruntanili viḷakkappaṭukiṉṟatu.
iṅkē avarkaḷ mūṅkil aṟuttu 'vevvāyt taṭṭi" ceytu. ataṉāl puṉattiṟ paṭinta
kuruvikaḷi oppiṉa ceyti, "amiyaṟuttu iyaṟṟiya vevvāyt taṭṭiyiṉ," (2)
"peruvariyaṭukkattuk kuruviyōppi" (5) eṉṟa aṭikaḷāl kuṟikkap paṭukiṟatu.
akkuruvikaḷi oppiyataṟkuk kāraṇam kūṟuvataṟkāka, “uḷik kural ciṟutiṉi kavartaliṉ"
(4) eṉkiṉṟār. attiṉiyiṉ arumiyi uṇarttum poruṭṭu, attiṉippuṉam āṅkiruntu
cantaṉa maraṅkaḷi muṟṟa eṟintu, avviṭattiyum aritiṉ uḻutu payir
ceyyappaṭṭateṉkiṉṟār. itaṉi “naṟuviri yāram aṟa eṟintu uḻuta uḷik kural ciṟutiṉi"
eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷār. vantu paṭinta kuruvikaḷum oṉṟiraṇṭalla. pala eṉṟaṟku “kiḷiyamal
kurīi" eṉkiṉṟār.
iṉi, ittiṉip puṉamirunta iṭam teṉmaliyiṉ carivu; tiṉimuṟṟum kālam vēṅki malarum
kālamākum. appōtu tāṉ appuṉam kākkap paṭutal vēṇṭum. tiṉippayirum mika vuyaramāka
vaḷarntiruntatu. ākavē, puṉaṅkāttaṟku amikkum paraṇ mika vuyarntirukka
vēṇṭiyatāyiṟṟu. ik karuttellām tōṉṟavē, ic cāṉṟōr, “ōṅkiruñ cilampiṉ oḷḷiṉar
naṟuvi, vēṅkiyaṅ kavaṭṭiṭi nivanta itaṇattu’ eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟār.
iṉi, paraṇmītirunta naṅkiyar, kuruviyōppi, paraṇarukē malarntu maṇakkum vēṅki malariṟ
paṭintu tātutum tumpiyiṉ iṉṉiciyil karuttic celutti yiruntaṉaraṉṟō? iṉṟēl,
aṅkē valviṟ kāḷiyik kaṇṭiruppar. ataṉāltāṉ tōḻi, "tumpi iṉṉici orā
iruntaṉamāka” eṉkiṉṟāḷ.
iṉi, "miyirōti" eṉpatumutal, "pōkaluṟumō maṟṟu" eṉpatuvari valviṟkāḷi vaḻaṅkiya
coṟkaḷākum. puṉam nōkki vantavaṉ etirē tam kariya kuḷirnta kuḻalik kāṭṭit tam iḷami
nalam ilaka niṉṟaṉar immakaḷir. keṭuti viṉavivaruvōṉ,avar muṉvantu niṉṟu cevviyaṟintu
kēṭkum attuṇi maṉavamitiyilaṉāvāṉ. vēṟu oci ceyyiṉ, avar añcuvar. avaṉ kaṇṇetirē
tōṉṟuvatu avartam kariya kuḻalē. ataṉāl, "miyirōti maṭanallīrē" eṉkiṉṟāṉ. atu kēṭṭa
ammaṭavār tiṭukkiṭṭu avaṉ mukanōkkiṉar. avaruḷ taliviyiṉ kūriya pārvi avaṉukku
varuttattic ceytatu. ataṉi ōrāṟṟāl maṟittuk koṇṭu, "yāṉ eyta pakaḻiyāl puṇ
mikuntu eḻunta tuyarattōṭu vanta yāṉi yoṉṟik kaṇṭīrō” eṉṟu kēṭkaluṟṟu, "novviyaṟ
pakaḻi pāynteṉap puṇkūrntu, evvamoṭu vanta uyar marup poruttal num puṉattuḻip pōka
luṟumō” eṉkiṉṟāṉ, taṉ ampiṉi "novviyaṟ pakaḻi"eṉṟataṉāl, taṉ ampiṉ vaṉmiyum,
avaḷatu pārviyiṉ kaṭumiyum cuṭṭiṉāṉ, “pāynteṉap puṇ kūrntu"eṉṟataṉāl, ampu
kuṟivaryttu nōy ceytatum, tāṉ vēṭkiyuṟṟu vētaṉi yuṟṟatum kūṟiṉāṉ.
"evvamoṭuvanta uyarmarup poruttal" eṉṟu kūṟavē, ciṉam mikuntu vārātu,
puṇṇuṟṟeytiya āṟāttuṉpattāl vantatu yāṉi eṉṟum, āyiṉum aḵtu uyar maruppuṭiya
pēr yāṉiyeṉṟum, tāṉ taliviyit talikkūṭa vēṇṭumeṉṉum vēṭki nōyāl veytuṟṟu
vanta ceytiyum cuṭṭiyirukkiṉṟāṉ. tāṉ vantatu pōla, yāṉiyum vantatuṇṭō eṉṟaṟku,
"numpuṉattuḻip pōkaluṟumō” eṉṟu viṉaviṉāṉ.
ivvāṟu kēṭṭavaṉ neṭitu nillātu ciṟitu nērattiṟkuḷ kaḻintāṉ; avaṉōṭu vanta vēṭṭi
nāykaḷ vilaṅkukaḷiṉ naṭamāṭṭamaṟintu ciṉaṅkoṇṭu kurittuk kutittu ōṭattalippaṭṭaṉa.
ataṉāl avaṉum avaṟṟiṉ piṉṉē virintēka vēṇṭiyavaṉāṉāṉ; iṉṟēl, ciṟitu neṭittē
ceṉṟiruppaṉ. ikkarutti, “ciṉavukkoḷḷumali ceyirttup puṭiyāṭac collikkaḻinta
valviṟkāḷi” eṉṟu kuṟikkiṉṟār.
itukāṟum paṇṭu nikaḻntatu kūṟiya tōḻi, iṉi, nikaḻvatu kūṟattoṭaṅki, taṅkaḷ
niliyum, tāyatu niliyum kūṟaluṟukiṉṟāḷ:
“valviṟkāḷi, cāntār akalamum takiyum mika nayantu īṅku nām uḻakkum evvam uṇarāḷ
naṉṉar neñcamoṭu mayaṅki veṟiyeṉa aṉṉi.”
tāy taṉ makaḷatu evvam uṇarāḷāyiṉaḷ; ataṉāl aṉpuṭiya avaḷatu nalla neñcam
mayaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu; vēlaṉik koṇṭu veṟiyeṭukka niṉikkiṉṟāḷ eṉpatu itaṉāl
terikiṟatu.
iṉi veṟiyāṭum vēlaṉ kūṟuvati muṉṉaṟintu,
"aṉṉi tanta mutuvāy vēlaṉ emmiṟi aṇaṅkaliṉ vantaṉṟu innōy taṇi maruntu aṟival"
eṉpaṉa, avvāṟu kūṟuvāṉāyiṉ, nām talivaṉ keṭuti viṉāvi vantu talip peytatik kūṟi
"viṉaviṉ evaṉōmaṟṟē? kaṉal ciṉa miyal vēḻameyyuḷam pōka ūṭṭi aṉṉa ūṉpuraḷ ampoṭu
kāṭṭumāṉ aṭivaḻi oṟṟi vēṭṭam cellumōtum iṟi eṉavē” eṉṟu moḻikiṉṟāḷ.
"nummiṟi vēṭṭam cellumō?" eṉṟāl, avaṉ "avvāṟu celvōṉ nam iṟi" eṉṟu uṇarvāṉ.
ūṉ paṭintu utiram vārac cekkarc cevēleṉac civantu tōṉṟum ampiṉi "ūṭṭiyaṉṉa
ūṉpuraḷ ampu" eṉṟu pāṭiyuḷḷārē, ippulavar yārāka irukkalām? ūṉmikap paṟṟip
puraḷum ampiṉi, ūṉ ūṭṭiyatu pōṉṟa ampu eṉkiṉṟa ivar "ūṭṭiyaṉṉa oṇtaḷirc ceyali"
(akam 68) eṉappāṭiya pulavarāṉa avarō eṉṉum niṉivu piṟakkiṟataṉṟō? avarē tāṉ itaṉip
pāṭiyavarum. avar peyar ūṭṭiyār eṉpatu.
--------

7.poruṇmoḻi

yāṉ ciṟitu nāṭkaḷukkumuṉ oru āṅkila nūlip paṭittu varuṅkāl, ittalip
poruḷpaṟṟiya coṟpoḻi voṉṟu ataṉkaṇ irukkak kaṇṭēṉ. ataṉiyāṉ nuṇukip paṭittu
varuṅkālattil, cila nuṇporuḷkaḷ amin tiruppatiyuṇarntu ataṉ uṭporuḷit tamiḻil
eḻutiṉ palarkkum payaṉpaṭu- meṉṟeṇṇiṉēṉākaliṉ, itupōtu eḻutuvēṉāyiṉēṉ.
poruṇmoḻi yeṉpatu paḻamoḻiyip pōl nuṇ poruḷi yakattē koṇṭa uyarntōr kūṟum
naṉmoḻiyākum. itu nam tamiḻ nūlkaḷil “poruḷuri" yeṉṟum “poruṇmoḻik kāñci"
yeṉṟum vaḻaṅkap peṟukiṉṟatu. *[1]"poyyil pulavaṉ poruḷuri yeṉṟu cīttalic
cāttaṉārālum, 'mūturi poruntiya"eṉṟa cūttirattuḷ"poruṇmoḻikkāñci" yeṉṟu
aiyaṉāritaṉārālum kūṟappaṭutal kāṇka. poruṇmoḻik kāñciyāvatu, "erintilaṅku
caṭimuṭi muṉivar, purintu kaṇṭa poruṇmoḻintaṉṟu"[2](pu-ve-māli 271) eṉpar.
poruṇmoḻi yeṉṟatoṭar poruḷi moḻital eṉaviriyum vēṟṟumit tokiyākum. moḻi,
mutaṉilit toḻiṟpeyar. poruḷāvatu muṉivarkaḷ virumpik kaṇṭu teḷinta
uṇmiyeṉpatākum.
-----
[1]. maṇi.22-6/
[2]. pu.ve.māli'cū.12.
----------
itaṉkaṇ muṉivarāvār 'caṭimuṭiyuṭiyarāy uyarnta voḻukkamum' ulakiṉiyum ataṟku
muḻumutalākiya iṟivaṉiyum oppakkāṇum ñāṉak kaṇṇumuṭiyarāy, makkaṭku vēṇṭiya aṟivu
nūlkaḷum piṟavum ākkittarum pēraruḷ uṭiya periyōrāvar eṉpatu aṟiñar muṭipu.
ivartam aṟivum āṟṟalum ēṉiyarkkuḷḷaṉa pōlvaṉa alla. curuṅkac colliṉ, makkaḷuṭ
kaṟṟōriṉum ciṟanta kalviyaṟivum, oḻukalāṟum uṭiyōr eṉpatu mikiyākātu. ākavē,
muṉivar perumakkaḷ nammaṉōrkku aṟivukoḷuttu māṟṟāl urippaṉavē poruṇ moḻiyeṉpatu
aṟiñar koḷḷum koḷkiyāyiṟṟu.
ipporuṇmoḻi viḻumiya poruḷit taṉṉakattē koṇṭu perukiyum, curuṅkiyum varutal nam
nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇalām. curuṅki varutalē perumpāṉmi- yeṉiṉum, curuṅkiyē varutal
vēṇṭumeṉṟa variyaṟiyiṉmiyāl perukiyum varutal iyalpāyiṟṟu. ivi ettuṇic
curukkamāyirukkiṉṟaṉavō attuṇiyum poruṭ perukkamuṭiyavātal kaṇkūṭu.
eṭuttukkāṭṭāka, tirukkuṟaḷ oṉṟē yamiyum. ciṉmeṉ moḻiyāl viḻumiyaporuḷit
taṉṉakattē yuṭittāy ovvoru tirukkuṟaḷum aminti, ruttalum, toṭṭaṉit tūṟum
maṇaṟkēṇiyip pōlak kaṟṟaṉittuṟum poruḷāḻamuṭimi ciṟattalum koṇṭu poruṇ
moḻiyātalil evvakiyiṉum muṟpaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. ittirukkuṟaḷi oru periyōr “kaṭukit
tuḷittuēḻ kaṭalip pukaṭṭik kuṟukat taṟitta kuṟaḷ" eṉṟaṉar eṉpa. ivviyalpē
poruṇmoḻikkaṇ iruttal vēṇṭu meṉpatu āṅkilap periyōrkaḷiṉ muṭipumākum. poruṇ
moḻikkaṇ varum poruḷ tattam maṉattiṉkaṇ muḷittut tōṉṟiya paḻutta eṇṇaṅkaḷākavum, paranta
ulakil nilavum poruḷkaḷip pulamiyāṟ kaṇṭu ārāyntu teḷinta muṭipukaḷākavum iruttal
vēṇṭumeṉpatum, avi oru cila coṟkaḷālākiya coṟṟoṭarkaḷāyiruttal vēṇṭu meṉpatum,
avi yārāyum tōṟum payaṉtaruvaṉavāyiruttal vēṇṭumeṉpatum poruṇmoḻiyiyalpukaḷi
yārāyum āṅkilap pulavarkaḷiṉ koḷki. curuṅkac colliṉ, paṉiyaḷaviṉavāya poruḷum
muṭiyum oruṅkē tiṇikkap peṟṟut tiṉi yaḷaviṟṟāya coṟṟoṭarkkaṇ viḷaṅkuvatu
poruṇmoḻiyiṉ uyirniliyeṉpar.[1]
---
1. *"The essence of aphorism is the compression of a mass of thought and
observation into a single saying.” - John Morley.
-----
iṅṅaṉam, coṟcurukkamum poruṭperukkamu muṭimiyāl, itaṉip poṉṉēpōṟ pulavarkaḷum
maṟṟavarkaḷum pōṟṟi oḻukukiṉṟaṉar. peruṅkāppiyattuṭ ciṟantu viḷaṅkuñ
cintāmaṇipāṭiya tiruttakkatēvarum tirukkuṟaḷāya poruṇ moḻikkaṇiruntu cila koṇṭu
tam, nūliṉkaṇ vittiḻittu aḻaku ceytu koḷvārāyiṉar. avaṟṟuḷ, [2]“toḻutakiyuḷḷum
paṭiyoṭuṅkum oṉṉār - aḻutakaṇṇirum aṉittu’ [3] “ceyka poruḷic ceṟunar
cerukkaṟukkum, eḵkataṉiṟ kūriya til’ eṉpaṉa aṭaṅkum.
----
[2]. tirukkuṟaḷ - 828.
[3]. kuṟaḷ - 759.
----
ivaṟṟuḷ, mutaṟkaṇṇatāya poruṇmoḻiyi,
[1]. “toḻuta tamkiyiṉuḷḷuntuṟu muṭiyakattuñcōra
aḻutakaṇṇiṉuḷḷum aṇikalattakattum āyntu
paḻutukaṇṇarintukollum paṭiyuṭaṉeṭuṅkum paṟṟā(tu)
oḻikayār kaṇṇumtēṟṟam teḷikuṟṟār viḷikuṟṟārē.”
eṉṟa pāṭṭilum, iraṇṭāvataṉi.
[2]. “ceykaporuḷyārumceṟuvāricceṟukiṟkum
eḵkupiṟitilliyiruntēyuyirumuṇṇum
aiyamiliyiṉpamaṟaṉōṭaviyumākkum
poyyilporuḷēporuḷmaṟṟallapiṟaporuḷē.”
eṉṟa pāṭṭilum amittuk koṇṭiruttalum pulappaṭum. ------
[1]. cin. 89. [2]. cin, 497,
----
vināyaka purāṇam pāṭiya āciriyarum tirukkuṟaḷiṉ poruṭpālē tampurāṇam
pāṭuntoḻiṟku vāyttaporuḷum karuviyumākak koṇṭatu, ipporuṇmoḻi nūṟkaṭkiṭantu
aṇiceytalē yaṉṟit toḻiṟkaṇmuṭṭaṟukkum tuṇik karuviyātalum valiyuṟuttum. ivvāṟu
ivi payaṉpaṭutalōṭu amiyātu iṭiyūṟukaḷāl kalaṅkit tikippārkkuk
kalakkantīrkkumaruntumāy uṇmip poruḷaṟitaṟkutavi ceytal kuṟittē pēkkaṉ eṉṉum pulavar
perumakaṉār ivi aṇiyātalē yaṉṟit toḻilmuṭṭiṟuttum, kalakkattiṭit
teḷivupiṟappittum nalamceykiṉṟaṉa eṉṉum karuttuṟak kūṟiṉar. [3]. ippēkkaṉ eṉpavarē
piṟitōriṭattil ipporuṇmoḻikaḷi uppuṅ kaḻikaṭku oppumi kūṟi, kaḻiyiṭattu
uppiṉippeṟṟu, vēṇṭumiṭattuk kalantu cuviyākkik kōṭalpōl, ivaṟṟiṉiṭattu
meypporuḷ kaṇṭu vēṇṭumiṭattut tuṇiceytu koḷḷalāmeṉṟār.
---
[3]. “These wise sayings, sid Bacon, the auther of some of the wisest
of them, are not only for ornament but for action and business, having a
point or edge, whereby knots in business are pierced and discovered. And
he appluds Cicero’s description of such sayings as salpits, that you
may extract salt out of them and sprinkle it where you will” - John
Morley. -
------------
‘muntiyiruntu naṭṭōr koṭuppiṉ, nañcu muṇpar naṉinā karikar" eṉpatu naṟṟiṇikkaṇ
varuvatōr (355) poruṇmoḻiyākum. itaṉi āciriyar tiruvaḷḷuvaṉār “peyakkaṇṭum
nañcuṇṭamivar nayattakka, nākarikam vēṇṭu pavar" eṉṟa kuṟaḷiṉkaṇ (50) peytu koṇṭaṉar.
mēlum, “nīriṉ ṟamiyā vulakam pōlat, tammiṉ ṟamiyā nantayattu” eṉṟa ataṉ
kaṉanīriṉṟamiyātu ulakam eṉpatu oru poruṇmoḻi (naṟ.1) itaṉṉayum tirukkuṟaḷil
“nīriṉṟamiyā tulakeṉiṉ yāryārkkum, vāṉiṉ ṟamiyā toḻukku eṉṉuṅ kuṟaḷiṉkaṭ
(kuṟaḷ.20) peytu koṇṭaṉar.
innaṟṟiṇip pāṭṭil “tāmarit taṇṭā turati mīmicic cāntiṟ ṟoṭutta tīntēṉ pōlap
puriya maṉṟa puriyōr kēṇmi" “eṉavaruvatu oru poruṇ moḻiyākum. itaṉ poruḷ -
tāmarittātiṉiyum cantaṉattātiṉiyum ūti accantaṉamrattiṟ ceyta vaḷḷattuḷ vittaviṭattu
avi iṭattu nikaḻporuḷum iṭamumāyiyintu tam perumipulap paṭuttiṉāṟpōla uyarntōr
tammiṭattē vittu kēṇmiyum perumi pulappaṭutti viḷaṅkum eṉpatu. itaṉi maṇivācakap
perun takiyār tam tiruk kōviyāriṉkaṇ, “cīriya lāviyum yākkiyu meṉṉac
ciṟantamiyāṟ, kāriyal vāṭkaṇṇi eṇṇaka lārkamalaṅ kalanta, vēriyum cantum viyaṟan teṉak
kaṟpil niṟparaṉṉē, kāriyal kaṇṭarvaṇ ṭilli vaṇaṅku meṅkātalarē, ‘ eṉṟa tiruppāṭṭil
(301) amittuk koṇṭaṉar. ivi pōlum poruṇmoḻiyāṭcikaḷ palavātaliṉ immaṭṭil
niṟuttukiṉṟēṉ.
ipporuṇmoḻikaḷ pulavar perumakkaḷ iyaṟṟum nūlkaḷiṉ iṭiyiṭiyē kiṭantu ulakiyalil
makkaḷ oruvarōṭoruvar kūṭiyum pirintum oruṅkiyintum, piṟa nikaḻccikaḷik kaṇṭum.
uṇarum uṇmikaḷi eṭuttukkāṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. vāḻkkiccākāṭu ukikkum makaṉ
avvāḻkkikkuriya māṇpukaḷiyuṇarntu vallaṉāyiruppiṉ, atu ūṟiṉṟāki āṟṟi yiṉitu
ceṉṟu payaṉtaruvatākum; ataṉiyaṟiyāṉāyiṉ, avvāḻkki tuṉpaniliyamāy mikappala nōykaḷit
talittalittaruvatāy muṭiyum. ivviyalpu paṟṟiyē ulakiyalaṟiviṉi vaḷarkkum poruṇ
moḻikaḷ mikka iṉṟiyamiyātaṉavāya- mintirukkiṉṟaṉa. vāḻkki arampum allalum karampum
ceṟintiruttalāl, iṉṉōraṉṉa poruṇmoḻikaḷik kiṭantaṉa kiṭantāṅkē eṭuttukkōṭal
vēṇṭum. avaṟṟiṉkaṇ orārāycciyum vēṇṭāvām. innalam kuṟittē, ivi nāmaṟiyum
palavuṇmikaḷuḷ, oṉṟi vilakkiyātal, vēṟoṉṟit taḻuviyātal, oruṇmiyiṉ
orupuṭiyivilakkiyātal, maṟṟorupuṭiyi mikipaṭa viritturittātal cellātu ettakiya
kaṭṭuppāṭṭiṟkum, nilikkum, oḻuṅkiṟkuntaka amintiruttal vēṇṭum eṉpar. ātalāl,
ivi makkaḷ vāḻviṟkeṉavaminta vitikaḷ eṉpatu mikiyākātu.
itukāṟum nāmporuḷmoḻikaḷāvaṉa iviyeṉṟum, avi potuvakiyiṟ paṭumāṟu ituveṉṟum,
avaṟṟiṉ potuviyalpum aṟintōm. iṉi ivaṟṟāl nām peṟum aṟiviyalpiyum,
ipporuṇmoḻikaḷ payaṉpaṭu māṟṟiyum oruciṟitu kāṇpām.
aṟivāvatu "nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ tīmiyum uḷḷa vāṟē yuṇartal" eṉpatu. itu makkaḷ
tōṉṟumpōtē uṭaṉ tōṉṟi avarit toḻiṟpaṭuttukiṉṟatu. nām peṟṟatum peṟuvatumākiya
aṟiviṉ iyalpiṉi āṅkilap perumakkaḷ nālvakippaṭuttut talimi, kaṭimi, iṭimi mutaliya
ēṟṟattāḻvukaḷ kaṟpikkiṉṟaṉar. oruvaṉ taṉ iyaṟkiyaṟiviṉāl poruḷiyiyalpuṇarntu
peṟuvatu taliyāya aṟivu eṉṟum, ēṉi makkaḷiṉ oḻukkaneṟikaḷil niṉṟum avarōṭu
payiṉṟum avvāṟē poruḷkaḷiyuṇarntum peṟuvatu ataṉiṟ kuṟinta ciṟappiṉiyuṭiya
aṟivāmeṉṟum, mūṉṟāvatu pala nūlkaḷik kaṟṟu avi kāṭṭum neṟikkaṇ maṉattic celuttip
poruḷkaḷi yārāyntu teḷintu peṟuvatāya aṟivu eṉṟum, ivaṟṟiṟ kaṭiyāya aṟivāvatu
nūlkaḷiṭattum āciriyarkaḷiṭattum kaṟṟatuṇiyē peṟuvatu eṉṟum kūṟuvar.
[1]ippākupāṭṭiṉ kaṭ poruṇmoḻikaḷ, mūṉṟām pakutikkaṇ amintu, uṇmiyoṉṟiṉ oru
cār iyalpiṉi namakkuṇartti ēṉiyavaṟṟi ārāyumāṟu ceyyavallaṉavākiṉṟaṉa.
--------
[1]. * “It has been sid that our knowledge is this; that we know best,
first, what we have divined by native instinct; second what we have
learned by experience of men and things; third what we have learned not
in books, but by books - that is, by the reflections that they suggest;
fourth, last and least what we have learned in books or with masters.
The virtue @f an aphorism comes under the third of these heads; it
conveys a portion of a truth with such point as to set us thinking on
what remins.’ - J. Morley.
----------------
immūṉṟām pakutikkaṭpaṭum aṟivu ulakil makkaḷiyum piṟaporuḷkaḷiyum kaṇṭum paḻakiyum
uṇarntum peṟap paṭutalāl, itaṉi vāḻkkiyaṟivu eṉṟal poruntuvatām. itu oruvaṉ
nalvāḻkkikkut tuṇiceyyum aṟivup poruḷ niṟinta moḻiyām. oruvaṉ kaṟpatum,
kaṟṟārōṭu paḻakuvatum ellām ulakil nalvāḻvu naṭātti nalam peṟutaṟ poruṭṭēyām.
ākavē, avaṉ kaṟkum nūlkaḷuḷ ip poruṇmoḻikaḷ iṭam peṟāvāyiṉ, aviyum avaṟṟip
paṭikkuṅkāṟ kaḻinta poḻutum payaṉpaṭātoḻivaṉavēyākum. ākavē, ipporuṇmoḻi ovvoru
nūlilum amital iṉṟiyamiyātām.
nām vāḻum ikkālamum, nammaṉap pāṉmiyum putiya vuṇarcciyum oḻukkamum koṇṭa
muṉṉēṟṟa meṉṉum poruḷāl iḻukkappeṟukiṉṟaṉa. "oru ūriṉkaṇ oru aracaṉ iruntāṉ -
eṉṟu toṭaṅkum cuvaṭikaḷ maṟikiṉṟaṉa; iṉṉayāṇṭil ittiṅkaḷil innāḷil innikaḻcci"
naṭipeṟṟateṉṉum muṟi eḻuntu nilavukiṉṟatu. epporuḷik kēṭpiṉum, epporuḷik
kaṟpiṉum, apporuḷik kēṭṭavāṟō, kaṟṟavāṟō uṇarta liṉṟip palvakiyiṉum ārāyvatum,
muṭipukūṟutalumē eṅkum viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṅṅaṉamē nampaṇṭi varalāṟum, aṟital
vēṇṭumeṉṉum avā nākarikamum eṅkum ellōru maṉattilum eḻuntulavutalik kāṇkiṉṟōm.
ivvuṇarcci mikkueḻum ikkālattil, ‘paṇṭi varalāṟṟārāycciyum,
poruḷiyalpuṇarcciyum, araciyal kiḷarcciyum viravic cellum ikkālattil,
makkaḷiyalpiyum ārāytal vēṇṭumeṉpatu kūṟāmalēyamiyum. makkaḷiyalpu poruṇmoḻikkaṇ
aṭaṅki paruttalāl, avvārāycci poruṇmoḻi yārāycciyē-yāyiṟṟu.
ivvārāyccikkup peritum payaṉpaṭum poruṇ moḻikaḷik kūṟavallavar oruvar vēṇṭumaṉṟē.
avaryār? avarē nampulavar perumakkaḷ. avarē, ulakil nām
vāḻviṉiyalpiyuḷḷavāṟēyurikkum āṟṟal paṭittavarāvar; cila uṇmikaḷiyum meymmikaḷiyum
eṭutturikkum cilariṉum, ivarkaḷ pōṟṟaṟkuriyar. pulavarkaḷiṉ ceyal nilivaṉappum
tūymiyum niṟintatu. carittiraṅ kūṟutal, oḻukkaneṟiyamittal, araciyalārāytal,
coṟpoḻivu ceytal ākiya iviyāvum oruvaṉatu ulakiyalaṟiviṉ tiṭpanuṭpattukkēṟpa
vamintaṉavām. oḻukkaneṟiyum, carittir muṟiyum, araciyalvātamum, avartam kalvi,
kaṭippiṭi mutaliyavaṟṟiṟkēṟpa makkaḷāl matikkappaṭuvaṉavām. maṟṟu, eḷiyatāya uri nūl
eḻutum oruvaṉ, oṉṟiraṇṭāya poruṇmoḻikaḷit taṉ nūliṭiyē toṭuppaṉēl avaṉ
muṉivariyoppak karutappaṭukiṉṟāṉ. ituvē apporuḷkaṭkum itaṟkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu. .
“tīmi kaṇṭōr tiṟattum periyōr
tāmaṟin tuṇarka eṉpa mātō.”
      -- (naṟ. 116) eṉṟum,
“neṭiya moḻiya kaṭiya ārtalum
celva maṉṟutaṉ ceyviṉip payaṉē
cāṉṟōr celva meṉpatu cērntōr
puṉka ṇañcum paṇpiṉ
meṉkaṭ celvam celvameṉ patuvē”
      -- (naṟ. 210) eṉṟum,
“tīyum valiyum vicumpu payantāṅku
nōyum iṉpamum ākiṉṟu mātō.”
      -- (naṟ. 294) eṉṟum
varum poruṇmoḻikaḷāl, ivaṟṟi yākkiya periyōrkaḷum nallicic cāṉṟōr variciyuḷ iṭam
peṟuvarāyiṉar. ittakiya poruṇmoḻikaḷ ilavāyiṉ, irāmāyaṇamum cintāmaṇiyum piṟavum
niṉṟunilavutal yāṅṅaṉam amiyum?. mēṉāṭṭār pḷuṭārk (Plutarch) eṉpavari mika
mēmpaṭavurippatu, kati kūṟuvatiṉum makkaḷiyum avartam paṇpiṉiyum veḷippaṭuttuvatē
cīriya karuttāyk - koṇṭamiyē yeṉpar. iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, ‘araciyal piḻittōrk kaṟam
kūṟṟāvatu um, uricāl pattiṉik kuyarntōr ēttalum, ūḻviṉi yuruttuvan tūṭṭu
meṉpatu um, cūḻviṉic cilampu kāraṇamākac cilappati kārameṉṉum peyarāl, nāṭṭuvatum
yāmōr pāṭṭuṭic ceyyuḷ" (cilappatikam) eṉṟu toṭaṅkiṉamiyum ikkaruttē paṟṟiyeṉka.
poruṇmoḻi yiṭiyiṭi viraviya nūlkaḷ cilavē āṅkila nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉaveṉṟum, avi
ettuṇi mikutip paṭukiṉṟaṉavō attuṇi oḻukkamum uyarvum makkaṭkuṉ ṭākumeṉṟum,
ivaṟṟil muṟpaṭṭu viḷaṅkuvaṉa namnāṭṭu moḻikaḷē yeṉṟum jāṉmārli eṉpavar 1887ām
āṇṭu navampar mātam 11-ām nāḷ eṭiṉpark eṉṟa nakarattil ceyta coṟpoḻivil
kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. avar nāṭṭil oru kiṟittava āciriyar cūtiṉāl tamkipporuḷiḻantu,
kaṭaṉ vāyppaṭṭu atu tīrkkum āṟṟalilarāy amerikkāviṟku oṭip pōy makkaṭkeṉac
cilaporuṇmoḻi niṟinta nūloṉṟi “cilacolliṟ palaporuḷkaḷ” (Lacon or Many things
in few words) eṉṟa peyariṭṭu 1820-ām āṇṭil veḷiyiṭṭār eṉṟum, annūṟkaṇ uḷḷaṉa
poruḷuriyākātu poyyuri (uḷḷiṭillāta uri, veṟṟuri) yāka iruntami kaṇṭu tām
tīyiṭṭerittuviṭṭatākavum avar kūṟukiṉṟār.
pērilakkiyaṅkaḷum ciṟṟilakkiyaṅkaḷum poruṇmoḻi puṇarttukkūṟal tamakku aṇiyākak
koṇṭiruttal vēṇṭu māyiṉum avi perumpāṉmiyum makkaṭku vēṇṭum potuvuṇmikaḷiyē
yuṇarttukiṉṟaṉa.
“immi yulakat ticiyoṭum viḷaṅki
maṟumi yulakamu maṟuviṉ ṟeytupa
ceṟunarum viḻiyum ceyirtīr kāṭcic
ciṟuvarp payanta cemmalōr"
(akam- 66) eṉṟum,
“aṟantalip piriyā toḻukalum ciṟanta
kēḷir kēṭupala āṉṟalum nāḷum
varuntā vuḷḷamō ṭiruntōrk killeṉac
ceyviṉi purinta neñciṉar.”
(akam - 173) eṉṟum,
‘neṟiyi ṉiṅkiyōr nīrala kūṟiṉum
aṟiyā miyeṉ ṟaṟiyal vēṇṭum”
(cilap-10) eṉṟum
“nallatu ceyta lāṟṟī rāyiṉum
allatu ceyta lōmpumiṉ atutāṉ
ellāru muvappa taṉṟiyum
nallāṟṟup paṭuu neṟiyumā ratuvē”
(puṟam - 195) eṉṟum,
“celvattup payaṉē yītal,
tuyppē meṉiṉē tappuna palavē.”
eṉṟum varuvaṉavaṟṟālaṟika.
makkaḷ nāṭōṟum iyaṟṟum kuṟṟaṅkaḷiyum nalaṅkaḷiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭi avaṟṟi nīkkutaliyum,
pōṟṟutaliyum ceyyum poruṇmoḻikaḷ niṟinta nūlkaḷ vēṇṭum. āṅkilēyarkaḷ makkaḷiṉ
oḻukkaṅkaḷiyum, avar ceyyum piḻikaḷiyum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavaṟṟiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭum
poruṇmoḻikaḷip puṇarttaṟku kati vaṭivaṅkaḷiyē karuviyākak koṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉar. ivi
perumpāṉmiyum eḷiya naṭiyil vaḻuvillāta iṉiya tīñcoṟkaḷālākkappaṭṭup
pērilakkiyaṅkaṭkuḷḷa amiti nirampimiḷirkiṉṟaṉa. nam nāṭṭil ammuṟiyik kiyāṭal
vēṇṭic cilar puṉikatikaḷ eḻutukiṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟuṭ perumpāṉmiyi makkaḷiṉ
oḻukkakkēṭuṇarttum poruṇ moḻi puṇarttalil amintaṉavāyiṉum, ammoḻikaṭkuriya
coṟcurukkamum poruṭperukkamumiṉṟi, vaḻuvellām niṟintu ulavukiṉṟaṉa. "murukaṉ"
eṉṉum kati nūloṉṟu ivvāṇṭil ceṉṉip palkalik kaḻaka vakuppiṟkup pāṭamāka
amintuḷḷatu. ataṉkaṇ makkaḷ vāḻvu peṟuvatum, naṭippiṟaḻcciyāl kēṭṭiṟkuḷḷāki
varuntutalum, uḻavut toḻiṟku vēṇṭum poruṇ moḻikaḷum niṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟu,
ataṉ coṉṉaṭi mikat tāḻnta niliyil uḷatu. nūleḻutappukuvōr.
--
“Plutarch’s aim was much less to tell a story than as he says ‘to
decipher the man and his nature" - Studies in Literature P.66.
-----------
emmoḻiyil eḻutavirumpukiṉṟaṉarō ammoḻiyil - iyaṉṟavāṟu piḻiyiṉṟi viḷaṅka
eḻututal vēṇṭum. coṟṟoṭarkaḷil ilakkaṇa vaḻukkaḷ amintiruttal orupuṭi niṟpa,
"oruvaḷ” mutaliya ilakkaṇa varampukaṭanta coṟkaḷ pala viraviyiruttal kaṟpārkkuk
kēṭupayakkum taṉmiyatākiṉṟatu. niṟka, uri nūlkaḷiṟ kāṇappeṟum poruṇmoḻikaḷ
eṇṇiṟantaṉavākalāl orucilakūṟi ikkaṭṭuriyiṉi muṭikkiṉṟēṉ.
“ēṭṭuc curikkāy kaṟikkākātu. puttakañāṉamēyuṭiyavarkaḷ viṭayaṅkaḷi yārāyumpōtu
tamatu conta niliyiyiḻantu apputtaka niliyiyē peṟṟu ārāyvārkaḷ. oru tēcattiṉ
iyalpiṉi at tēcattiṟkuc ceṉṟu tirumpiṉōr kūṟakkēṭṭa aṟivu evvāṟu
valiyuṭiyatākumō avvaḷavuvaliyē puttakañāṉamē yuṭiyōṉ ārāycciyiṟpaṭum
poruḷumākum. puttakamē kaṟṟōṉ kāṇum poruṇmi palliḻantōṉ taṉ vāyilamittuk koḷḷum
pōlip pallippōl maṉattiṟ patikiṟatu. tāmē tamconta aṟiviṉāl aṟintatō
iyalpākavēyaminta avayavam pōl maṉattil valuppaṭṭu niṟkiṟatu” - ti. celvak kēcavarāya
mutaliyār.
“kāmukaṉāvāṉoruvaṉ oru vaṉitiyiṭattu aṉpuṭi yaṉ eṉpatu avaḷ okkalik kaṇṭuḻi
nikaḻum aṉpiṉ aḷavu paṟṟiyē aṟiyappaṭum.” - civañāṉayōkikaḷ.
“māṉamāvatu taṉṉilimiyiṟ ṟāḻāmiyum, teyvattāṟ ṟāḻvuvantuḻi uyirvāḻāmiyumām."
"uyir nilattu viṉivittiṭṭārkku viḷivum atuvē - parimēlaḻakar"
cila āṅkilap poruṇmoḻikaḷ:
1. nuṇṇaṟivōr iḻikkum tavaṟukaḷum, aṟiñar ceyyum āravāramum, nallōr piḻikkum
kuṟṟaṅkaḷum ākiya iviyāvum cēranikaḻvatu kiḷarcciyē.
Follies committed by the sensible, extravagances uttered by the clever,
crimes perpetrated by the good, that is what makes revolution.
2. makkaḷiṉ maṭami nōkki mikka ciṉaṅkoḷḷutal maṭaṉeṉappaṭṭa yāviyum - oruṅkē yaḻittuk
koḷḷum ceyalkaḷuḷ mikkatoṉṟām.
Excessive anger aginst human stupidity is itself one of the most
provoking of all forms of stupidity.
3. iṉpamaṉṟu aṟiñar vēṇṭuvatu; tuṉpattiṉīkkamē. avar tēṭiccelvatum ataṉiyēyām.
Not pleasure but freedom from pin. is what the sensible man goes after.
4. taṉakku muṉṉiruntōṉ oruvaṟku oppākak karutil, avaṉiṉ iraṭṭitta āṟṟaluṭiyaṉātal
vēṇṭum.
To equal a predecessor one must have twice his worth.
5. ceytaṟ keḷiyatāyiṉum oṉṟic ceytaluṟupavaṉ, ataṉi ariteṉṟum, ariyatāyiṉ
eḷiteṉṟum karutippukutal vēṇṭum.
What is easy ought to be entered upon as though it were difficult, and
what. is difficult as though it were easy.
itukāṟum kūṟiyavāṟṟāl, poruṇmoḻiyeṉpatu tamiḻ nūlkaḷil poruṇmoḻik
kāñciyeṉṉum tuṟip pakutiyuḷ aṭaṅkuvateṉpatum, atu coṟcurukkamum poruṭ
perukkamum peṟṟu nūlkaṭku aṇiyātalēyaṉṟit toḻilmuṭṭaṟukkum karuviyāmeṉpatum,
poruṇ moḻikaṭku āṅkilēyarkūṟum ilakkaṇam ituveṉpatum, oruvaṉ kaṟṟaṟkuriya
poruḷkaḷuḷ ivaṟṟik kaṟṟal avaṉ vāḻkkic cākāṭukittaṟkaṇ iṉṟiyamiyātu
vēṇṭappaṭumeṉpataṉāl, ivi makkaḷ vāḻviṟkeṉavaminta vitikaḷām eṉpatum, aṟivāvatu
iḵteṉpatum, atu nālvakip pākupāṭupeṟum eṉpatum, appākupāṭṭiṉkaṇ
poruṇmoḻiyāleytum aṟivu mūṉṟām pākupāṭṭilaṭaṅkumeṉpatum, immoḻikaḷ makkaḷiṉ
maṉap pāṉmiyiyuḷḷavāṟu kāṭṭum iyalpiṉa eṉpatum, ārāycciyē poruḷākac cellum
ikkālattil poruṇmoḻikaḷik kaṟṟu makkaḷiṉ maṉappaṇpārāytalum vēṇṭumeṉpatum, maṉappaṇpu
kaṇṭu uṇarntu kūṟum poruṇmoḻiyāṭciyāl oruvaṉ naṉku matikkappeṟuvaṉ eṉpatum,
namnāṭṭu ilakkiyaṅkaḷ makkaḷiṉ potuvāya paṇpiṉi yuṇarttukiṉṟaṉaveṉpatum, ikkāla uri
nūlkaḷiṟ cila, makkaḷiṉ ciṟappup paṇpiṉiyuṇarttiṉum vaḻumalintuḷḷaṉaveṉpatum,
ēṉiyuri nūlkaḷiṟ cila poruṇmoḻikaḷ ivaṟṟiyum uṇarttukiṉṟaṉaveṉpatum piṟavum
kūṟappaṭṭaṉavām.
----------

8. ulaka varalāṟu

nam kaṭpulaṉām ivvulakiṉ tōṟṟam, vaṭivu mutaliyavaṟṟi ārāyntaṟinta pēraṟivāḷarkaḷ
mikappalar eṉiṉum, avaruṭciṟantuniṉṟār koṇṭatu ulaku oru uruṇṭi vaṭivāyaporuḷ
eṉpatu. atu, vaṭivil uruṇṭiyeṉiṉum, eṇṇariyaparumaṉum, oḷiteṟikkum iyalpum
porunti, aḷappariya ākāyameṉṉu maḷakkaril mitakkum palvēṟu vakippaṭṭa poruḷkaḷ ceṟinta
oru tippiyap poruḷākum. iṉṉum ataṉik kūrntu nōkkiṉ, atu, oru nārattam
paḻattiyottu, mēluṅkīḻuntaṭṭiyāy, irumaruṅkiṉum accukkaḷ niṟutta avi uṭceṉṟu
naṭuvil kūṭumeṉa niṉittaṟku ētuvāy, avvacciṉ vaḻiyākavē cuṟṟukiṟatu eṉak
karutuvataṟkum iṭaṉāyirukkiṟatu.
ivvāṟu, itu cuṟṟuvataṉāl, itaṉ eppākam ñāyiṟṟiṉ muṉ varukiṟatō, appākam,
aññāyiṟṟiṉ oḷi peṟṟup pakaṟ kālattip peṟukiṟatu akkāli, maṟṟip pākattiliruḷ
parava irākkālam nikaḻum. niṟka, itu peṟum oḷikkuk kāraṇamāyatu ñāyiṟu
eṉpateṉṉiyeṉiṉ, atu oru oḷiniṟinta aḻaṟpiḻampu eṉpar. ataṉiṭattiliruntē ivvulaku
tōṉṟiṟṟeṉavum kūṟuvar. vērkkaṭali, mocci mutaliyavaṟṟit tīyiliṭuṅkāl, avi
veppattāl veṭippa, avaṟṟiṉiṉṟum vitikaḷ mikunta virivuṭaṉ. veḷikkiḷampaloppa,
aññāyiṟeṉṉum aḻaṟpiḻampiṉ kaṇ veṭittu virinteḻunta poruḷkaḷuḷ oṉṟu itu eṉavum
kūṟuvar. iṉṉaṇam ñāyiṟṟiṉiṉṟum veḷip pōnta poruḷām ippūmikkum iññāyiṟṟukkum
oru kaṭpulaṉākāt toṭarpuṇṭu. attoṭarpu oṉṟiyoṉṟu paṟṟiyē niṟkum. viḷaṅkakkūṟiṉ,
ñāyiṟu pūmiyit taṉ māṭṭiḻuttalum, appūmi ataṉi yiḻuttalumāya toṭarpu. itaṉi
vaṭamoḻiyāḷar ākarṣaṇa caktiyeṉpar. mēṉāṭṭār. Attraction of Gravitation (aṭrākṣa
ṉāv kirāviṭēṣaṉ) eṉpar. aṉṟiyum, or yāṇṭil nikaḻum kār, kūtir, muṉpaṉi,
piṉpaṇi mutalāya paruvaṅkaḷ tōṉṟutaṟkum accuṟṟē kāraṇameṉavum kūṟuvar.
ikkūṟiya koḷkiyē perumpālār koṇṭa teṉiṉum, itaṟku muraṇāyiṉārumuḷar. itiṟ
ciṟitu vaḻuviyārumuḷar eṉavuṅ koḷka. ivarkaḷuṭ paṇṭiya muṉivarkaḷum oru cārārāvar.
ivarkkuḷ, cilar ippūvulaku oru camaṉāṉaveḷi yeṉṟum, itaṉit tāṅkum palatiṇṇiya
tūṇkaḷ pala itaṉkīḻē yuḷaveṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar. cilar ituvoru pēraraviṉ
muṭiyilirukkiṟateṉṟum, vēṟucilar, oru aṭalmikkayāmiyālitu cumakkap peṟukiṉṟa
teṉavuṅ kūṟuvar. ivarkaḷ kūṟiya tuṇ, arā, yāmi mutaliyaṉa niṉṟu tāṅkaṟku uriya iṭam
eṉṉeṉak kaṭāyiṉārkku viṭiyiṟut talākāmiyiṉ, avi koḷḷaṟpālaṉavalla veṉka.
pirāmaṇarkaḷ, viṇṇulaku maṇṇulakic cārntu mēṉiṟkiṟateṉavum, ivaṟṟiṉiṭiyil
nīriṭittavaḻum mīṉiṉam pōl, ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum vāṉiṭi mitantu kiḻakku mēṟkākac
ceṉṟu. tattamakkuriya kālattiṟutiyil ulaku viḷimpi (Horizon) yaṭintu, muṭivil
tattam muṟippaṭit tōṉṟumiṭattiyaṭiyumeṉavum kūṟuvar.
niṟka, purāṇikarkaḷ, ippūmiyoru taṭṭic camaveḷi yeṉṟum, itaṉic cuṟṟip pāṟkaṭalit
talikkoṇṭa eḻuvakik kaṭalkaḷum, avaṟṟiṉiṭiyil, mērumutalāka eḻuvaki
malikaḷumirukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟuṅ kūṟalōṭamiyātu, cūriya cantira kirakaṇaṅkaḷāvaṉa
irākuveṉṉum pēraravu cilakālaṅkaḷiṟṟōṉṟi avaṟṟuḷoṉṟi viḻuṅkuṅ kālamē eṉavuṅ
kūṟupa.
apulkācaṉali yeṉṉum pēraṟivāḷaroruvar tam nāṭṭu vallār urikaḷik kaṟṟut tam
nuṇṇaṟiviṟkut tōṉṟiya cilavaṟṟit tām eḻutiyuḷḷa "poṉṉilamum maṇiccuraṅkamum"
(Mourondge-ed-dharab, or The golden meadows, and the mines of precious
stones) eṉṉum ulaka varalāṟṟuṭ kūṟumāṟu:-
"ippūvulaku oru taṉipperum paṟaviyākum. itaṉ taliyē mekkā, metiṉā eṉṉum
nakaraṅkaḷ. pāracīkamum intiyāvum ataṉ valacciṟakākavum, kāk (Gog) eṉṟa nāṭu
iṭacciṟakākavum, āppirikkā ataṉ vālākavum amintuḷḷaṉa. iṉṟiyappōtu nām vāḻum
ippūvulakiṉ muṉpu, vēṟoru ulakiruntatu. atutōṉṟi ēḻāyiram āṇṭukaḷē iruntatu.
ataṉi ippūppaṟaviyiṉ kuñceṉiṉum poruntum. tōṉṟiyirunta atu, iṭiyil nikaḻnta
veḷḷam, nilanaṭukkam mutalāya iṭiyūṟukaḷāl iṉṉalvāyp paṭṭiṟantoḻiya itu tōṉṟiṟṟu.
iṅṅaṉam, pūvulakam tōṉṟiyaḻital iyaṟkiyē. avaṟṟiṟkuriya kālam eḻupatiṉāyiram hasarōvam
ākum. oru hasarōvameṉpatu paṉṉīrāyira māṇṭukaḷām..."
ikkālak kuṟippum, variyaṟiyum ivar tammōṭirunta pirāmaṇa naṇparkaḷoṭu kalantu
ceytatātal innūlāṟ ṟerikiṟatu.
innūlāciriyarip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ:- ivaratu tanti ālkāṉ; avar tanti aliyeṉpār;
avar tanti aptur rahimāṉ; avar tanti aptullā; avar tanti macuṭal ateli. ivari
yāvarum macuṭi yeṉavē yaḻippar. piṟaku ivaratu aṟivikuṟittu, ivarkkuk “kutputīṉ"
eṉṉum paṭṭamum akkālattār aḷittaṉar. eṉiṉum, ivar appaṭṭap perumiyiyum karutātu
lāhēp arracu (paramporuḷiṉ tūtaṉuṭiya tōḻaṉ) eṉṉu morutāḻvāṉa (akkālattār
karuttu) paṭṭattiyē yēṟṟaṉar.
nūṟkuṟippu:-
itu ulakiṉ tōṟṟa mutal innūl eḻutiyakālam variyiṟ kūṟum varalāṟākum. ivaratu kālam
mōtipillāviṉ kilāpat kālam. atāvatu hijirā 336.
itukāṟum kūṟiya koḷkikaḷum iṉṉōraṉṉapiṟavum pūvulakip poruttamaṭṭil mikappala uṇṭu.
ovvoru koḷkikkum ataṉiyāyntaṟinta periyōrkaḷ, avvak kālattukkēṟpap palacila
kāraṇaṅkaḷum, karikaḷum kāṭṭiyē niṉṟaṉar.
iṉi, ñāyiṟu mutaliya vāṉulakap poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi avarkaḷ niṉittavaṟṟip pārppōm.
ñāyiṟṟiṉ piṟappu mutaliyavaṟṟik kūṟavanta pēraṟiñarkaḷum palarē. avarkaḷ ovvoruvarum
kāṭṭiya cāṉṟukaḷ, avarotta aṟiñarkaḷi mayakkun taṉmiyavāy niṟpa, avaravar
tattammāliyaṉṟavaḷavu piṟappuk kūṟiṉar. aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉār tokiyittukki nōkkuṅkāl,
avarkaḷ mūṉṟu vakiyuḷaṭaṅkuvar. oruvakiyār mikap paṇṭik kālattu aṟiñarkaḷāvār.
avarkaḷ, ñāyiṟu oru aḷattaṟkariya aḻalāḻi (Wheel of fire) yeṉpar; oru cārār, oru
paḷiṅkotta voḷiteṟikkum (Transparent) kaṇṇāṭi allatu uruṇṭi vaṭiviṟṟāya paḷiṅku
eṉṟaṉar; mūṉṟām piriviṉar, kallum irumpum kalantu, pēraḻalvāyp paṭṭuruki,
oḷikālum taṉmivāynta or arumperum poruḷ eṉpar. ivaruṭ ṭaliyāyavar, aṉaksākoras
(Amaxagorus) eṉṭār. aṉṟiyum, ivar viṇmīṉ, tiṅkaḷ mutaliya vāṉap poruḷkaḷaṉittum
maṇṇiṉiṉṟum teṟitteḻunta kaṟkaḷeṉavum, avi ovvoṉṟiṟkumuḷḷa vāṉiṭit toṭarpu
(allatu taṉvayaṅkōṭal) Attraction of gravity) vayappaṭṭu, virintu cuṟṟic cellum
iyalpuṭimiyiṉ payaṉākat tīkkoṇṭu oḷiyiṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟuṅ kūṟuvar.
ivar ittakiya ārāycci muṭivi urōma (Rome) tēyattumakkaḷukku veḷiyiṭṭakāli, avarkaḷ
itaṉiyēṟṟaṟku maṟuttatōṭamiyātu, ivariyum tam nāṭṭiṉiṉṟun turatti viṭṭārātaliṉ
itaṉi nām naṉku āytal vēṇṭiṟṟilli. aṉṟiyum, paṇṭik kālattu urōma tēyattu makkaḷ
tamakku ēṟkātakoḷki, eṇṇam mutaliyavaṟṟi oruvar veḷiyiṭuvarēl ataṉi maṟukkum vakiyāl
avari veḷināṭukaḷukkut turatti viṭutal marapeṉavum koḷka.
niṟka, vēṟucilar, maṇṇiṉiṉṟum aḻaṟ poruḷkaḷ pala mēlē kiḷarntu ceṉṟu,
nāḷāvaṭṭattil vāṉaveḷiyiṟṟirintu niṟpat taṟceyalāka avi oṉṟu kūṭi oru pēraḻaṟ
piḻampāyiṉa; appiḻampē iññāyiṟu, apporuṭkaḷuṭ cila (aviyum eṇṇiṟantaṉavē) oṉṟu
kūṭātu, taṉittō, tampōṉṟa cila kūṭit tiraṇṭa ciṟu piḻampukaḷākavō viṇmīṉkaḷeṉat
tikaḻkiṉṟaṉa; ivikaḷ, kālam nīṭitta kāli, vēṟu cila aḻaṟporuḷ tammuḻi vantucēra,
oruṅkē oḷipeṟuvatumuṇṭu eṉṟu kūṟuvar.
cilar, namkaṭpulaṉām ñāyiṟṟiṉmuṉ vēṟōr ñāyiṟu iruntateṉavum, atu oru kālattut
taṉ oḷimuḻutum oḻintumaṟiya, oru tiṅkaḷkāṟum ulakam oḷiyiṉṟiyiruntatu; appōḻtē,
iññāyiṟu tōṉṟiṟṟu eṉavuṅ kūṟuvar. vēṟucilar, ñāyiṟu eṉpatu orpeṟalarum
cīruñciṟappu mamintu, pēraḻakuvāynta pērakameṉavum, aṅku makkaḷ ceṉṟu
vāḻtaluṅkūṭumeṉṟum kūṟi, appērakattiṉ mītu oru vakittāṉa oḷi, veppam, nīr
mutaliyavum vēṟu piṟavum kalanta mēkapaṭalam cūḻavuḷateṉṟum, appaṭalattiṉ ceyalē
ippūvulakupeṟum ñāyiṟṟoḷiyeṉavum kūṟupa ivaruṭṭaliyāyavar herskēl eṉpavar.
immuṟiyē, avaravar kūṟiyuḷḷavaṟṟit tēṭik koṇṭē celvatu virivukāṇumātalāl, itaṉi
yimmaṭṭiṉiṟutti, iṉi, innilam, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ viṇmīṉ mutaliyavi tattam
niliyiṟṟiriyātu koṭpuṟum taṉmiyiyum, viriviyum paṟṟiyeḻunta periyōrkaḷiṉ
palavēṟu vakippaṭṭa mataṅkaḷiyum kūṟi muṭivil, nam nāṭṭiṉ varalāṟṟiyum nōkkuvōm.
ñāyiṟṟiṉ iyaṟkiyip paṟṟip pala vēṟu vakippaṭṭa āciriyarkaḷ kūṟiyavaṟṟiyum, avaṟṟōraṉṉa
piṟavaṟṟiyum nām ārāyntu koṇṭē celliṉ, ittalip poruḷ muṟṟup peṟal
aritāmākaliṉ, avvārāycciyi im maṭṭiṉiṟutti, ikkaṭṭurit toṭakkattiṟkūṟiya
pūmiyiṉ cuḻaṟciyip paṟṟic ciṟitu nōkkippiṉ ataṉ varalāṟṟi yārāyvōm.
mēl nāṭṭil paḻaṅkālattil leyṭeṉ (Leyden) eṉṉum palkalik kaḻakamoṉṟil,
pērāciriyarāy vāṉpuṭiṅ kāpṭ (Wompuddingcoft) eṉpāroruvariruntār, avar oru
pērāciriyaṉukkuḷḷa amitimuṟṟum nirampap peṟṟavareṉiṉum tērtal (Examination)
kālaṅkaḷ aṟintu cōrtuyil koḷvār. ataṉāl, avar māṇavarkaḷ, kaṟṟaṟṟiṟam ciṟitum
kaḷiyāṭṭayartal peritum payiṉṟuviḷaṅkiṉar.
orunāḷ, ivar māṇavarkku nilavulakip paṟṟiya ōr virivuri nikaḻttumamiyattu, vāḷi
(Tub or bucket) yoṉṟiṟṟaṇṇir koṇṭu, ēntiyakiyarāy ataṉi nīṭṭiya vaṇṇam nērē
piṭittuccuṟṟiṉar. cuṟṟuṅkāl vāḷiyilirunta nīr kīḻē vīḻātu, ataṉiṭattēyē niṉṟatu.
itaṉi or kāṭṭākak koṇṭu, avar piṉvarumāṟutaṉ māṇavarkaṭkuk kūṟuvārāyiṉar.
"ivvāḷiyē pūmiyākavum, ataṉ nīrē kaṭalākavum nīṭṭip piṭitta kiyē pūmikku
ñāyiṟṟiṉukkumuḷḷa toṭarpākavum, eṉ ceṉṉiyē ñāyiṟākavum koḷka. yāṉ vāḷiyic
cuṟṟuṅkāleḻunta viriviṉ payaṉāka, eṅṅaṉam ataṉ kaṇirunta taṇṇīr kīḻē viḻātu niṉṟatō
aṅṅaṉamē ippūvulaku cuṟṟum nērmiyāl ataṉkaṇuḷḷa kaṭaṉīrum irunta vaṇṇamē niṟkiṉṟatu,
eṉa aṟika. yāṉ cuṟṟum neṟiyiṉiṉṟum uṭaṉē niṟuttiṉālum avvāḷiyē yātarṉu
moṉṟāl taṭipeṟṟālum ataṉir kivaḻiyē eṉ tali miciviḻum. itu uṇmi. iṅṅaṉamē,
pūmiyum taṉ virinta cuḻaṟciyiṉiṉṟum yātāṉumoṉṟāṟṟakiyap peṟiṉ, kaṭaṉīrum, ataṉ
payaṉāka ñāyiṟṟiṉ mītu viḻum, viḻiṉum ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmiyum, oḷiyiṉ mikutiyum
evvāṟṟāṉuṅ kuṟipaṭā veṉpatic ciṟitum maṟavaṟka. eṉṉi: ñāyiṟṟiṉ vemmikku immaṇ
cūḻnta kaṭaṉir āṟṟātākaliṉ eṉka."
itaṉik kēṭṭa māṇavarkaḷuḷoruvaṉ, taṉ āciriyaruritta poruḷ ettuṉiyuṟutiyuṭittu,
eṉakkāṇṭal vēṭki mīturntu, vāḷiyic cuṟṟi niṉṟa. āciriyaratukiyi iṭiniṉṟu
taṭuttāṉ. taṭukkavē avvāḷi nīr avar uṭal muḻutum vīḻntu naṉittatu, vāḷiyum
kīḻvīḻntaḻivuṟṟatu. āciriyarkkum aṭakkalākā vekuḷipiṟantatu. mukaṅkuruti pāyntu
cekkarccevēreṉac civantatu. uṭaṉē kaṇkaṟuppa vīci tuṭippa, avaratu maṭittavāyiṉiṉṟum,
veṭitta coṟkaḷ pala veḷivantaṉa. kaṇṭār māṇavarkaḷ. kaṇṭāratu karuttil pēraccantōṉṟi oru
puṭivaruttiṉum, maṟupuṭi, vāḷi nīr pāyntum, taṅkaḷ āciriyar mukam taṉṉoḷi
keṭātiruntami kaṇṭu, iṉṉaṇamē kaṭaṉīr viḻiṉum, ñāyiṟṟiṉṟaṉmi tiriyā teṉpatu
muṇmiyē eṉamaṉantēṟic ceṉṟaṉar.
innikaḻcciyil, māṇavaratu aṟiyāmik kuṟumpu oru puṭit tōṟṟamāyiṉum, nām
nuṇukinōkki aṟiyakkiṭappaṉa palavuḷa. avi uyarntōr tam uṇmiyaṟivāṉārāyntu kāṇum
ariyaporuḷkaḷi, iyaṟki naṅkiyum uṭaṉ koḷḷātu. avi tāmē mutirntu varuntuṇiyum
vēṟāy niṟpaḷeṉpatum: iṉṉōraṉṉa ariya poruḷkaḷ aṟivuṭiyōr maṉakkaṇṇiṟṟōṉṟi, avarāṉ
veḷiyiṭap peṟuṅkāl, avaṟṟiyēṟkum āṟṟalillāp piṟamakkaḷ - iyaṟki naṅkiyiṉ
iḷañciṟārāya immakkaḷ - ēṟkātoḻivataṉṟi, avaṟṟiyum, veḷiyiṭuvōriyum mikappala
iṭiyūṟṟukkuḷḷākkit tuṉpuṟuttuvareṉpatum; itaṉāleytum kuṟi av aṟiñarkaḷatō,
maṟṟu, avarkaḷ kaṇṭa poruḷkaḷatōveṉpārkku atu avviraṇṭiṭattu maṉṟāy iyaṟkiyiṉ
pāṟṟāmeṉak kōṭal vēṇṭumeṉpatum; ivviyaṟki naṅkitāṉ, avi veḷi varuṅkāl, makkaḷ
avaṟṟi yeḷitilaṟintu ēṟkāvaṇṇam mayakkuvatōṭu amiyātu, av aṟiñarkaḷukku iṉṉa
viḻikkumāṟu avarkaḷit tūṇṭutalum ceyvāḷeṉpatum, iṅkukkūṟiya iraṇṭu kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ
vayappaṭṭa ivaṭaṉ māyiyē mēlē nikaḻnta nikaḻccikkum kāraṇamāmeṉpatum; tam uṭaṟkum
vāḷikkum iṭi niṉṟa kiyē ñāyiṟu pūmi mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ iṭiniṟkum toṭarpeṉṟum,
ittoṭarpē ulakam niṉṟu nilavuvataṟku iṉṟiyamiyāp poruḷeṉṟu mavar kūṟiyatu
uṇmiyēyām eṉpatum; itaṉi avvāciriyar veḷiyiṭutalumiyalpē yeṉpatum; iṭiniṉṟa
toṭarpu taṭippaṭṭaḻiyuṅ kālattut taṉ māṭṭiḻukkum taṉmittāya ñāyiṟu taṉ taṉmi
tiriyātu niṟkumeṉpatum iṉṉōraṉṉa piṟavumām.
ikkaruttu meykkaruttēyeṉa mēṉāṭṭup perum pulavarkaḷ aṉivarum uṭaṉpaṭṭaṉar; iṉṟum
uṭaṉpaṭukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ "nilattukkum ñāyiṟṟiṉukkumuḷḷa toṭarpu or kālattu
aḻiyum; avvaḻivāl nilam.taṉnili peyarntu ñāyiṟṟiṉ kaṭ paṭṭaḻiyum.” eṉpatiyē
iṉṉum etirnōkki niṟkiṉṟaṉar. maṟṟu, avartam nōkkattiṟku māṟāka, immaṇṇulaku taṉ
toṭarpi iḻattaliṉṟittaṉ nilimi māṟāmiyum peṟṟu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. ivviyaṟkip
pulavarkaḷuṭiya koḷkikaḷ muṟṟup peṟunāḷ ennāḷō! itu kāraṇamāka eḻunta koḷkikaḷum
pulavarkaḷum viṇ mīṉ tokiyiṉum palarē. eṉ ceyvar tām āyntaṟinta uṇmippaṭi ulakam taṉ
toṭarpu keṭṭālaṉṟō avarkaḷ maṉam amitipeṟum! iṉṟēl, ippulavarkaḷiyum iyaṟkiyiyum
oṉṟuvikkum cantām taṉmiyuṭiya pulavarkaḷēṉum varal vēṇṭum; avarkaḷum vantārilar!!
ākavē, tām koṇṭa uṇmippaṭi ulakam naṭi peṟāmikaṇṭa avarkaḷ ulaka naṭippaṭit tam
muṇmikaḷic celutti nōkkiṉar. atu koṇṭē mēṟpōnta pērāciriyarum "pūmikkum
cūriyaṉukkum uḷḷa toṭarpu keṭiṉ, avi akkēṭṭiṟkuk kāraṇamāyavaṟṟit toṭarātu,
oṉṟāṉoṉṟu aḻintoḻiyu meṉiṉum, ivvulakam ñāyiṟṟic cuṟṟi varutaleṉpatuṇmi"yeṉa
āṇṭukkūṟātu kūṟippiṉṉarō ramayattiṉaṉku viḷakkiṉar. atu mutaṟkoṇṭu, ulakam pakalavaṉic
cuṟṟi varukiṟa teṉpatum, accuṟṟukkum ñāyiṟṟiṉukkumuḷḷa toṭarpum neṟiyum
cuṟṟum poruḷiccārntaṉaveṉpatum muṭinta muṭivukaḷāyiṉa.
ulakiṉ tōṟṟam
mēṟkūṟiyavāṟṟāṉ maṇ, ñāyiṟu mutaliya ulakaṅkaḷiṉ potuviyalpukaṇṭa aṉparkaḷ avaṟṟiṉ
tōṟṟattikkāṉa avāvuvaraṉṟē! avvavāvum aṟiviṉpālatēyām. āyiṉum, ulakiṉṟōṟṟattik
kāṇṭal cellum nām mutaṟkaṇ nam tōṟṟatti yaṟivōmāveṉpatu viṉā. avviṉāviṟkup
pōtiya viṭiyaḷikkavalla muṭipukaḷ itukāṟum kiṭittila kiṭippatumaritē. aṟṟēl, nam
nāṭṭuppaṇṭiya muṉivarkaḷum, periyōrkaḷum kaṇṭāyntavi eṉṉāyavōveṉiṉ, avi avaravarkaḷ
koṇṭuceṉṟa aṟiviṉṟiṇ mikkēṟpa amintuḷḷaṉavēyaṉṟi, ellāmakkaḷāṉum koḷḷap
peṟavilli. iṉṉaṇamē piṟanāṭṭār kūṟiya muṭipukaḷum amintuḷḷaṉa eṉka. eṉavē,
irutaliyum tūkki nōkkuṅkāl oru vaki muṭivum peṟutal aritākiṉṟatu. ittaṉiyum
nōkkiyō nam nāvariyarum "vantavā ṟeṅṅaṉē? pōmāṟētō? māyamām peruvāḻvu" eṉat
tiruvāymalarntatu!
iṅṅaṉam nam tōṟṟamē māyameṉiṉ, nām niṉṟu nilavum ulakiṉ tōṟṟamum māyamēyām.
āyiṉum, nam nāṭṭup periyōratu koḷkiyōṭu piṟanāṭṭup periyōrkaḷatu kūṟṟukkaḷi
nōkki maṉattāṉ ārāyvōm.
----------
